# Scripting And Performance

_Luau scripting practice, architecture, performance, mobile optimisation, security/exploits._

Evidence base: 1728 extracted items from 666 videos by 1_Bruce, AlvinBlox, Amora, ArTheDev, B Ricey, BubuTheDev, ByteBlox, CallMeDogey, ContextLost, Creators on the Blox, Crit, Crusherfire, Cumin, Dev Total, DitchyDevelopments, Duckable, Emancyphur, GameMakers, Generalist Programmer, GnomeCode, ItsZyonic, JustDucksey, Last Level Studios, Leif, Lnor Roblox, LovJak, MoseyTube, PineappleDev, Possible Panda, RoDev, Roblox, Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Roox4, RoyalDev, SimTek Game Development, Stewiepfing, Suphi Kaner, TechSpectrum, TheDankDeveloper, TheDevKing, TheNexusAvenger, VuukStudios, XTB7, janisjanis01, waveshine (2015-01-03 to 2026-10-30). Roblox changes quickly: check the date on each source and see Date context below.

## Overview
Scripting and performance advice has shifted toward server-authoritative, event-driven, budgeted code. Keep damage, hits and purchases on the server. Batch and compress remote traffic, and use buffers or Packet on hot paths. Slice heavy work, pool and clean up instances, and profile before optimising. Mobile and memory limits are the binding constraints. Roblox's 2025–2026 changes add new defaults and APIs: --!optimize 2 by default, the Include/Exclude raycast lists, BindToSimulation for prediction, AuthorityMode=Server, and Motor6D being replaced by AnimationConstraints. Where older indie advice conflicts with these, prefer the newer guidance.

## Rules and findings
- **Validate everything on the server** (multiple-creators): Clients can send arbitrary values even with server authority. Have clients request actions and validate on the server (state, ammo, position, cooldown). Keep currency and value logic server-side; client-built values are easy to exploit. Sources: [Leif 2025-08 5:39](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=339), [Roblox 2025-09 33:38](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=2018), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-12 0:00](https://youtu.be/1jRaSYhrEcU?t=0), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 28:34](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1714), [Roblox 2018-07 27:26](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1646). Newest source 2025-09-26.
- **Debounce/cooldown remotes and reward mechanics** (multiple-creators): Add server-side cooldowns to ability/slap remotes (e.g. 5 s build cooldown). Put shared cooldowns on reward mechanics so players can't hop instances/plots to abuse them. Sources: [ItsZyonic 2023-12 10:09](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=609), [Leif 2025-08 5:39](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=339), [Leif 2025-08 5:39](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=339), [MoseyTube 2026-06 2:07](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=127). Newest source 2026-06-10.
- **Process purchases only in server ProcessReceipt, idempotently** (multiple-creators): Handle purchases in ProcessReceipt on the server. Record each PurchaseId in a DataStore and check before granting; check player exists via GetPlayerByUserId; return PurchaseGranted only after rewards are saved; return NotProcessedYet if the player left so it retries on rejoin. Dispatch via a per-product-ID handler table. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2018-08 31:31](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=1891), [AlvinBlox 2018-08 35:45](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=2145), [AlvinBlox 2024-06 2:32](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=152), [AlvinBlox 2024-06 4:31](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=271), [AlvinBlox 2024-06 7:17](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=437). Newest source 2024-06-04.
- **DataStore safety basics** (one-creator-repeated): Key by UserId, not name; never rename the DataStore after publishing; wrap GetAsync in pcall; save in PlayerRemoving and use BindToClose (about 5 s wait). Studio writes to live data when API access is enabled. Name leaderstats folder exactly 'leaderstats'. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2018-08 5:12](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=312), [AlvinBlox 2018-08 5:12](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=312), [AlvinBlox 2018-08 9:17](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=557), [AlvinBlox 2018-08 12:58](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=778), [AlvinBlox 2018-08 20:37](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=1237). Newest source 2026-07-27.
- **Hide unreleased event code from exploiters** (single-mention): Keep event code in ServerStorage until launch, then copy into ReplicatedStorage on the countdown event. Countdown = start timestamp minus os.time(). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-12 10:52](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=652), [AlvinBlox 2020-06 8:18](https://youtu.be/spFZ44yqIII?t=498). Newest source 2021-12-01.
- **Choose hit-detection authority by game type** (multiple-creators): Casual/FPS: client-side hit detection with server checks favors feel. Competitive: server-side hit logic resists exploiters at cost of responsiveness. Use fixed attack timings (700 ms heavy) and non-physics checks for duels. Monitor exploit hubs monthly. 2018 guidance; server authority now reduces the tradeoff. Make hitboxes generous. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 18:31](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1111), [Roblox 2018-07 19:12](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1152), [Roblox 2018-07 27:26](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1646), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 28:34](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1714), [Roblox 2018-07 18:31](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1111). Newest source 2025-03-22.
- **Projectiles: older vs newer approach** (multiple-creators): 2018: set network ownership to shooter and enlarge colliders against tunneling (physics 240 Hz). Newer: under server authority create projectiles on both client and server instead of fake invisible rockets. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 36:05](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=2165), [Roblox 2018-07 36:46](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=2206), [Roblox 2026-10 11:27](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=687). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Prefer server authority for characters** (one-creator-repeated): Set Workspace authority mode to Server for latency compensation and built-in anti-speed/fly (~1000-line anti-fly script became redundant). Read/write only synchronized data (input actions, attributes on HumanoidRootPart); store rollback-sensitive state like scores in attributes. Keep prediction on auto; enable only for key objects. Test with 60 ms latency/10 ms jitter. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 28:27](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=1707), [Roblox 2026-10 8:35](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=515), [Roblox 2026-10 5:47](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=347), [Roblox 2026-10 21:03](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1263), [Roblox 2025-09 34:46](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=2086). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Put simulation logic in the right hooks** (one-creator-repeated): Use fixed heartbeat / bind to simulation (not RenderStepped or Heartbeat) since they re-run on rollback. Share code via a ModuleScript called from both client and server; LocalScripts can't drive a server-authoritative humanoid. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 31:21](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=1881), [Roblox 2026-10 7:53](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=473), [Roblox 2026-10 7:13](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=433). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Use the Character Controller Library** (one-creator-repeated): CCL is a transparent, debuggable Lua Humanoid replacement; extend with dashes/double jumps, V2 adds crouch/sprint; action slots bind per action not device. Only simulates characters that matter, so crowds scale. Sources: [Roblox 2026-09 38:25](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=2305), [Roblox 2026-10 27:01](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1621), [Roblox 2026-10 7:50](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=470), [Roblox 2026-10 9:51](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=591). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Use the input action system and PreferredInput** (one-creator-repeated): Define actions with touch/gamepad/keyboard bindings and connect game code once. Use PreferredInput over LastInputType for hints. Use key-code image API for PlayStation/Xbox icons and haptics. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 17:49](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1069), [Roblox 2025-09 16:12](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=972), [Roblox 2025-09 28:08](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1688), [Roblox 2024-09 17:07](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=1027). Newest source 2025-09-26.
- **Avoid per-frame work; use events and slice work** (multiple-creators): Prefer events over every-frame loops; slice long work; spread raycasts and heavy loops across frames. Heartbeat/Stepped preferred over RenderStepped, which inflates render time. Watch worst-case frames. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 19:23](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1163), [Roblox 2018-07 20:46](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1246), [Roblox 2024-09 25:25](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1525), [Roblox 2018-07 4:06](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=246), [Roblox Creators 2022-11 35:49](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=2149). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Use efficient algorithms for bulk/proximity work** (multiple-creators): Use a spatial hash grid instead of all-pairs checks; use math rather than loops for bulk item opens; cache property reads and object creation; use built-in APIs. Sources: [Leif 2026-08 3:35](https://youtu.be/UhpaVrb5Imo?t=215), [1_Bruce 2026-03 8:55](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=535), [1_Bruce 2026-03 9:38](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=578), [Roblox 2018-07 21:46](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1306). Newest source 2026-08-27.
- **Budget triangles for phones** (multiple-creators): Budget in-camera triangles (~500k); check with Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+F2. Cap model triangles and brick counts; simplify and combine meshes (splitting does not help). Use clean topology and custom LOD for large maps. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 5:59](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=359), [Roblox 2024-09 8:38](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=518), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 32:36](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1956), [Roblox 2018-07 11:33](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=693), [Roblox 2023-09 24:02](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=1442). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Budget draw calls and batch** (one-creator-repeated): Keep scene draw calls ~500 (subtract UI). Reuse MeshId and material; Color3 and SurfaceAppearance changes batch well; transparency, decals, textures add calls. Shadow calls up to 3x is healthy. Disable outlines when unneeded; Humanoids for characters only. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 10:39](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=639), [Roblox 2024-09 12:43](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=763), [Roblox 2024-09 18:56](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1136), [Roblox 2024-09 10:39](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=639), [Roblox 2024-09 17:36](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1056). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Test CPU on real low-end phones** (multiple-creators): A 10 ms PC frame can be ~40 ms on low-end phone; target under 16.66 ms. Keep a budget Android device; Android is the focus when pushing updates. Optimise for old devices. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 21:29](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1289), [Roblox 2024-09 21:29](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1289), [Roblox 2024-09 22:09](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1329), [Roblox 2024-09 10:52](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=652), [Roblox 2024-09 27:19](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1639). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Memory first: target 2 GB devices** (multiple-creators): Ejections rise near 66% of memory (~1.3 GB; ~1.4 GB crash). Most crashes are out-of-memory. Check F9 on the real device. Prefer memory over speed. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 27:36](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1656), [Roblox 2024-09 27:36](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1656), [Roblox 2025-09 4:32](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=272), [Roblox 2024-09 26:26](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1586), [Roblox 2025-09 5:12](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=312). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Limit instances and replication costs** (multiple-creators): Use StreamingEnabled (~1 KB/part). Limit server-owned moving assemblies to ~50 (~1 KB/s each; watch recv Ctrl/Cmd+F7). Don't store server-only data as attributes/tags on many parts; don't resize welded/anchored parts per frame; cosmetics client-side. Hide models rather than reparenting. Anchor/disable animators on idle NPCs (Dead Rails halved server CPU). Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 30:49](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1849), [Roblox 2024-09 30:49](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1849), [Roblox 2024-09 34:15](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=2055), [Roblox 2024-09 34:15](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=2055), [Roblox 2025-09 17:16](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1036). Newest source 2025-09-26.
- **Be sparing with lights** (one-creator-repeated): Don't quadruple lights for GI; use few bounce lights. Toggle lights to check necessity; audit with woot3's plugin; avoid overlapping radii. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 20:46](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1246), [Roblox 2024-09 31:19](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1879), [Roblox 2024-09 21:28](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1288), [Roblox 2024-09 31:19](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1879). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Use Slim, mesh LOD and texture streaming** (one-creator-repeated): Slim cuts drawn triangles 93% and memory 60% (simulated); avatar Slim+LOD ~10x fewer at distance (R15 only). Use Slim only if you can bake and not alter at runtime; mesh LOD/streaming has no such limit (S20 GPU ~12.5 to <6 ms). Texture streaming cut load times and bytes. Sources: [Roblox 2026-09 39:49](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=2389), [Roblox 2026-09 37:04](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=2224), [Roblox 2026-10 11:26](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=686), [Roblox 2026-10 11:26](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=686), [Roblox 2026-10 29:44](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1784). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Triage performance methodically** (one-creator-repeated): Confirm game works, test on real hardware, check frame rate, stats screen, then Microprofiler (Ctrl/Cmd+F6). Profile affected phone sessions; capture dumps during the issue and diff against pre-event dump. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 29:26](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1766), [Roblox 2026-10 10:31](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=631), [Roblox 2026-10 11:16](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=676), [Roblox 2024-09 25:25](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1525). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Instrument live ops** (one-creator-repeated): Attach version, device group and game-state metadata to alerts and logs; monitor FPS/crash alerts during intense live events. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 8:26](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=506), [Roblox 2026-10 9:09](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=549), [Roblox 2026-10 13:21](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=801). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Keep joins fast** (multiple-creators): Join time depends on instance/voxel count; use streaming. Never block character loading on third-party calls (analytics outage caused 30 s waits at 15,000 CCU). Plan for crowd joins; join queue exists. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 8:46](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=526), [Roblox 2018-07 10:08](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=608), [Roblox 2023-09 25:54](https://youtu.be/V1uFFUUCLpo?t=1554), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 24:12](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=1452). Newest source 2023-09-19.
- **Structure code with modules** (multiple-creators): Singleton service modules with init, dot-call with typed self, small categorized helpers; generate remotes in code via <50 line wrapper; tags with components; separate front end from back end via signals; multiple remotes for high volume; data update helper. Sources: [Leif 2025-08 2:31](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=151), [Leif 2025-08 2:31](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=151), [Leif 2025-08 7:24](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=444), [Leif 2025-08 3:54](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=234), [Leif 2025-08 4:36](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=276). Newest source 2026-03-27.
- **Luau tooling and React hygiene** (one-creator-repeated): Use VS Code with Luau LSP, Selene, StyLua, auto-import. In React: return useEffect cleanup, always pass dependency array, useBinding for per-frame values, UI state in React and game data in external store (Charm). Sources: [Leif 2025-05 1:09](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=69), [Leif 2025-05 7:11](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=431), [Leif 2026-01 9:53](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=593), [Leif 2026-01 10:36](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=636), [Leif 2026-01 13:26](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=806). Newest source 2026-01-03.
- **Localization and text** (multiple-creators): Disable auto-localize on script-handled GUIs; localize client-side and send event data over remotes. Use variables in quest text. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 22:09](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1329), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 44:22](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2662), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 4:37](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=277). Newest source 2021-11-11.
- **Ads API usage** (single-mention): Call ad availability before showing the button and after each watch, close to when pressable; never show to ineligible users. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2025-08 24:40](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1480), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 25:21](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1521), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 27:09](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1629), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 25:21](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1521). Newest source 2025-08-29.
- **Prepare for upcoming platform features** (one-creator-repeated): Device integrity API (97% less cheating on Android in Rivals test); safety callback API; queue service/compute functions; split pure gameplay vs server-only scripts for offline play. Sources: [Roblox 2026-09 53:33](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3213), [Roblox 2026-09 53:33](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3213), [Roblox 2026-09 53:33](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3213), [Roblox 2026-09 52:13](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3133), [Roblox 2026-09 50:50](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3050). Newest source 2026-09-17.
- **Learn by reading code** (multiple-creators): Study free models, uncopylocked and open source games; read every line, look up each API; google exact errors; find practical examples first. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2021-01 0:31](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=31), [AlvinBlox 2021-01 0:31](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=31), [AlvinBlox 2021-01 2:24](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=144), [AlvinBlox 2021-01 3:45](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=225), [AlvinBlox 2021-01 1:14](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=74). Newest source 2023-11-17.
- **Misc design and workflow** (single-mention): Abstract complexity out of exposed APIs; build generic primitives; store huge incremental values as strings with a Big Num library; lower Studio rendering if laggy; scrap buggy unfamiliar-code features if cheaper; pre-generate AI assets; Discord stat bots; avoid unnecessary network requests. Sources: [Roblox 2022-06 39:18](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=2358), [Roblox 2022-06 17:43](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=1063), [Roblox 2022-06 40:39](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=2439), [1_Bruce 2026-03 3:17](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=197), [ItsZyonic 2023-12 2:09](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=129). Newest source 2026-05-09.
- **Keep purely cosmetic popups on the client** (single-mention): Spawn popup orbs or similar effects from a LocalScript so only the owning player sees them, and cap the count. An indie creator reports that setting the count too high crashed the game. Treat this as low-advice and verify on low-end phones. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2024-04 10:50](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=650). Newest source 2024-04-23.
- **Run slow or independent work in task.spawn** (multiple-creators): Put each orb or independent loop iteration in its own task.spawn so one slow item doesn't block the loop. Wrap RecommendTopProductsAsync and RankProductsAsync in task.spawn with pcall so slow ranking calls can't stall the game. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2024-04 5:15](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=315), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use WaitForChild for required modules in replicated scripts** (single-mention): Replicated scripts can run before their modules replicate to the client. Call WaitForChild on each required module, otherwise the script errors on load. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-04 4:30](https://youtu.be/e48wgHQll2Y?t=270). Newest source 2025-04-27.
- **Scale UI strokes with viewport height** (single-mention): Store stroke thickness at a 1080p baseline and set thickness = viewportSize.Y × (originalThickness / 1080). Borders then look the same on phones and desktops. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-04 0:19](https://youtu.be/e48wgHQll2Y?t=19). Newest source 2025-04-27.
- **Format numbers only in the display module** (single-mention): Send raw numbers over the network and format them (for example 1k) only in the display module. Formatting before sending breaks later arithmetic, because adding 1k to a number that is already a string fails. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2025-08 7:34](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=454). Newest source 2025-08-10.
- **Use sub-step position checks for fast movers** (single-mention): Between frames, check positions along the movement path, not just at frame endpoints. This keeps tags and hits reliable for fast-moving players and supplements the hit-authority rules above. Sources: [DitchyDevelopments 2026-03 1:27](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=87). Newest source 2026-03-14.
- **Create the reward dev product in the same universe** (single-mention): For rewarded ads, create the reward developer product in the same universe as the place. The server calls CreateAdRewardFromDevProductId and ShowRewardedVideoAdAsync, then grants the reward in ProcessReceipt. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678). Newest source 2025-07-24.
- **Read experiment config values only when needed** (single-mention): Calling GetValue for an experiment config key enrolls the player immediately. Gate the call behind the feature check so players are not enrolled in experiments they never reach. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385). Newest source 2025-11-06.
- **Group related config values into one JSON config** (single-mention): Put related values into a single JSON-type config to namespace keys and simplify management. Add configs ahead of time so scripts can read them when needed. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385). Newest source 2025-11-06.
- **Call ConfigService only from server scripts, with pcall and fallbacks** (one-creator-repeated): Client calls to ConfigService error. Call GetConfigAsync on the server, wrap it in pcall, and fall back to safe default values so a failed fetch doesn't break the game. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Refresh config snapshots on UpdateAvailable** (one-creator-repeated): Snapshots do not update on their own. Connect UpdateAvailable to Refresh, and use GetValueChangedSignal to apply changes at safe moments, such as between rounds in a shooter. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Award and check badges on the server with pcall** (one-creator-repeated): Award badges only from server Scripts. Call GetBadgeInfoAsync first and confirm IsEnabled before AwardBadgeAsync, wrapping both in pcall. Check ownership in PlayerAdded with UserHasBadgeAsync, or use GetUserBadgesAsync for batch checks. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Call RankProductsAsync once at join** (single-mention): RankProductsAsync has a strict rate limit. Call it once when the player joins rather than repeatedly, and cache the result for the session. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Avoid custom camera scripts unless necessary** (single-mention): Custom cameras opt out of engine camera updates and need manual merging of VR camera fixes. Use the default camera where possible, and merge current VR camera scripts if you must use a custom one. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Enable instance streaming on headsets** (single-mention): Turn on instance streaming so content streams in and out dynamically on the headset. This reduces memory pressure on VR hardware, combined with the draw-call and mesh budgets above. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep server heartbeat high** (one-creator-repeated): A low server heartbeat lowers frame rate for every client and increases latency. Keep server CPU per frame under 16 ms so the heartbeat stays at the 60 FPS target. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Fix memory leaks and check startup spikes** (one-creator-repeated): Memory can grow over time even when starting usage is low, so fix leaks. Reduce memory to avoid server crashes that disconnect everyone and to reach low-end and mobile devices. If a server crashes within minutes of startup, suspect a memory spike during initialization, such as heavy assets cloned at startup without being parented or destroyed. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Trace bloated nodes with viewer breadcrumbs** (single-mention): Use viewer breadcrumbs to trace bloated nodes to their parent containers. Then inspect those paths in Studio Explorer and add cleanup logic for them. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Target 60 FPS and budget 16 ms per side** (one-creator-repeated): Target 60 FPS by default. Expensive computation that overloads CPU or GPU is the typical cause of low frame rate. Keep client CPU and server CPU each under 16 ms per frame. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use multithreading when server CPU is high but cores are idle** (single-mention): If server CPU time is high but core usage is low, investigate multithreading. If core usage stays high after optimising, consider Extended Services for Compute. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Locate memory hogs with memory tags and MicroProfiler groups** (single-mention): Check memory tags in the Developer Console. Drill into process groups in the MicroProfiler to find the expensive processes. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Strip unique identifiers from error messages** (single-mention): Remove user IDs, coordinates and timestamps from error text so repeats consolidate into one entry with a higher count. Otherwise each occurrence looks like a new error. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Order and test error rules before saving** (one-creator-repeated): Use Ignore rules only for permanent noise, such as unavoidable engine sound errors or legacy print statements. Use Group rules to merge a family of errors into one named row, for example HttpError:.* as Network Issues. The first matching rule wins, so order by priority. Test regex against real error strings first. Patterns cannot start with .* or .+ or nest repeaters like (\w+)+. Rules apply only to errors logged after they are saved. Each experience allows up to 100 custom rules. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Verify errors locally before publishing** (single-mention): Use the Developer Console (F9), the Script Profiler, and the Studio Output window to find and verify errors before each publish. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Store the transferRequestId from Robux transfers** (single-mention): Call PromptRobuxTransferAsync from a server Script and wrap it in pcall. Store the returned transferRequestId so you can correlate the transfer with receipts. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/robux-transfers). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check Premium before scripting Premium logic** (one-creator-repeated): Check player.MembershipType == Enum.MembershipType.Premium before running Premium logic or showing the modal. Debounce the purchase modal (the docs example shows at most once every 5 seconds) and handle Players.PlayerMembershipChanged to react to upgrades. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Gate ranked queues with IsVerified** (single-mention): Use Player:IsVerified(Enum.VerifiedLevel.High) to gate ranked matchmaking queues. Per the docs, verified users are less likely to cheat, spam or get blocked. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/account-verification). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Configure RTBF deletion templates before custom erasure** (one-creator-repeated): Set up Right to Erasure deletion templates for each game's data stores so Roblox auto-deletes matching keys. Use a creator webhook with the Right to Erasure Request trigger only for complex cases: custom deletion logic, unsupported schemas, or personal data stored outside data stores. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Show and fetch regional prices at runtime** (one-creator-repeated): Use GetProductInfoAsync or GetDeveloperProductsAsync at runtime so custom UI shows the same regional price as the purchase prompt. Call GetUsersPriceLevelsAsync at join and do not cache results after the session, because user price levels can change. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build on Lua knowledge and avoid hard-coded logic** (single-mention): Luau is backward compatible with Lua and adds strict typing, so Lua knowledge carries over; turn on typing to catch errors early. Avoid hard-coded logic: reading values from data or config reduces bugs and post-launch patching. Sources: [GameMakers 2021-01 32:05](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=1925), [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279). Newest source 2023-08-18.
- **Set SignalBehavior to Deferred explicitly** (multiple-creators): Official 2026 docs recommend Workspace.SignalBehavior = Deferred; the default currently equals Immediate and may change. Under Deferred, handlers run at the next resumption point, so yield before reading values set by callbacks. Use Event:Once() or Disconnect for one-shot listeners; Destroy does not cancel pending calls. Re-entrancy depth caps at 10. Crusherfire reports cueing handlers improved performance. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/task-scheduler). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Run heavy compute in Actors with desynchronize** (multiple-creators): Luau runs serially unless opted in. Put heavy work (AI pathing, raycasts, terrain) under Actors, call task.desynchronize(), then task.synchronize() before writing instances. Scripts in one Actor share a thread, so split work across many Actors (64+ suggested). Use ConnectParallel for mostly-computational Heartbeat callbacks. Skip parallel if serial already fits the frame budget; a 72-deer test cut frame time from about 50 ms to about 16 ms. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep parallel code free of unsafe writes and shared state** (multiple-creators): Most API members are not parallel-safe (default tier Unsafe). Check the tier (Unsafe, Read Parallel, Local Safe, Safe) before use. Wrap writes such as CFrame or MoveTo in task.synchronize(). Never call require inside desynchronized code. Parallel modules run in separate Actors with separate VMs, so require caches are not shared; communicate via SharedTable or actor messaging. BindableEvent:Fire is safe from parallel code. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading), [Crusherfire 2024-02 22:47](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=1367), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Move models with PivotTo and tween roots, not SetPrimaryPartCFrame** (multiple-creators): Use Model:PivotTo() to move all descendant PVInstances; MoveTo moves only the PrimaryPart. SetPrimaryPartCFrame is deprecated, needs a PrimaryPart, and repeated moves accumulate floating-point offset errors that tear models apart. Tween the root CFrame, not Position, because welds only propagate CFrame changes. Use TweenService directly, not CFrameValue proxies. For many moving parts, Workspace:BulkMoveTo with FireCFrameChanged. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/models), [Roblox DevForum 2019-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253), [Roblox DevForum 2019-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253), [Roblox DevForum 2019-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253), [Crusherfire 2025-07 27:51](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=1671). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Cut physics and collision cost** (multiple-creators): Use Box or Hull CollisionFidelity for small or non-interactive meshes; PreciseConvexDecomposition is the most expensive. Set CanTouch false on objects that need no touch events. Anchor only the root part of an assembly; anchoring every part creates more assemblies. Avoid setting AssemblyLinearVelocity directly; use VectorForce, LinearVelocity or ApplyImpulse. Set AlignPosition MaxVelocity to a finite value (e.g. 10), since Infinity flings objects. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/assemblies), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/assemblies), [B Ricey 2023-04 9:32](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=572). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Debounce every Touched handler** (one-creator-repeated): Touched fires repeatedly from settling and from each body-part contact. Guard with a boolean flag or attribute cooldown (example 1 s) set before work and cleared after. For TTS or animation triggers, set the flag before Play and clear after Ended:Wait(). One-time reward parts should be destroyed after awarding so they cannot be farmed. Check Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter and the Humanoid before acting. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/fading-trap), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/animation/play-character-animations), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-text-to-speech), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Handle ProximityPrompts centrally** (one-creator-repeated): Listen to PromptTriggered, PromptButtonHoldBegan and PromptButtonHoldEnded once in ProximityPromptService rather than attaching a script to each prompt. Keep the service thin and delegate action logic to a ModuleScript used by all prompts. HoldDuration 0 is for instant actions; any non-zero value requires hold-event handling. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/proximity-prompts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/proximity-prompts), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/proximity-prompts). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Nil out and scope temporaries** (multiple-creators): A closure holds strong references to outside-scope variables, and a table stays alive while any strong reference remains. Set large locals to nil when finished, wrap temporaries in do blocks, and clear tables you still hold but no longer use. Clear OnInvoke and callback references as you would connections. Never-ending coroutine loops never collect their root values, so scope their temporaries. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954), [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954), [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954), [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954), [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use weak tables carefully for caches** (multiple-creators): Use setmetatable with __mode 'k' for caches keyed by an entity; entries drop when the key is no longer referenced. Use 'kv' when values reference their key, since a strong reference keeps the key alive. Do not rely on weak tables to hold Roblox Instances: the docs say they are never weak and behaviour is inconsistent. Hold a strong ref and release it when Parent becomes nil. Audit third-party module caches of instances. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954), [Crusherfire 2024-10 0:35](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=35), [Crusherfire 2024-10 5:33](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=333), [Crusherfire 2024-10 6:54](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=414), [Crusherfire 2024-10 10:22](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=622). Newest source 2025-10-06.
- **Track every connection and instance in one Trove or Maid** (multiple-creators): Collect connections, observers, zones and instances in a single Trove or Maid and destroy it on cleanup. Destroy breaks connections recursively, but Parent=nil does not destroy. Key per-player state tables by player and clear them on PlayerRemoving. Use a per-model trove for connections recreated on rebirth. Set Workspace PlayerCharacterDestroyBehavior to Enable so old characters are destroyed, not parented to nil; test that PlayerRemoving cleanup still works. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2018-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/nil), [Roblox DevForum 2019-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-a-maid-class-on-roblox-to-manage-state/340061), [B Ricey 2021-01 6:51](https://youtu.be/xwaCg7SWUhc?t=411), [Crusherfire 2025-10 12:47](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=767). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Create and parent instances efficiently** (multiple-creators): Set all properties before parenting so the engine does not process each change; assign Parent on its own line rather than Instance.new(class, parent). To clear many children, destroy the whole container folder and create a new empty one. Use Debris:AddItem for spent effects and parts. Keep assets that clients rarely need in ServerStorage rather than ReplicatedStorage. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333), [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842), [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078), [B Ricey 2021-01 24:01](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1441), [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345). Newest source 2025-01-08.
- **Use Workspace:Raycast correctly and budget it** (multiple-creators): Replace the FindPartOnRay family with Workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, params); the direction magnitude is the ray length (max 15,000 studs). Always nil-check the result. Use RaycastParams with Include or Exclude FilterType and ignore the shooter's character. Set BasePart.CanQuery false to exclude parts. Start from the attachment's world position. Casting many rays per frame is costly: one official example with 1,500 random rays per second in Stepped caused a major frame drop, so spread or batch them. Sources: [B Ricey 2020-09 1:46](https://youtu.be/4fJBWFz9I-E?t=106), [B Ricey 2020-09 4:02](https://youtu.be/4fJBWFz9I-E?t=242), [B Ricey 2020-09 8:37](https://youtu.be/4fJBWFz9I-E?t=517), [B Ricey 2021-01 12:09](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=729), [B Ricey 2021-01 12:53](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=773). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Clean up projectiles by distance and lifetime** (multiple-creators): Destroy projectiles once they exceed a maximum travel distance to avoid leaking instances. Remove spent bullets with Debris:AddItem (demo lifetime 2 s). Offset the bullet by its segment length so it stops behind walls rather than phasing through. A starting velocity around 1000 gives visible travel and drop, but velocity with Touched is laggier and less accurate than raycasting. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208), [B Ricey 2021-01 24:01](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1441), [B Ricey 2021-01 24:01](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1441), [B Ricey 2021-01 19:48](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1188), [B Ricey 2021-01 21:57](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1317). Newest source 2021-05-01.
- **Profile before micro-optimising** (multiple-creators): Profile before and after changes; some older micro-optimisations have no effect on the newer Luau VM. Worth doing: precompute constants outside loops, cache methods and constructors in locals, store GetChildren() outside loops, and use ipairs for arrays. Use FindFirstChild only when the child may be missing, and FindFirstChildWhichIsA to locate by class. Skip FindFirstChild where existence is guaranteed. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2016-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756), [Roblox DevForum 2016-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756), [Roblox DevForum 2016-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756), [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842), [Roblox DevForum 2021-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastores-beginners-to-advanced/1275421). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Schedule with task.defer, task.spawn and task.cancel** (multiple-creators): Use task.spawn for immediate threads that contain yielding calls, and task.defer when work can wait to the end of the resumption cycle. task.wait() with no argument resumes on the next Heartbeat, so it is a yield, not a timer; a measured task.delay(1) ran about 1.12 s. Store the thread from defer or delay and task.cancel it when unneeded. Check coroutine.status is 'suspended' before task.spawn on a signal-module thread. Avoid spawn(), which was reported delayed by up to 15 s. Sources: [Crusherfire 2025-03 2:41](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=161), [Crusherfire 2025-03 12:38](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=758), [Crusherfire 2025-03 24:08](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=1448), [Crusherfire 2025-03 25:01](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=1501), [Crusherfire 2025-03 16:26](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=986). Newest source 2025-03-22.
- **Never yield in module Init or Start** (multiple-creators): Yielding in Init or Start stalls the module loader for every other module; the loader warns once a yield threshold is exceeded. Move WaitForChild calls for dependencies into task.spawn so they do not block the setup loop, and resolve dependencies without yielding in start code where possible. Sources: [Crusherfire 2025-08 9:18](https://youtu.be/dJjZ7ml9bSo?t=558), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Protect yields with pcall, xpcall and timeouts** (multiple-creators): Wrap MemoryStore, MemoryStoreSortedMap, DataStore and UI-callback calls in pcall, since these are network calls that can fail. Pass pcall(fn, object, arg) rather than a closure. xpcall cannot yield, so use debug.traceback as its handler; ypcall is unnecessary because pcall can yield. Race RBXScriptSignal waits against task.delay for a timeout. Check coroutine.resume's success value and the pcall success flag before indexing returned data. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/hash-map), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/sorted-map), [Roblox DevForum 2021-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastores-beginners-to-advanced/1275421), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui), [Roblox DevForum 2019-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Budget DataStore and Open Cloud requests** (one-creator-repeated): Count UpdateAsync as two requests, since it consumes read and write budgets. Call GetRequestBudgetForRequestType before issuing bursts, and SetRateLimitForRequestType to cap each server's share when also using Open Cloud. Keep GetAsync caching on: cached reads do not count toward limits. Return nil from an UpdateAsync callback to abort. Batch or reduce requests on HTTP 429; investigate failures above 20% and throttling above 10% of requests in an hour. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/versioning-listing-and-caching), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/hash-map). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Batch and pace HttpService calls** (one-creator-repeated): Start concurrent calls with task.spawn per item; the engine batches same-method concurrent calls (example: 20 product lookups became 1-2 HTTP requests). Serial waits send one request per call and risk rate limits. In-game HttpService is capped at 500 requests per minute per server, and Open Cloud calls count toward it. On 429 honour retry-after; otherwise use exponential backoff, and watch x-ratelimit-remaining. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep remote payloads small and typed** (one-creator-repeated): Send only the fields the client needs. Pick the smallest fixed-width type (u8, u16); a 64-bit number sends about 9 bytes versus 3 in a u8 buffer. Buffers have no cursor, so track byte offsets manually. writebits/readbits pack 0-32 bit values. Map 0-1 floats to 8-bit ints. Serialise Color3 only if throughput truly needs it. Measured: 100 u16 IDs 900 bytes as an array versus 203 in a buffer. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-best-practices-how-to-keep-your-games-ping-low/1913475), [Crusherfire 2025-04 17:08](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=1028), [Crusherfire 2025-04 17:49](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=1069), [Crusherfire 2025-04 21:27](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=1287), [Crusherfire 2025-04 8:00](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=480). Newest source 2025-04-19.
- **Type-check remote arguments with the T library** (multiple-creators): Validate values received through RemoteEvents and RemoteFunctions at runtime with the T library instead of repeated type() comparisons. Use T.interface schemas for complex tables and nest interfaces or tuples. This complements, not replaces, server-side validation: never accept client-supplied damage or outcomes without a server recheck. Sources: [Crusherfire 2025-05 8:52](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=532), [Crusherfire 2025-05 10:56](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=656), [Roblox DevForum 2021-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507). Newest source 2025-05-24.
- **Use server time and server-side randomness** (multiple-creators): Base timers and simulation on workspace:GetServerTimeNow() or time(), not tick(), os.time(), os.clock() or DateTime.now(), which are local clocks. One report found os.time drifting 3-5 minutes between servers; fetch external time if needed. Seed shared daily items with Random.new(floor(day)) so every server agrees without communicating. Do not use math.random alone for rolls players can predict; seed from several entropy sources on the server. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog), [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401), [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401), [Roblox DevForum 2020-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use CollectionService tags and one central script** (multiple-creators): Drive one script over many tagged instances instead of duplicating scripts (e.g. 300 killing bricks), so fixing one fixes all. Handle GetTagged together with GetInstanceAddedSignal and GetInstanceRemovedSignal so late or re-ordered objects still work. Tag with an attribute such as Target rather than relying on names. Place rarely-changing logic like streetlights or hazards in one central ServerScriptService script. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collectionservice-in-a-nutshell/668984), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collectionservice-in-a-nutshell/668984), [B Ricey 2022-04 10:31](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=631), [B Ricey 2023-04 14:12](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=852), [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use attributes correctly** (multiple-creators): Use GetAttribute and SetAttribute; attributes are not properties. Store attribute names in constants, since a typo silently creates a new attribute. Setting nil deletes it. React with GetAttributeChangedSignal. Prefer a few JSON-encoded attributes over hundreds for bulk data; encoded data may contain only numbers, strings, booleans and nested tables. Official limits: the first 64 attributes replicate, and names and string values max 50 characters. Sources: [B Ricey 2021-01 4:04](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=244), [B Ricey 2021-01 6:10](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=370), [B Ricey 2021-01 7:32](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=452), [B Ricey 2021-01 7:32](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=452), [B Ricey 2021-01 13:45](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=825). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep server-only logic and secrets out of client-visible code** (multiple-creators): Put gameplay logic in ServerScriptService and never mix server-only and client-only code in one ModuleScript; decompilation exposes passwords and secrets. Keep API keys outside source code. Enable Allow HTTP Requests and Studio Access to API Services in Experience Settings > Security before using them. Set Player.FrustumStreaming only on the server. Module-hiding tricks add protection but are not absolute. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/script-game-behavior), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/access-control), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/secrets), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Trim visual and per-instance cost on mobile** (multiple-creators): Disable CastShadow on foliage away from gameplay areas, review physics and rendering parameters near the end of development, and avoid DescendantAdded on the whole game for large batches, since it adds a script execution per instance. Do not use anti-lag scripts; the guide says they do the opposite of what they claim. Throttle bulk sound triggers so a child-added burst plays once. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/optimize-your-experience), [Crusherfire 2024-10 19:32](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=1172), [Roblox DevForum 2025-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345), [Crusherfire 2025-05 13:03](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=783), [Crusherfire 2025-05 13:03](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=783). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use AudioPlayer and animation markers** (multiple-creators): Legacy Sound, SoundGroup and SoundEffect are discouraged; use AudioPlayer, AudioEmitter, Wire and audio effects, and call AudioPlayer:Play() from a gameplay event. Listen to AudioPlayer.Ended as well as IsPlaying, since true-to-false changes may not replicate reliably. Guard rapid-click UI sounds with IsPlaying checks. Trigger hit frames and effects from GetMarkerReachedSignal on an AnimationTrack instead of timed waits. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/objects), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/objects), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-2D-audio), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-2D-audio), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Respect MessagingService budgets** (one-creator-repeated): Stay within per-server message budgets: sent 600 + 240 x players per minute; received per topic 40 + 80 x servers; subscriptions 20 + 8 x players; subscribe requests 240 per minute. Topics max 80 characters, messages 1,024 characters. Disconnect subscriptions (store the SubscribeAsync connection) when the player leaves, to avoid leaking subscriptions. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use gamepass prompts and server owner checks** (multiple-creators): Prompt gamepasses with MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase; PromptProductPurchase silently fails for them. Check ownership on the server with UserOwnsGamePassAsync when the player joins and apply perks there, not on the client. Map gamepass IDs to handler functions in a server module so new passes need no join-code changes. Pass player.UserId, not the Player, to BadgeService:AwardBadge. Sources: [B Ricey 2022-04 9:08](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=548), [Crusherfire 2024-03 1:38](https://youtu.be/kDCLGPMpLl0?t=98), [B Ricey 2022-04 15:03](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=903), [B Ricey 2022-04 12:17](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=737), [B Ricey 2022-04 13:39](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=819). Newest source 2024-03-27.
- **Cap servers and size leaderboard values** (multiple-creators): Set a server player limit before launch; one creator's servers were overwhelmed around 300 players. Keep spawn points at least 4 above max players to buffer reallocation errors. Leaderboards have a numeric cap of about 9.22 quadrillion, so store values scaled down (divide by 100) and restore on load. Fetch leaderboards on a long interval (120 s example) to limit DataStore and thumbnail load. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-06 14:35](https://youtu.be/e725ByFD8EI?t=875), [B Ricey 2024-02 16:18](https://youtu.be/FaEMgVDlaNQ?t=978), [janisjanis01 2026-05 10:01](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=601), [janisjanis01 2026-05 10:01](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=601), [Roblox DevForum 2019-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754). Newest source 2026-05-10.
- **Keep --!optimize 2 and avoid loadstring, getfenv and setfenv** (one-creator-repeated): Live games already run at optimize level 2, but Studio may differ, so add --!optimize 2 at the top of scripts to match. Level 0 disables all Luau optimisations. loadstring, getfenv and setfenv force safeenv false, which drops the script to level 0 and is also barred in Store assets. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941), [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941), [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941). Newest source 2025-09-18.
- **Write compiler-friendly Luau in hot paths** (one-creator-repeated): Avoid unnecessary closures (non-capturing functions compile to cheaper DUPCLOSURE). Use operators such as // for floor, # for length and % for modulo instead of builtins. Keep calls to FASTCALL builtins (math, string, table.insert, bit32, buffer, rawget, select). Use the generic for with ipairs, pairs or next. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941), [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941), [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941), [Roblox DevForum 2025-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941). Newest source 2025-09-18.
- **Batch remote calls into one per frame** (multiple-creators): Merge many small remote fires into one remote per frame. Packet benchmark: 1,000 fires went from 14 KB (RemoteEvent) to 1 KB. BridgeNet reported ~63 KB/s vs ~110 KB/s for 200 blank calls per frame. Batching is the largest single saving. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/bridgenet-insanely-optimized-easy-to-use-networking-library-full-of-utilities-now-with-roblox-ts-v199-beta/1909935), [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 49:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2982), [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 59:50](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=3590), [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/bridgenet-insanely-optimized-easy-to-use-networking-library-full-of-utilities-now-with-roblox-ts-v199-beta/1909935). Newest source 2025-03-26.
- **Do not batch unreliable events** (multiple-creators): Unreliable remotes are capped at 1,000 bytes per message and a lost batch drops many events. Reserve unreliable for event spam where drops are harmless. Use Packet's serialisation or compression only, not batching, on that path. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 49:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2982), [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 49:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2982), [ByteBlox 2024-02 6:06](https://youtu.be/VitH_k_-AZU?t=366). Newest source 2025-03-26.
- **Serialise hot payloads with buffers and the smallest types** (multiple-creators): Pack Vector3 (12 bytes), axis-aligned CFrame (13 bytes, vs ~200 as a JSON table) and Color3 (4 bytes) into buffers. Use U8 over F16 or any, and static or characters types for fixed sets. Roblox numbers cost 9 bytes vs U8 1 byte. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-luau-buffer-type-beta/2724894), [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-luau-buffer-type-beta/2724894), [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 9:19](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=559), [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 31:09](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=1869), [Suphi Kaner 2025-03 36:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2202). Newest source 2026-07-15.
- **Send each client only the instances and data it needs** (multiple-creators): Replicate per-player content (such as a player's interior) to that player instead of ReplicatedStorage. This saves bandwidth, CPU and memory, which matters most on mobile. For large NPC counts, skip replicated Humanoids and send position data for clients to render. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374), [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236). Newest source 2025-12-31.
- **Move many moving parts with physics, not per-frame CFrame writes** (one-creator-repeated): Physics-driven movement cost about half of CFrame writes at 1,000 parts (~4 ms vs ~8 ms). Replicating 100 parts cost ~271 KB/s via CFrame vs ~40 KB/s via physics. Physics parts also collide, but CFrame-moved parts don't fire Touched or query events, so choose by need. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2022-07 0:41](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=41), [Suphi Kaner 2022-07 6:19](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=379), [Suphi Kaner 2022-07 7:00](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=420), [Suphi Kaner 2022-07 10:04](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=604), [Suphi Kaner 2022-07 11:26](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=686). Newest source 2025-09-28.
- **Avoid server-side tweens for cosmetic motion** (one-creator-repeated): A server tween replicates every property change: incoming traffic rose from ~0.16 to ~1.66 KB/s in one test, while client tweens stayed flat. Create TweenInfo once and reuse it. Set final properties on the server after (DelayTime+Time)*(RepeatCount+1) so states match. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 0:00](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=0), [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 0:40](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=40), [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 9:42](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=582), [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 13:13](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=793), [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 15:18](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=918). Newest source 2023-02-27.
- **Throttle client look-vector updates to the server** (single-mention): Naive camera-CFrame remotes cost ~1.8 KB/s while rotating. A dot-product threshold of 0.99 (~8 degrees) plus a minimum 0.1 s interval dropped it to ~30–40 B/s slow and ~0.4–0.5 KB/s fast. Don't use 0 as the threshold or it never sends. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 27:59](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=1679), [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 41:58](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=2518), [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 30:45](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=1845), [Suphi Kaner 2023-02 35:46](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=2146). Newest source 2023-02-13.
- **Use OverlapParams or box queries for ability hits, not Touched** (multiple-creators): Query hitboxes with OverlapParams, excluding the caster, hitbox and temp folder. Track already-hit characters in a table so one target isn't damaged every frame. Box queries (GetPartsInBox) cost more than magnitude checks, so use them only for box or wide shapes. Sources: [ArTheDev 2026-10 22:53](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=1373), [ArTheDev 2026-10 22:09](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=1329), [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930), [SimTek Game Development 2022-12 8:28](https://youtu.be/c1jFXwmRtzI?t=508), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143). Newest source 2026-10-05.
- **Use RaycastParams Include and Exclude lists (2026 API)** (one-creator-repeated): ExcludeInstances and IncludeInstances are much faster than FilterDescendantsInstances, especially with large lists, and updating them is cheap. Set IncludeInstances = nil for the most permissive filter and {} for include-nothing. Exclusion wins if an instance is in both lists. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575), [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575), [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575), [Roblox DevForum 2026-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575). Newest source 2026-04-07.
- **Scope CollectionService queries and avoid expensive combinators** (one-creator-repeated): Create collections with a small rootInstance, not workspace-wide. Use class specifiers (Part#Killbrick) so non-matching classes aren't monitored. Avoid >, >>, has() and in-radius(), which are high-cost. Tag and attribute selectors don't speed queries up. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143), [Roblox DevForum 2026-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143). Newest source 2026-08-13.
- **Harden pathfinding: pcall ComputeAsync, check Status, handle Blocked** (multiple-creators): Wrap ComputeAsync in pcall with a retry cap (MAX_RETRIES 5, 5 s cooldown). Only proceed when path.Status == Success, else fall back (e.g. teleport). Advance waypoints on MoveToFinished. Recompute on Blocked only if the blocked index is ahead. Honour Jump waypoints. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2021-07 5:32](https://youtu.be/lJktfRT2pbg?t=332), [Suphi Kaner 2021-11 43:17](https://youtu.be/JyMxrcqEzu8?t=2597), [Roblox DevForum 2022-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779), [SimTek Game Development 2021-07 14:46](https://youtu.be/lJktfRT2pbg?t=886), [Suphi Kaner 2021-11 29:57](https://youtu.be/JyMxrcqEzu8?t=1797). Newest source 2022-07-03.
- **Throttle AI sight checks and cone scans** (multiple-creators): Run each NPC's sight check on a loop with task.wait(1) (~1 s) rather than every frame. Check a cheap distance and dot-product FOV before raycasting. Exclude the NPC model from the filter. Sample a few random points in a cone rather than a full 5-degree sweep when performance matters. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-realistic-npc-eyesight-system/2456522), [Roblox DevForum 2023-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-realistic-npc-eyesight-system/2456522), [Roblox DevForum 2023-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-realistic-npc-eyesight-system/2456522), [SimTek Game Development 2025-07 36:27](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=2187), [SimTek Game Development 2023-12 24:39](https://youtu.be/eDTbzVDm1aY?t=1479). Newest source 2025-07-28.
- **Treat thread recycling as an unverified optimisation** (one-creator-repeated): A pooled-thread approach was claimed to be ~2x faster than task.defer per defer, but this is one speaker's test, not an official benchmark. Measure with MicroProfiler before adopting it. Defer is the default for signals, so custom event classes should mirror that. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2024-08 1:23:19](https://youtu.be/DTEraIOfoS0?t=4999), [Suphi Kaner 2024-11 1:12:12](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=4332), [Suphi Kaner 2024-08 1:23:19](https://youtu.be/DTEraIOfoS0?t=4999), [Suphi Kaner 2024-11 46:24](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=2784), [Suphi Kaner 2024-08 21:52](https://youtu.be/DTEraIOfoS0?t=1312). Newest source 2024-11-28.
- **Use Parallel Luau only for heavy work and clamp worker counts** (multiple-creators): Actor overhead can make simple tasks slower, so reserve Parallel Luau (desynchronize) for expensive work like raycasting. Cap workers to rows (one per row) and sanitise inputs before creating Actors. Reference numbers: 32–64 Actors, and the client thread cap is 3. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566), [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design), [Roblox DevForum 2025-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566), [Roblox DevForum 2025-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Enable adaptive physics stepping** (single-mention): Set Workspace PhysicsSteppingMethod to Adaptive. Official docs say it can improve physics performance by up to 2.5x and is recommended in most cases. Verify with MicroProfiler on your own scene. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/adaptive-timestepping). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Enable AuthorityMode=Server deliberately** (single-mention): Setting Workspace AuthorityMode to Server auto-sets NextGenerationReplication, PlayerScriptsUseInputActionSystem, SignalBehavior=Deferred, StreamingEnabled and UseFixedSimulation. Those five settings are then required, so review them as a set before migrating an existing place. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993), [Roblox DevForum 2026-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993). Newest source 2026-07-09.
- **Run actions on the client first, validate on the server, and roll back on rejection** (single-mention): For responsiveness, run hit, damage and visual feedback immediately on the client, send the result to the server, and roll back if the server rejects it. Expect rollback edge cases. Keep damage authority on the server. Client-only VFX is safe; client hitboxes are exploitable. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940), [Roblox DevForum 2022-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487), [Roblox DevForum 2022-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487). Newest source 2025-10-31.
- **Gate actions with a shared state and an in-progress flag** (one-creator-repeated): Use one shared can't-act state so players can't attack, block or evade while stunned or busy. Set a summon-in-progress flag on the server before rolling to block duplicate requests and double-spending. Check is-playing before replaying animations to avoid overlap. Sources: [ArTheDev 2025-07 36:18](https://youtu.be/Pi8rQ9gGTKE?t=2178), [ArTheDev 2026-04 14:56](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=896), [ArTheDev 2025-07 16:14](https://youtu.be/csxJTfPVW8U?t=974). Newest source 2026-04-18.
- **Avoid Store-barred dynamic loading and inspect untrusted packages** (multiple-creators): In Store assets, require(id), LoadAsset, LoadString and getfenv/setfenv are blocked and prohibited by policy. Inspect scripts in packages you didn't create before running them, and save the place first so you can revert. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/protect-your-games-with-script-capabilities-sandboxing/4634642), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Cache and queue external HTTP calls** (one-creator-repeated): Enable response caching so identical requests aren't repeated. Route calls through a queue that retries 429 responses, rather than raw HttpService. Set maxSimultaneousSendOperations no higher than the service's limit (Trello example: 10 requests per 10 s, cooldown 10 s). Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2019-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-requests-simple-powerful-http-in-roblox/355294), [Roblox DevForum 2019-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-requests-simple-powerful-http-in-roblox/355294), [Roblox DevForum 2020-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587), [Roblox DevForum 2020-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587), [Roblox DevForum 2020-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587). Newest source 2020-05-03.
- **Use the DataStore queue pattern: RemoveAsync after processing** (single-mention): For ordered or queued processing, call RemoveAsync immediately after handling an item so it isn't processed twice. Set the invisibility timeout longer than your read-and-remove time. Add task.wait(1) after a failed ReadAsync so errors don't hammer the service. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/queue), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/queue), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/queue). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Retry saves a few times and check Studio API access** (single-mention): Retry failed saves in a loop, about three attempts with a short wait, since most transient failures clear on the second try. Enable Studio Access to API Services or saves appear to do nothing. For hot read keys, shard copies (Weather_1..3) by JobId; sharding does not fix write throttling. Sources: [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 2:47](https://youtu.be/YMvezOFyl8o?t=167), [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 4:47](https://youtu.be/YMvezOFyl8o?t=287), [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 4:47](https://youtu.be/YMvezOFyl8o?t=287), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Set hit-detection intervals instead of every frame** (one-creator-repeated): Run hit checks on an interval: ~0.05 s for magnitude checks, ~0.1 s for box and raycast loops, and ~0.1 s for projectile checks. Use one shared connection for all active projectiles rather than one per projectile, and move non-physics projectiles on the client. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332), [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332), [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930), [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930), [Roblox DevForum 2024-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332). Newest source 2024-06-24.
- **Limit knockback to short impulses and clean up forces** (one-creator-repeated): Destroy VectorForce and AngularVelocity after ~0.1 s with Debris so they act as impulses.
