Only apply forces when speed is under the threshold (~20 studs/s). Set RelativeTo to the root attachment so push follows the character. Example demo values were 10,000 force and 50 spin. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2024-11 20:13](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=1213), [SimTek Game Development 2023-02 11:37](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=697), [SimTek Game Development 2024-11 16:46](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=1006), [SimTek Game Development 2024-11 18:50](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=1130), [SimTek Game Development 2024-11 10:49](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=649). Newest source 2024-11-02.
- **Reduce Humanoid and animation callbacks; keep per-step code light** (single-mention): Limit callbacks on Humanoid.StateChanged, Running, Died and AnimationTrack KeyframeReached/Ended, since each adds per-step cost. Keep functions on RunService.Stepped, Heartbeat and RenderStepped light; spread expensive work across frames. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Pool tables and clear unbounded tables** (multiple-creators): Pool tables and reduce temporary table creation to lower Luau GC cost. Clear per-player tables once empty and remove entries on leave. Unbounded tables that never release entries cause memory growth and crashes. Destroy tracked instances on every creation path. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/monitor), [Suphi Kaner 2024-01 9:53](https://youtu.be/A14qFAyFYhQ?t=593), [ArTheDev 2026-04 12:26](https://youtu.be/RDjsR4iIRlg?t=746), [ByteBlox 2024-05 4:35](https://youtu.be/D6DwmHcmwwQ?t=275). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Pick weighted random outcomes with an early return** (multiple-creators): Roll first, then accumulate weights, and return as soon as the cumulative weight reaches the roll. Clamp the roll to [0, totalWeight]. Don't depend on pairs() order, since table iteration is unordered. Sources: [ByteBlox 2024-05 7:53](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=473), [ByteBlox 2024-05 10:35](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=635), [PineappleDev 2025-07 1:33](https://youtu.be/QRkRk-FHN4s?t=93), [ByteBlox 2024-05 9:54](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=594). Newest source 2025-07-15.
- **Handle streamed-in instances safely on the client** (multiple-creators): Don't scan Workspace for required instances at start; streamed content may not be loaded. Wait for Workspace.PersistentLoaded or WaitForChild. Handle existing children first, then connect ChildAdded so nothing is missed. Destroy client copies when a streamed model is removed. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/stream-in-immersion), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/stream-in-immersion), [Suphi Kaner 2023-08 33:37](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=2017), [Suphi Kaner 2023-08 36:22](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=2182). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Replace per-object polling loops with one manager and events** (multiple-creators): Avoid one background loop per object and world-wide scans every second. A safe-zone script scanning all world objects each second used about 30% of server resources. Keep one central table or manager, react to events, and yield so the server stays near 60 fps. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-08 30:33](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1833), [Last Level Studios 2020-08 30:33](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1833), [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 1:31](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=91), [Last Level Studios 2020-08 34:38](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=2078). Newest source 2025-12-03.
- **Track occupancy with entry/exit sensors, not GetTouchingParts** (one-creator-repeated): For track or block systems, model each block with entry and exit sensors. Add an entity to an in-block table on enter, remove it on exit or despawn. Run a safety loop that force-exits entities far from every sensor (about 1500 studs, roughly 3x train length, in one large train game). Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-08 4:29](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=269), [Last Level Studios 2020-08 31:53](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1913), [Last Level Studios 2020-08 33:57](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=2037). Newest source 2020-08-24.
- **Yield by time budget during bulk part loads** (one-creator-repeated): When initially processing hundreds of thousands of parts, yield on a time budget rather than after every part, or the load becomes unacceptably slow. Yield periodically so frame time stays healthy. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-08 21:38](https://youtu.be/0RRGneqSZRY?t=1298), [Last Level Studios 2020-08 30:33](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1833). Newest source 2020-08-24.
- **Audit redundant welds and break cycles yourself** (one-creator-repeated): Joints returned by GetJoints have a read-only Active property showing which redundant welds the engine uses. Delete inactive welds that contribute nothing. Welds that form a cycle are ignored arbitrarily by the spanning tree, so break cycles yourself when behaviour must be predictable. Sources: [ContextLost 2018-11 0:40](https://youtu.be/fNf3JtvrzRg?t=40), [ContextLost 2018-11 2:44](https://youtu.be/fNf3JtvrzRg?t=164). Newest source 2018-11-06.
- **Grant accessories with AddAccessory and per-player clones** (one-creator-repeated): Use Humanoid:AddAccessory instead of parenting hats to the character. Clone a real accessory from ServerStorage for each player so one hat can be granted to many. Check the character does not already have it first. Tag accessories with CollectionService so new hats only need a tag. Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-04 18:24](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=1104), [Emancyphur 2021-04 20:15](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=1215), [Emancyphur 2021-04 22:22](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=1342). Newest source 2021-04-23.
- **Use GetPartBoundsInBox instead of deprecated FindPartsInRegion3** (one-creator-repeated): FindPartsInRegion3 is deprecated; use workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox. Derive region corners from part.Position plus or minus part.Size/2 rather than placing manual corner parts. Use OverlapParams with FilterType Include and the character as the filter descendant so only the player's own parts are detected. Sources: [Amora 2023-07 3:57](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=237), [Amora 2023-07 6:21](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=381), [Amora 2023-07 7:44](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=464). Newest source 2023-07-31.
- **Enforce one active zone at a time and disconnect removed zones** (one-creator-repeated): Bind overlapping zones to a group such as EnterOnlyOneZoneAtATime so they do not fight over Atmosphere changes. When a zone is removed, disconnect its connections and nil them so it stops listening. Sources: [Emancyphur 2022-07 9:28](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=568), [Emancyphur 2022-07 10:52](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=652). Newest source 2022-07-25.
- **Run per-player atmosphere changes in a LocalScript** (single-mention): Atmosphere zone changes made on the server affect every player. Run zone visual changes in a LocalScript so each player sees their own zone state. Sources: [Emancyphur 2022-07 3:30](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=210). Newest source 2022-07-25.
- **Pick a zone system by entity and zone count** (single-mention): Zone detection cost grows with moving entities and zone count. The creator benchmarked 500 moving entities against 10,000 zones and got QuickZone at 60 FPS, QuickBounds 58, SimpleZone 5 and Zone Plus 4. Benchmark your own scale before choosing a zone library. Sources: [Possible Panda 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/C0cOB7Xqc1M?t=0), [Possible Panda 2026-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/C0cOB7Xqc1M?t=0). Newest source 2026-10-04.
- **Handle players already in the server on startup** (single-mention): Loop over Players:GetPlayers() and run the playerAdded handler with task.spawn, alongside the PlayerAdded connection. This covers players who joined before the script ran. Sources: [Amora 2023-08 8:33](https://youtu.be/vr1fae2qROM?t=513). Newest source 2023-08-03.
- **Use FindFirstChild for optional objects and WaitForChild for expected ones** (multiple-creators): Use FindFirstChild with a conditional when an object may not exist yet. Use WaitForChild(name, seconds) when the object will appear, and set a timeout explicitly, since the default is 5 seconds. WaitForChild yields the code below it, so place it in a separate script or reorder. Sources: [Emancyphur 2023-03 6:08](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=368), [Emancyphur 2023-03 6:49](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=409), [Emancyphur 2023-03 6:49](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=409), [LovJak 2025-05 1:12](https://youtu.be/M-o5hLZbjGY?t=72). Newest source 2025-05-03.
- **Read the error path, then check names exactly** (one-creator-repeated): Read the script path and line number first; the error names the missing child and the parent searched. Match spelling and capitalisation against Explorer or Properties, since names are case-sensitive (BrickColor, not Brickcolor). Use the full dot-path through nested folders; a search result elsewhere does not make the path valid. Sources: [Emancyphur 2023-03 0:56](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=56), [Emancyphur 2023-03 2:18](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=138), [Emancyphur 2023-03 3:43](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=223). Newest source 2023-03-08.
- **Give scripted objects predictable locations** (multiple-creators): Parent scripted emitters or parts to a named folder in workspace so the script can find them reliably. To run a script inside a part, set its Run Context to Client; ordinary LocalScripts only run in Backpack, Character, PlayerGui or PlayerScripts. Sources: [TheNexusAvenger 2015-04 22:23](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=1343), [Amora 2023-07 2:15](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=135). Newest source 2023-07-31.
- **Respect ColorSequence and NumberSequence constraints** (single-mention): Color3 values in a ColorSequence take 0 to 1, so convert RGB 0 to 255 values first. Size values in a NumberSequence are capped at 10 on the instance, and keypoints need time 0 to 1. These are older (2015) notes; confirm against current docs. Sources: [TheNexusAvenger 2015-04 18:12](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=1092), [TheNexusAvenger 2015-04 18:55](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=1135). Newest source 2015-04-24.
- **Gate Lighting Technology Future and provide a fallback** (single-mention): Future lighting only shows for players with 5 or more graphics bars (or Studio quality level 10 or higher). Others fall back to ShadowMap or Voxel. Do not make gameplay visibility depend on Future; this is a 2020 observation and should be re-checked. Sources: [Emancyphur 2020-08 0:58](https://youtu.be/ScdTM3ju-yk?t=58). Newest source 2020-08-02.
- **Sync visuals to audio by time, not by looping the table** (one-creator-repeated): Compute frame index as playback time multiplied by recording FPS (30 by default) instead of stepping through frames in a loop, which drifts when frames are dropped. Update bar sizes every render step (about 1/60 s) from the computed frame, not on a fixed timer. Sources: [TheNexusAvenger 2016-09 4:12](https://youtu.be/T5T9RMbjHBU?t=252), [TheNexusAvenger 2016-09 4:12](https://youtu.be/T5T9RMbjHBU?t=252). Newest source 2016-09-05.
- **Tune path costs and add waypoints when default paths fail** (multiple-creators): Set a material's pathfinding cost (0 makes it preferred) to steer enemy routes. When PathfindingService fails on stairs, add custom waypoints for that section and have enemies follow them. Precompute frequently used path positions into a module rather than calling PathfindingService every time. Sources: [Dev Total 2022-11 22:48](https://youtu.be/0K-5sifYhxs?t=1368), [Dev Total 2022-11 24:52](https://youtu.be/0K-5sifYhxs?t=1492), [Roox4 2023-04 3:15](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=195). Newest source 2023-04-15.
- **Build doors as PathfindingLink traversal** (one-creator-repeated): Use two anchored, non-collidable parts with Attachments on each side of the door, linked by a PathfindingLink. Open the door by setting its open BoolValue to true. Return true from PathfindingLinkReached, or it stalls the Forbidden movement loop; warn and return true as a fallback. Then humanoid:MoveTo and wait for MoveToFinished. Sources: [Crit 2025-10 4:00](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=240), [Crit 2025-10 6:01](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=361), [Crit 2025-10 7:26](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=446), [Crit 2025-10 8:07](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=487). Newest source 2025-10-07.
- **Separate NPC collisions and cap active pathfinding agents** (multiple-creators): Use collision groups so doors do not collide with NPCs and NPCs do not collide with each other; this reduces stuck NPCs and improves pathing performance. Limit active pathfinding agents and enemy counts, since too many PathfindingService users made one prototype very laggy. Sources: [Crit 2025-10 10:51](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=651), [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 1:31](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=91). Newest source 2025-12-03.
- **End enemy attack hitboxes when the animation ends** (single-mention): Make sure attack hitboxes stop when the animation ends. Lingering hitboxes that damage after an attack has stopped feel unfair and amateur. Sources: [Cumin 2025-12 20:45](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1245). Newest source 2025-12-23.
- **Guard data access until the profile has loaded** (single-mention): Check an is-loaded flag or listen for a per-player loaded signal before reading player data. This prevents nil errors before the profile loads. Sources: [Possible Panda 2025-12 13:00](https://youtu.be/0rmh0FpHBYM?t=780). Newest source 2025-12-29.
- **Chain raycasts beyond the 15,000-stud limit** (single-mention): Raycasts have a 15,000-stud maximum range. At extreme distances, chain several raycasts to reach nearby targets; the creator needed about 10 consecutive casts to hit an enemy 2 billion studs away. Sources: [Roox4 2024-12 6:14](https://youtu.be/_E1stqb2fNw?t=374), [Roox4 2024-12 6:14](https://youtu.be/_E1stqb2fNw?t=374). Newest source 2024-12-06.
- **Use closed-form ballistics for arcing projectiles** (one-creator-repeated): For non-linear projectiles such as grenades, use a closed-form ballistic equation to get position at any time instead of simulating physics objects. Keep bullets visual on clients and let the server simulate from start position, direction and velocity. Avoid rotating turrets with CFrame updates or mechanical constraints at scale. Sources: [Roox4 2025-06 2:46](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=166), [Roox4 2025-06 3:29](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=209), [Roox4 2025-06 5:34](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=334). Newest source 2025-06-19.
- **Set IK up with an Animator and non-colliding targets** (multiple-creators): IK Control silently fails without an Animator (AnimationController or Humanoid) present. Set IK target parts to CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery off and Massless on. Weld raycast parts to HumanoidRootPart, keep them unanchored, and use an Exclude filter with the whole model. Sources: [TheNexusAvenger 2022-09 4:12](https://youtu.be/C4ASAayPpkY?t=252), [Roox4 2024-06 2:44](https://youtu.be/DPpAsjUr278?t=164), [Roox4 2024-06 4:35](https://youtu.be/DPpAsjUr278?t=275). Newest source 2024-06-19.
- **Make viewmodel offsets relative to the camera** (single-mention): When offsetting viewmodels or CFrames, check whether the offset is in object space or world space. Mixing them broke scope alignment until the offset was made relative to the camera. Sources: [Roox4 2024-07 2:42](https://youtu.be/OYfrdTxNdjA?t=162). Newest source 2024-07-25.
- **Check the Network tab for service-call spam** (single-mention): Open the Network tab in developer stats and look for frequent calls to services such as FriendService, MarketplaceService or GamePassService. Reduce them on the server. Sources: [TechSpectrum 2021-01 15:26](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=926). Newest source 2021-01-23.
- **Use a tracked scope for cleanup (Scythe)** (one-creator-repeated): Scythe is a third-party cleanup library that can replace Trove or Maid. Use scythe.add(scope, object) rather than a method on the scope, since scopes are numeric IDs. Check scythe.isAlive(scope) before clean. Add RunService connections, cleanup functions, or any object with Destroy directly. Sources: [Possible Panda 2026-09 0:22](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=22), [Possible Panda 2026-09 2:27](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=147), [Possible Panda 2026-09 3:09](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=189), [Possible Panda 2026-09 3:18](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=198), [Possible Panda 2026-09 3:59](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=239). Newest source 2026-09-27.
- **Log onboarding funnel steps on the server** (single-mention): Call AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent(player, stepNumber, stepName) from server code, passing the player, a step number and a step name. Verify the signature against current docs before relying on it. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-09 3:26](https://youtu.be/e953Nvhpdjo?t=206). Newest source 2026-09-21.
- **Use a command framework for argument parsing** (single-mention): Use a command framework such as Commander so arguments, such as converting names to Player objects, are handled before your command code runs. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2020-03 23:08](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=1388). Newest source 2020-03-01.
- **Build a custom GUI when the default VR GUI misbehaves** (one-creator-repeated): Roblox's default VR GUI behaves oddly. The creator found building a custom GUI system easier than working around it. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2021-12 16:19](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=979), [Last Level Studios 2021-12 17:02](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=1022). Newest source 2021-12-09.
- **Disconnect animator and character signals before destroying them** (single-mention): Connections made to Animator, AnimationTrack or character events can keep connected tracks in memory after the instance is destroyed. Disconnect them explicitly, or register them in a Trove/Maid, before destroying the character or animator. This extends the general Trove/Maid cleanup rule with an animation-specific case. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 13:23](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=803). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Keep game rules as pure Luau modules and unit test them outside Studio** (single-mention): Put drop tables, crafting odds and pricing in plain modules that take all data as arguments and touch no services. Such modules can be tested from the command line; Roblox reports about 97% of these unit tests finish in under 1 ms. This complements the existing module-structure rule. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 21:53](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1313), [Roblox 2026-10 22:33](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1353). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Reuse Roblox's pre-built physics behavior scripts for vehicles** (single-mention): For cars, boats, drones and helicopters, start from Roblox's pre-built physics-based behavior scripts instead of generating a controller from scratch with an LLM. The source says these scripts retarget to new segmented meshes. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 12:35](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=755). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Use Character Control Library v2 for NPCs instead of Humanoid** (single-mention): Roblox presents CCL v2 as 2-4x more efficient than Humanoid, based on the speaker's internal evaluation. It has been in Studio beta since keynote week (2026-10), so confirm its release status and benchmark on target phones before shipping. Where available, this newer advice supersedes Humanoid-default NPC guidance. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 2:33](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=153), [Roblox 2026-10 2:33](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=153). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **For large NPC counts, push CCL and expect navigation v2 memory savings** (single-mention): For crowds, use CCL as far as possible. Navigation v2 is claimed to reduce nav mesh memory by up to 90%, but it was described as in development, so do not plan around that figure yet. Keep the existing pathfinding hardening rules in place. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 17:18](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=1038), [Roblox 2026-10 1:52](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=112). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Generate reserved server IDs with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false) on the server** (single-mention): Create reserved server GUIDs from a server Script using HttpService:GenerateGUID(false). Clients cannot predict or control the value if it is generated server-side. Do not generate or choose reserved server identifiers in a LocalScript or derive them from client-supplied data. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers). Newest source 2026-10-30.
- **Put custom camera code in a client LocalScript** (one-creator-repeated): The camera is client-side, so camera logic belongs in a LocalScript under StarterPlayer > StarterPlayerScripts. Keep custom camera work minimal, consistent with the existing 'avoid custom camera scripts unless necessary' rule. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 0:42](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=42). Newest source 2026-09-19.
- **Loop until Workspace.CurrentCamera exists** (one-creator-repeated): CurrentCamera can be nil at startup. WaitForChild does not work on it because it is a property, not a child instance. Poll or loop until it is non-nil before using it. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 2:49](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=169). Newest source 2026-09-19.
- **Wait for the character before reading it** (one-creator-repeated): Local scripts can run before the character loads. Call Player.CharacterAdded:Wait() before reading the character, then WaitForChild the Humanoid and Head before iterating character parts to avoid load-order races. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 3:31](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=211), [SimTek Game Development 2026-08 15:31](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=931). Newest source 2026-09-19.
- **Read the character CFrame with GetPivot** (single-mention): Use character:GetPivot() for the character's CFrame. The source calls it the preferred method over reading HumanoidRootPart. Newer than the older root-part habit. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 3:31](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=211). Newest source 2026-09-19.
- **Multiply per-frame motion by delta time** (multiple-creators): For rotation, zoom, or any stepped motion in Heartbeat or RenderStepped, multiply the step by delta time. Without normalization, speed varies with frame rate (a fixed per-frame step runs faster at higher FPS). Sources: [Stewiepfing 2026-08 10:28](https://youtu.be/Apqkcrd_gz0?t=628), [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 9:44](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=584). Newest source 2026-09-19.
- **Default to plain CFrames for orientation** (single-mention): Plain CFrames are sufficient for most orientation work. Reach for quaternions mainly when combining rotations or smoothly blending between orientations. Low-advice source; treat as a guideline. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2026-08 4:49](https://youtu.be/Apqkcrd_gz0?t=289). Newest source 2026-08-09.
- **Tune zoom endpoints and step values to remove end-of-zoom jolt** (single-mention): A linear zoom from 60 studs to 12 studs at -1 stud per frame worked in the source, but the author adjusted height and focus values (3.25 and 1.5) to remove a jolt at the end of the zoom. Expect to tune endpoints and offsets per game. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 2:09](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=129), [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 2:09](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=129), [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 9:44](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=584), [SimTek Game Development 2026-09 11:48](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=708). Newest source 2026-09-19.
- **Build team loadouts from ServerStorage folders matched to Teams** (one-creator-repeated): Name each ServerStorage loadout folder exactly as its Team and validate folder names against Teams before granting. Map tool name to team name in a dictionary for fast lookup. Handle teams added at runtime via Teams.ChildAdded and each team's PlayerAdded. Check that the Player, Character and Backpack exist before use. Destroy unauthorised tools after a short delay (about 1 s) to avoid unequip/remove races. Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-01 4:00](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=240), [Emancyphur 2021-01 16:03](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=963), [Emancyphur 2021-01 28:25](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=1705), [Emancyphur 2021-01 5:49](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=349), [Emancyphur 2021-01 43:28](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=2608). Newest source 2021-01-28.
- **Teleport with TeleportAsync inside pcall, and test on a published place** (one-creator-repeated): Deprecated teleport methods (TeleportToPlace etc.) are consolidated into TeleportAsync, so use that. Wrap it in pcall because it can fail. It only works in published experiences, not Studio. Studio cannot reserve servers, so publish and read the access code to test reserved-server flows. Sources: [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 7:18](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=438), [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 9:21](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=561), [Suphi Kaner 2022-08 21:17](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=1277). Newest source 2022-08-09.
- **Give every recursive generator a base case and a depth limit** (one-creator-repeated): Put an explicit if-return base case at the top of each recursive function. For branching structures (e.g. fractal trees) pass a depth counter and stop at a MAX_DEPTH constant (the example used 10). Clone a template part per branch, parent to Workspace, and anchor parts so the generated structure stays fixed. Sources: [B Ricey 2023-10 0:53](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=53), [B Ricey 2023-10 6:51](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=411), [B Ricey 2023-10 16:19](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=979), [B Ricey 2023-10 0:53](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=53). Newest source 2023-10-20.
- **Put grid snapping in one shared module used by client and server** (multiple-creators): Place snapping math in a ModuleScript in ReplicatedStorage so client preview and server validation compute identically. Snap with floor division (//), which handles negatives correctly, using one GRID_SIZE constant. Pick placement direction with math.sign on the relative snapped component. Store face directions as unit vectors multiplied by block size so a size change touches one constant. Sources: [B Ricey 2024-01 5:16](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=316), [B Ricey 2024-01 5:16](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=316), [B Ricey 2024-01 5:57](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=357), [B Ricey 2024-01 17:18](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=1038), [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 4:51](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=291). Newest source 2025-12-15.
- **Block placement with a shrunk overlap box and Include filters** (one-creator-repeated): Check the target cell with workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox and OverlapParams using an Include list; Whitelist/Blacklist are deprecated. Shrink the box about 0.2 studs per axis so adjacent blocks touching on edges do not cause false positives. Sources: [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 32:21](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1941), [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 33:01](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1981), [SimTek Game Development 2025-12 33:01](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1981). Newest source 2025-12-15.
- **Create leaderstats as a Folder with exact names on PlayerAdded** (one-creator-repeated): Create a Folder named exactly 'leaderstats' under each Player on PlayerAdded, then add IntValue or NumberValue children (e.g. 'coins', lowercase) so the built-in leaderboard displays them. Note that the name is case-sensitive in practice; a wrong name silently hides the stat. Sources: [ArTheDev 2025-02 1:00](https://youtu.be/twXk5B5G5dA?t=60), [ArTheDev 2025-06 0:41](https://youtu.be/2M8r1oU2k3U?t=41). Newest source 2025-06-16.
- **Define named handlers once and connect them** (single-mention): Define handler functions once at module or script scope and connect them per player or character, rather than creating a new lambda for each. Repeated closures add memory per connection on long-running servers. Sources: [Crusherfire 2024-05 6:48](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=408). Newest source 2024-05-22.
- **Make pickups non-colliding and award them once** (one-creator-repeated): Set pickup CanCollide to false so players do not bump them; they still fire Touched. Attach the Touched handler directly where the object is created, and disconnect after the first hit so several body parts cannot award twice. Touched does not fire between two anchored parts; use GetPartsInPart on a small probe part for that case. Sources: [ByteBlox 2025-01 12:28](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=748), [ByteBlox 2025-01 13:09](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=789), [ByteBlox 2025-01 14:33](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=873), [ByteBlox 2025-01 19:58](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=1198). Newest source 2025-01-24.
- **Pair CollectionService listeners with a startup query and scope to Workspace** (one-creator-repeated): Use GetInstanceAddedSignal together with a GetTagged loop so tagged parts present at startup are also set up. Filter to descendants of Workspace so tagged copies stored in ServerStorage are ignored. Sources: [GnomeCode 2025-05 3:39](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=219), [GnomeCode 2025-05 4:19](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=259). Newest source 2025-05-25.
- **Test AI-generated code one system at a time and profile after each** (multiple-creators): Do not trust generated code. Test each system (coins, checkpoints, upgrades) separately, since an AI coin listener that fired only for future coins left existing coins broken. Test for deprecated methods, and profile after adding systems: one test dropped from about 100 to 18 FPS when buying many upgrades. Clear references to deleted objects to avoid memory growth. Sources: [ByteBlox 2025-01 7:11](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=431), [Duckable 2025-08 7:09](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=429), [Duckable 2025-08 10:49](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=649), [Duckable 2025-08 10:49](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=649). Newest source 2025-08-29.
- **Use frame-rate-independent smoothing** (one-creator-repeated): Replace fixed lerp factors with alpha = 1 - math.pow(smoothness, dt), where dt is frame delta time. Use delta time for all timing in per-frame visual effects so behaviour matches across frame rates. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2026-03 5:42](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=342), [Stewiepfing 2026-03 0:17](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=17). Newest source 2026-03-29.
- **Use assert with a message for required values** (single-mention): Call assert(condition, message) when a required value is missing so the script fails loudly with a clear reason instead of silently continuing with nil. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2026-03 7:55](https://youtu.be/JG1fkhCKAB4?t=475). Newest source 2026-03-15.
- **Clamp UI progress ratios and bind them to stat changes** (one-creator-repeated): Wrap updates in a function such as updateProgressBar(percent). Clamp the ratio with math.clamp(n, 0, 1) so the fill never overflows when a value exceeds its target. Drive the bar from the leaderstat's GetPropertyChangedSignal('Value') so it updates automatically. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2024-06 3:52](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=232), [AlvinBlox 2024-06 3:52](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=232), [AlvinBlox 2024-06 4:40](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=280). Newest source 2024-06-08.
- **Typewrite dialogue with a skip** (one-creator-repeated): Build dialogue as say(message): clear the label, loop over message:split("") and append each letter with a short task.wait(). Let the player click to finish instantly, since waiting out a long line feels slow. Sources: [ByteBlox 2025-01 8:01](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=481), [ByteBlox 2025-01 6:40](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=400). Newest source 2025-01-31.
- **Observe ClickDetectors with one global observer** (single-mention): Use an observer over the ClickDetector class so every detector gets a connection, and the connection disconnects automatically when its parent is removed. This avoids per-object connection bookkeeping. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2026-04 9:23](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=563). Newest source 2026-04-12.
- **Use ConnectParallel on RunService events inside an Actor** (one-creator-repeated): To split demanding per-frame work (e.g., terrain generation) across CPU cores, connect RunService events with ConnectParallel. The script must sit inside an Actor. Call task.desynchronize inside the parallel code to switch to parallel execution. task.spawn and coroutines do not run in parallel. Sources: [Stewiepfing 2024-05 6:53](https://youtu.be/0gOLJ4-ea8w?t=413), [Stewiepfing 2024-05 7:34](https://youtu.be/0gOLJ4-ea8w?t=454). Newest source 2024-05-28.
- **Write require paths as literal full paths** (one-creator-repeated): Use literal paths such as ReplicatedStorage.Modules.X. The plugin tooling cannot resolve paths built through intermediate variables, so the navigation and checks fail on those requires. Sources: [Crusherfire 2024-05 3:32](https://youtu.be/yncfwrBAWVw?t=212). Newest source 2024-05-30.
- **Navigate functions with Go To Declaration** (one-creator-repeated): Use Studio's native Go To Declaration (Ctrl+click) on functions. It works across modules but not on require() lines for ModuleScripts, so navigate from the function call instead. Sources: [Crusherfire 2024-05 2:10](https://youtu.be/yncfwrBAWVw?t=130). Newest source 2024-05-30.
- **Animate each tagged part in its own thread** (single-mention): When one script animates every part with a tag, a single loop may animate only the first part. Run each part's animation in its own function or task.spawn thread so all parts move. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-03 1:24](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=84). Newest source 2024-03-08.
- **Check that parts exist before animating them** (single-mention): Procedurally generated parts may not be in Workspace yet. A script that looks them up without checking fails silently. Use FindFirstChild for optional parts, or WaitForChild if they are expected, before starting the animation. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-03 2:46](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=166). Newest source 2024-03-08.
- **Track active animations to block duplicate loops** (single-mention): Keep a table of parts that are already animating. Skip any part already in the table so it does not get a second loop, and add a cooldown to limit lag from repeated starts. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-03 3:29](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=209). Newest source 2024-03-08.
- **Make item gifting and trading server-validated and dupe-test them** (single-mention): The creator found a dupe bug in item stealing and gifting that had to be fixed before launch. Validate every transfer on the server and test transfers for duplication before release. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-07 9:21](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=561). Newest source 2025-07-13.
- **Check error counts for single-line floods** (single-mention): One creator saw 51,000 errors, caused mostly by a single line of code. Check the error count in the developer dashboard or Output, find the repeating line, and fix it first. Sources: [janisjanis01 2024-04 0:00](https://youtu.be/7z8XlhAtGpQ?t=0). Newest source 2024-04-14.
- **Prefer proven pathfinding tutorials over unverified AI scripts** (single-mention): Pathfinding enemies took hours to get working when built from unverified AI-generated scripts. Start from established tutorials or known patterns, then test each step. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-10 6:44](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=404). Newest source 2025-10-11.
- **Drive leaderboard numbers through the Value property** (single-mention): Change the IntValue's Value to update the number shown on the leaderboard. Renaming the value object does not change the displayed stat name. Keep the leaderstats folder name exact. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2018-12 13:20](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=800). Newest source 2018-12-08.
- **Never rename built-in services** (single-mention): Renaming a built-in service breaks GetService lookups and confuses anyone reviewing the code. Keep service names as Roblox defines them. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2020-03 18:15](https://youtu.be/jOFrwMkl8LI?t=1095). Newest source 2020-03-20.
- **Identify the player from a Touched hit before collecting** (multiple-creators): Read the touching part as the Touched parameter, then check game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(hit.Parent) before running collection logic. Return early if it is nil. Pair with a debounce. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2020-04 23:48](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=1428), [GnomeCode 2021-03 5:02](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=302). Newest source 2021-03-22.
- **Filter children by class before changing them** (single-mention): When looping a character's GetChildren(), check IsA('BasePart') or MeshPart before changing properties, since many children are not parts. Sources: [GnomeCode 2020-04 3:38](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=218). Newest source 2020-04-02.
- **Reuse animation tracks by name before loading** (single-mention): Check animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() for an existing track with the same name and reuse it. Loading a new track on every call hits the 256-track-per-Animator limit. Sources: [GnomeCode 2022-01 1:48](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=108), [GnomeCode 2022-01 1:48](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=108). Newest source 2022-01-04.
- **Use GetPropertyChangedSignal for a single property** (single-mention): Connect to GetPropertyChangedSignal('Name') rather than .Changed. Each .Changed listener runs on every property change, adding processing cost. Most recent advice on this item (2024-06). Sources: [AlvinBlox 2024-06 2:42](https://youtu.be/paRHxtuk_Mc?t=162). Newest source 2024-06-02.
- **Save original positions before moving objects** (single-mention): Store an object's original Position in a variable before moving it so it can be reset later, for example after a goal or round end. Sources: [GnomeCode 2021-03 10:54](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=654). Newest source 2021-03-22.

## Checklist
- [ ] Name leaderstats folder exactly
- [ ] Key DataStores by UserId and never rename
- [ ] pcall all DataStore calls
- [ ] Save in PlayerRemoving and BindToClose
- [ ] Handle ProcessReceipt on server idempotently
- [ ] Validate all remote inputs on server
- [ ] Add server cooldowns to remotes
- [ ] Keep unreleased code in ServerStorage
- [ ] Test on a real low-end Android
- [ ] Check in-camera triangles with Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+F2 (~500k)
- [ ] Keep draw calls ~500
- [ ] Keep client memory under ~1.3 GB on 2 GB devices (F9)
- [ ] Profile with Microprofiler Ctrl/Cmd+F6
- [ ] Check recv stat Ctrl/Cmd+F7
- [ ] Enable StreamingEnabled
- [ ] Limit moving server assemblies to ~50
- [ ] Avoid RenderStepped unless needed
- [ ] Spread heavy loops across frames
- [ ] Audit lights with woot3 plugin
- [ ] Reuse MeshId and material
- [ ] Consider server authority mode
- [ ] Test with 60 ms latency 10 ms jitter
- [ ] Generate remotes in code
- [ ] Return useEffect cleanup and pass dependencies
- [ ] Attach version/device metadata to alerts
- [ ] Put cosmetic popups and effects in a LocalScript, and cap spawn counts.
- [ ] Wrap slow or independent async calls in task.spawn with pcall.
- [ ] Use WaitForChild on every required module inside replicated scripts.
- [ ] Scale UI stroke thickness by viewport height using the 1080p baseline formula.
- [ ] Format numbers only in the display module, never before sending.
- [ ] Check fast movers with sub-step position tests, not just frame endpoints.
- [ ] Gate experiment GetValue calls behind the feature check.
- [ ] Group related config values into one JSON config and add them ahead of time.
- [ ] Call ConfigService and GetConfigAsync only from server scripts, inside pcall with fallbacks.
- [ ] Connect UpdateAvailable to Refresh for config snapshots, and use GetValueChangedSignal.
- [ ] Check GetBadgeInfoAsync IsEnabled before AwardBadgeAsync; use UserHasBadgeAsync at PlayerAdded.
- [ ] Call RankProductsAsync once at join and cache for the session.
- [ ] Avoid custom cameras in VR unless essential; merge VR camera fixes when you must.
- [ ] Enable instance streaming for headset builds.
- [ ] Keep server CPU and client CPU each under 16 ms per frame.
- [ ] Keep server memory under 50% of total and ideally under 3 GB.
- [ ] Fix leaks, and check startup allocations are parented or destroyed.
- [ ] Use viewer breadcrumbs and Studio Explorer to trace bloated nodes.
- [ ] Use MicroProfiler process groups and memory tags to locate expensive work.
- [ ] Remove unique IDs from error messages before they are logged.
- [ ] Order error rules by priority, test regex, and keep under 100 custom rules.
- [ ] Verify errors with F9, Script Profiler and Output before publishing.
- [ ] Store transferRequestId from PromptRobuxTransferAsync and correlate with receipts.
- [ ] Check MembershipType before Premium logic; debounce the Premium modal (about 5 s).
- [ ] Configure RTBF deletion templates first; use webhooks only for complex erasure.
- [ ] Set Workspace.SignalBehavior to Deferred explicitly, and yield before reading values that callbacks set.
- [ ] Put heavy per-frame compute under Actors; call task.desynchronize() and task.synchronize() before writes.
- [ ] Split parallel work across several Actors; never call require inside desynchronized code.
- [ ] Check each API member's thread-safety tier before using it in parallel code.
- [ ] Move models with Model:PivotTo(); replace SetPrimaryPartCFrame calls.
- [ ] Tween the root CFrame (TweenService directly), not Position or CFrameValue proxies.
- [ ] Use Box or Hull CollisionFidelity for small or non-interactive meshes; set CanTouch false where unused.
- [ ] Anchor only the assembly's root part; set AlignPosition MaxVelocity to a finite value.
- [ ] Debounce every Touched handler with a flag or attribute cooldown.
- [ ] Route all ProximityPrompt events through ProximityPromptService in one service script.
- [ ] Track every connection, observer and zone in a Trove or Maid and destroy it on cleanup.
- [ ] Enable Workspace PlayerCharacterDestroyBehavior and test cleanup on PlayerRemoving.
- [ ] Nil-check every Workspace:Raycast result; set CanQuery false on parts that should be ignored.
- [ ] Destroy projectiles past a max distance and clean spent bullets with Debris:AddItem.
- [ ] Profile before and after each micro-optimisation; keep only changes that show gains.
- [ ] Use task.defer or task.spawn, store the thread, and task.cancel unneeded work; avoid spawn().
- [ ] Wrap every DataStore, MemoryStore and MessagingService call in pcall and check the success flag.
- [ ] Count UpdateAsync as two requests; check GetRequestBudgetForRequestType before request bursts.
- [ ] Start concurrent HTTP calls with task.spawn; honour 429 retry-after headers.
- [ ] Choose the smallest fixed-width type or buffer for high-frequency remote payloads.
- [ ] Validate remote arguments with T or T.interface, then recheck outcomes server-side.
- [ ] Use workspace:GetServerTimeNow() for timers and simulation, not tick() or os.time().
- [ ] Use CollectionService tags with GetInstanceAddedSignal handling, not duplicated scripts.
- [ ] Use AudioPlayer, and trigger effects from animation markers rather than timed waits.
- [ ] Set the server player cap and spawn buffer before launch; scale leaderboard values under the cap.
- [ ] Add --!optimize 2 to new scripts; check Studio Output with a test run.
- [ ] Remove loadstring, getfenv and setfenv from scripts that run in live or Store builds.
- [ ] Merge remote fires into one remote per frame; use Packet or buffers for hot payloads.
- [ ] Keep unreliable remotes for spam only and never batch them.
- [ ] Pack Vector3, CFrame and Color3 into buffers; use U8 and static types where values are bounded.
- [ ] Replicate per-player content only to the player who needs it.
- [ ] Use physics for many moving parts; check Touched behaviour against CFrame moves.
- [ ] Create TweenInfo once; avoid server tweens for cosmetic motion.
- [ ] Throttle client-to-server camera or look-vector sends with a dot threshold and minimum interval.
- [ ] Use OverlapParams with exclusions for ability hits; track hit targets per cast.
- [ ] Migrate RaycastParams to ExcludeInstances/IncludeInstances in new code.
- [ ] Scope CollectionService to a small root and avoid >, >>, has() and in-radius() in queries.
- [ ] Wrap ComputeAsync in pcall with a retry cap and check path.Status before reading waypoints.
- [ ] Throttle AI sight to ~1 s intervals and check FOV with a dot product before raycasting.
- [ ] Benchmark Parallel Luau and thread recycling in MicroProfiler before adopting them.
- [ ] Set Adaptive physics stepping and verify the frame-time change.
- [ ] Gate currency actions with an in-progress flag and a shared can't-act state.
- [ ] Retry DataStore saves about three times; enable Studio API access before testing saves.
- [ ] Use Debris to destroy forces, casts and temporary instances; disconnect connections on end.
- [ ] Test on a midrange Android tablet, not just a gaming desktop.
- [ ] Use the Developer Console (F9) memory view and debug.setmemorycategory to find memory hogs.
- [ ] Use Graph view to find what holds references keeping objects alive.
- [ ] Use the AnimationGraph's SetParameter for complex blends; keep LoadAnimation where it works.
- [ ] Migrate Motor6D code to Attachments or AnimationConstraints before the 2026 changes break it.
- [ ] Keep distant props and off-screen effects from animating each frame; gate by a configurable range.
- [ ] Replace per-object background loops with one central manager table and events.
- [ ] Time-budget bulk part loads and yield periodically to keep the server near 60 fps.
- [ ] Use entry/exit sensors instead of GetTouchingParts polling for track or block occupancy.
- [ ] Check Joint.Active on joints from GetJoints and delete inactive redundant welds.
- [ ] Grant hats with Humanoid:AddAccessory; clone from ServerStorage per player and check for duplicates.
- [ ] Tag accessories with CollectionService so new hats need only a tag.
- [ ] Replace FindPartsInRegion3 with workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox.
- [ ] Use OverlapParams with FilterType Include and the character as the filter descendant.
- [ ] Bind overlapping zones to EnterOnlyOneZoneAtATime and disconnect zone listeners on removal.
- [ ] Run per-player atmosphere changes in a LocalScript.
- [ ] Benchmark zone libraries at your real entity and zone counts before choosing one.
- [ ] Handle players already in the server by looping GetPlayers with task.spawn.
- [ ] Use FindFirstChild with a conditional for optional objects.
- [ ] Use WaitForChild(name, seconds) with an explicit timeout for expected objects.
- [ ] Read the error's script path and line first, then check spelling and case.
- [ ] Set Run Context to Client for scripts that must run inside a part.
- [ ] Convert RGB 0-255 to 0-1 for Color3 in ColorSequences; keep NumberSequence sizes at 10 or below.
- [ ] Provide a fallback when using Lighting Technology Future.
- [ ] Compute animation or audio frame index from playback time, not a loop.
- [ ] Set material path costs and add custom waypoints where default stairs paths fail.
- [ ] Build doors with PathfindingLink and return true from PathfindingLinkReached.
- [ ] Set collision groups so doors and NPCs do not collide with NPCs.
- [ ] Cap the number of active pathfinding agents.
- [ ] End enemy attack hitboxes when the animation ends.
- [ ] Guard player data reads with an is-loaded check or loaded signal.
- [ ] Disconnect animator and animation-track signals before destroying a character or its Animator.
- [ ] Move drop tables, crafting odds and pricing into pure modules that take data as arguments, then add command-line unit tests.
- [ ] Before writing a vehicle controller, check whether Roblox's pre-built physics behavior scripts (car, boat, drone, helicopter) cover the need.
- [ ] For large NPC counts, benchmark CCL v2 against Humanoid on target phones if the beta is available in your Studio build.
- [ ] Confirm navigation v2 has shipped before counting on its up-to-90% nav mesh memory reduction.
- [ ] Label CCL v2 and navigation v2 figures as beta or speaker-stated when citing them.
- [ ] Generate any reserved server ID inside a server Script with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false).
- [ ] Confirm that no reserved server ID is generated, chosen, or derived from client input or client-side code.
- [ ] Put camera code in a LocalScript under StarterPlayerScripts.
- [ ] Loop until Workspace.CurrentCamera is non-nil before using it.
- [ ] Call Player.CharacterAdded:Wait() before reading the character.
- [ ] WaitForChild the Humanoid and Head before iterating character parts.
- [ ] Use character:GetPivot() for the character CFrame.
- [ ] Multiply per-frame rotation and zoom steps by delta time.
- [ ] Use plain CFrames by default; use quaternions only to combine or blend rotations.
- [ ] Tune zoom start, end, step, and offset values per game; check for end-of-zoom jolt.
- [ ] Clone accessory templates before AddAccessory so ServerStorage templates stay unchanged (already covered by existing per-player clone rule; confirm it is applied).
- [ ] Name ServerStorage loadout folders exactly as their Team and validate names before granting items.
- [ ] Use TeleportAsync inside pcall; never the deprecated teleport methods.
- [ ] Test teleports and reserved servers only on a published place, not in Studio.
- [ ] Add an if-return base case to every recursive function.
- [ ] Add a MAX_DEPTH counter to recursive generation over branching structures.
- [ ] Put grid snapping math in a ReplicatedStorage ModuleScript used by client and server.
- [ ] Snap with // and a single GRID_SIZE constant.
- [ ] Shrink placement overlap boxes about 0.2 studs per axis.
- [ ] Use OverlapParams Include lists, not Whitelist or Blacklist.
- [ ] Create leaderstats as a Folder with the exact name 'leaderstats' under each Player.
- [ ] Connect named handler functions once instead of per-player lambdas.
- [ ] Set pickup CanCollide to false and disconnect Touched after the first award.
- [ ] Pair GetInstanceAddedSignal with GetTagged and filter to Workspace.
- [ ] Test each AI-generated system separately, then profile FPS after adding it.
- [ ] Use alpha = 1 - math.pow(smoothness, dt) for frame-independent smoothing.
- [ ] Use assert(condition, message) for required values.
- [ ] Clamp UI progress ratios with math.clamp(n, 0, 1).
- [ ] Give dialogue typewriter text a click-to-finish skip.
- [ ] Keep every ConnectParallel script inside an Actor before connecting to RunService events.
- [ ] Call task.desynchronize inside parallel code before doing the heavy work.
- [ ] Learn Actors, the Microprofiler, and how threads map to CPU cores before adding parallel Luau.
- [ ] Wrap MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync in pcall and warn on failure so an API error does not break the script.
- [ ] Use Ctrl+click Go To Declaration on functions; do not expect it to work on require() lines.
- [ ] Write require paths as literal full paths, not paths built through intermediate variables.
- [ ] Re-check memory growth and button logic after launch with real players; leaks and pointer bugs can appear only then.
- [ ] Before animating procedurally generated parts, confirm they exist in Workspace (FindFirstChild or WaitForChild).
- [ ] Run each tagged part's animation in its own thread so one loop does not skip parts.
- [ ] Keep a table of parts already animating and skip duplicates; add a cooldown.
- [ ] Validate all item gifting and trading on the server and run dupe tests before launch.
- [ ] Check the error count when it spikes and fix the single line producing most errors first.
- [ ] Build pathfinding from a known tutorial or pattern before adapting AI-generated code.
- [ ] Use game:GetService for every service call, consistently.
- [ ] Confirm no built-in service or leaderstats name was renamed when code fails to find it.
- [ ] In each Touched handler, resolve the player with GetPlayerFromCharacter(hit.Parent) and return early if nil.
- [ ] Before LoadAnimation, look for an existing track by name in GetPlayingAnimationTracks().
- [ ] Use GetPropertyChangedSignal for single-property watches instead of .Changed.
- [ ] Filter GetChildren() loops with IsA('BasePart') before changing properties.
- [ ] Store original Position values before moving parts so they can reset.
- [ ] Change IntValue.Value to update leaderboard numbers; do not rely on renaming objects.
