## Where creators agree
- Official and indie creators (RobloxOfficial, AlvinBlox, Leif, TheDankDeveloper, Roblox) agree the server must be authoritative and validate client input.
- Roblox staff (RobloxOfficial, RobloxCreators, Roblox) agree on testing on real low-end Android/phones and prioritising memory.
- RobloxOfficial and RobloxCreators agree on avoiding per-frame work and spreading heavy work.
- AlvinBlox and TheDankDeveloper agree on learning from existing code.
- Official Creator Docs and indie creators agree on server-only handling of sensitive calls (ConfigService, badges, Robux transfers, receipts), each wrapped in pcall.
- Both official and indie sources agree that slow or rate-limited calls should not block the main game loop, using task.spawn or a single call at join.
- Official docs and indie advice agree that memory and CPU problems should be found with profiling tools (MicroProfiler, Developer Console, Script Profiler) before changing code.
- Prefer task.wait/task.* over deprecated wait and spawn, and avoid polling loops in favour of events (T5834, T6063, T5835, T6064, T5494).
- Disconnect connections and destroy or release instances when done; unreleased connections and upvalues leak (T4952, T5158, T5735, T8264).
- Only heavy computation benefits from parallel Luau; serial code that already fits the budget gains nothing (T5879, T10102, T10106).
- Keep authority on the server: validate hits and rewards there, and keep secrets out of client-visible modules (T5471, T7505, T9186, T9306).
- Use CollectionService tags to drive many instances from one script (T5306, T5999, T6065).
- Wrap network-backed calls in pcall and check results before indexing them (T8725, T10046, T9480).
- Debounce Touched and prompt handlers with a flag or cooldown (T7691, T8780, T8764).
- Keep damage, hit authority and purchases on the server; only cosmetic VFX goes to clients (T11367, T12148, T11901, T13304, Q1202).
- Prefer events and debounced touch handling over polling loops (T10826, T11513, T12042, T15542).
- Profile before micro-optimising, using MicroProfiler and debug.profilebegin/profileend (T11390, T11391, T15607).
- Slice long work across frames and use Parallel Luau for heavy compute only (T11395, T15260, T15261).
- Batch remote traffic and keep payloads small (T10823, T11431, T12652).
- Clean up instances and connections; pair every creation with a destruction path (T12109, T12587, T13863).
- Last Level Studios (T15834, Q1491) and Lnor Roblox (N6452) agree that world-wide polling loops are costly and should be centralised.
- Crit (T16287) and Lnor Roblox (T16346) agree that separating NPC collisions and limiting active pathing agents matters for pathing performance.
- Emancyphur (T15995, T15996) and LovJak (T16156) agree that WaitForChild is for objects that will appear, and FindFirstChild for optional ones.
- TheNexusAvenger (T15972) and Roox4 (T16071, T16074) agree IK needs an Animator and non-colliding target parts.
- SimTek Game Development (T17884) and Stewiepfing (T17794) both say per-frame rotation or motion should be multiplied by delta time for frame-rate-independent behaviour.
- The new startup-race tips (T17783, T17880, T17881) agree with the existing 'Handle players already in the server on startup' and 'WaitForChild for expected ones' rules.
- Handle instances that exist at startup and ones added later (Emancyphur T18004, GnomeCode T18253, ByteBlox T18156).
- Test generated or new code before trusting it (ByteBlox T18156, Duckable T18285).
- Profile FPS after adding systems, not just after writing them (Duckable T18286, Emancyphur T18008 style advice aside).
- Share identical math on client and server for placement (B Ricey T18086, SimTek T18311).
- Prefer event connections over polling or per-frame loops (T19003 on events vs polling; T18971 on adding waits inside while loops).

## Where creators disagree
- **Client vs server hit detection**
  - RobloxOfficial 2018: favor active attacker with client-driven logic (T66); RobloxCreators 2022: FPS client detection with server checks (T644)
  - RobloxOfficial 2018: competitive games should move hits to server (T72)
  - Roblox 2026: server authority gives latency compensation without custom code (T2186, T3124), reducing the tradeoff
- **Fast projectile handling**
  - RobloxOfficial 2018: client network ownership and bigger colliders (T77)
  - Roblox 2026: create on both client and server under server authority (T3127); prefer newer
- **Per-frame hook choice**
  - RobloxOfficial 2018: prefer Heartbeat/Stepped over RenderStepped (T132)
  - Roblox 2026: under server authority use bind to simulation, not RenderStepped or Heartbeat (T3120), plus fixed heartbeat (T2188)
- **Whether Instances can be held in weak tables**
  - Crusherfire (T6051-T6053): weak-key caches keyed by objects are auto-removed when unreferenced; use kv to avoid self-reference leaks.
  - Crusherfire (T6054-T6055): docs say instances are never weak and behaviour is inconsistent; hold a strong reference and release on Parent=nil. Test before relying on weak tables for instances.
- **Attribute name length limit**
  - B Ricey (N2324, 2021): stated limit 100 characters.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (N3274, N3275, 2026-10): max 50 characters for names and string values. Official 2026 figure wins.
- **Source of trustworthy server time**
  - Roblox DevForum (T5296, 2020): avoid os.time; fetch external time via the Google HTTP Date header.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T7070, T7864, 2026-10): use workspace:GetServerTimeNow() or time() for simulation and timers. Official guidance supersedes the external-fetch workaround.
- **WaitForChild on GUI children and on the server**
  - Crusherfire (T5863, T5860): index StarterGui children directly and avoid WaitForChild on server scripts.
  - Roblox Creator Docs (T8578, T8303): use WaitForChild when progress requires it or when requiring modules that may load first; dot access is safe for ServerScriptService and ServerStorage paths. Context-dependent, not strictly contradictory.
- **Value of micro-optimisations**
  - Roblox DevForum (T4909, 2016): profile; some changes have no effect because the newer Luau VM already optimises them.
  - Roblox DevForum (T4911, T4912, T5497): precompute constants, cache methods in locals, and store GetChildren outside loops. Apply after profiling rather than by default.
- **Batching unreliable remote events**
  - Roblox DevForum (BridgeNet post, T11431) batches calls per frame to avoid ordering stalls.
  - Suphi Kaner (T12653) says not to batch unreliable events: they cap at 1,000 bytes and lost batches drop many events.
- **Thread recycling vs task.defer**
  - Suphi Kaner (T12480, T12251) claims thread recycling is ~2x faster than task.defer; the claim is unverified.
  - Roblox official signal behaviour (T12246) defers by default, which is the baseline to measure against.
- **Client-first action vs server-only hits**
  - Roblox official (T13005) recommends running hit, damage and visuals on the client first, then rolling back if rejected.
  - Roblox DevForum (T11367, Q1202) keeps hitboxes and damage on the server because client hitboxes are exploitable.
- **Parallel Luau for small tasks**
  - Roblox DevForum (T12921) says actor overhead can make simple tasks slower.
  - Roblox official (T15261) suggests considering it for heavy off-thread work.
- **Whether to build a custom character controller or use the library**
  - Roox4 (T16127): if physics-driven movement matters, build a custom character controller because Humanoid overrides applied forces.
  - Existing knowledge file: use the Character Controller Library. Not a new creator statement; resolve by use case.
- **Fixed per-frame zoom step vs delta-time normalization**
  - SimTek Game Development: N7653 uses a fixed -1 stud per RenderStepped frame in the tutorial.
  - SimTek Game Development: T17884 says to normalize by delta time because fixed steps vary with frame rate.
  - Internal tension within one creator; the tutorial value is a simplification, and the delta-time advice is the more robust choice.
- **Per-frame visual code**
  - Stewiepfing (T18340): drive all visual effects from PreRender/RenderStepped every frame, with delta time.
  - Existing file rule: avoid per-frame work and use events and sliced work. Keep per-frame code to visuals only, and profile on phones.
- **Touched use for pickups versus folder listeners**
  - ByteBlox (T18157): attach Touched directly where objects are created instead of a folder-level listener.
  - Existing file rule: debounce every Touched handler; Touched is not the first choice for ability hits.

## Date context (what may be outdated)
- 2018 official advice (Heartbeat vs RenderStepped, network ownership, instancing lacking outlines/Humanoids, Studio DataStore workflow) predates server authority, input actions, CCL, Slim; prefer 2025-2026 guidance where it conflicts.
- Several 2026 items (safety callback, queue service, Player Data Store, Slim for NPCs) are upcoming/not yet released.
- Budget numbers (500k triangles, 500 draw calls, 1.3 GB) are from 2024-09 and may shift.
- Indie claims are self-reported.
- N1477 (2020-03) sets a 200-player per-server target. Newer official material (2024-02, N1576) describes experiences at 1m+ CCU, so prefer the newer figure.
- The 2026-10 official docs add frame-time budgets (16 ms per side), a server memory ceiling (3 GB), and error-tracking rules. Treat these as current. Older mobile memory advice (target 2 GB devices) still applies to clients.
- Guidance on custom cameras (T4035, 2026-10) says to replace them only if necessary. Older advice that custom cameras were a normal choice should be revised.
- Chat performance (T3696, 2025-09) is now under 1 ms and free when disabled. Older chat-cost assumptions are outdated.
- Items tagged low-advice (T3586, T3587, T4341, N1805) come from weaker sources; check them before relying on them.
- 2015-2016 DevForum micro-optimisation and CFrame advice predates the modern Luau VM; profile before applying (T4909).
- 2018-2020 advice favours wait(), spawn() and ypcall. Current guidance is task.wait, task.spawn/defer, pcall (which can yield) and xpcall for tracebacks (T5834, T6063, T5182, T5183, N2297).
- SetPrimaryPartCFrame is deprecated; use Model:PivotTo() and GetPivot() (T5105, T10095, T9175).
- FindPartOnRay and the RaycastFilterType options are deprecated; use Workspace:Raycast with Include or Exclude (T5336, T5318, T6085).
- Parallel Luau guidance from 2024 (T5879-T5888) describes early Actor workflow; the 2026-10 official model (T10102-T10111) is current and should be followed where they differ.
- As of 2026-10, official docs recommend Deferred SignalBehavior; the default still equals Immediate, so set it explicitly (T8981, T8985).
- Legacy Sound objects are discouraged in favour of AudioPlayer, AudioEmitter and Wire (T7626, 2026-10).
- Attribute limits moved to 50-character names and string values per official docs; the 100-character figure from 2021 is superseded (N3274, N2324).
- Leaderboard value cap of 9.22 quadrillion is a 2026 observation; re-verify before relying on it (N2750, T6504).
- 2026-01: AnimationConstraints replace Motor6D; C0/C1 and Part0/Part1 setters error on them (T13157, T13158). Older Motor6D Transform advice (T12609, T12613, T12615, 2025-03) must be rechecked.
- 2026-04: RaycastParams.ExcludeInstances/IncludeInstances are the faster API and supersede FilterDescendantsInstances (T11305, T11500, T11778, T11973) (T13273).
- 2025-12: BindToSimulation replaces FixedHeartbeat for predicted simulation (T13093); Humanoid:SetStateEnabled does not replicate (T13109).
- 2024-08: Roblox signals defer by default (T12246), which makes spawn/direct-call custom events a legacy pattern.
- 2025-09: All live scripts run at --!optimize 2; loadstring, getfenv and setfenv drop to level 0 (T12953, T12954).
- 2026-05: Store policy bans require(id), LoadAsset and LoadString in Store assets (T13388).
- 2026-07: AuthorityMode=Server bundles five engine settings (T13563, N5311).
- 2026-08: Collection query cost tiers were published; some pre-2026 query advice may be outdated (T13683, N5371).
- Older raycast and pathfinding guidance from 2020–2023 (ypcall, Region3, pathfinding tutorials) predates current APIs; ypcall is deprecated (N4277).
- 2015-2016 sources (T15659-T15664) describe older ColorSequence, NumberSequence and audio-sync practice; confirm against current docs before use.
- FindPartsInRegion3 is deprecated (Amora, 2023-07, T16015). Prefer GetPartBoundsInBox; the manual Region3 corner method (T16013) is superseded.
- Lighting Technology Future advice (2020, T15787) and server-scale figures (2020, N6133, N6177) predate current engine and graphics tiers; re-check.
- PathfindingLink door traversal (T16283-T16287, 2025-10) is the newest pathing guidance and supersedes older door workarounds.
- Newest items (2026-09 to 2026-10: T16681, T16703-T16708, T16720) are self-reported and not verified against official docs.
- As of 2026-10, CCL v2 is a Studio beta (since keynote week); its API and performance figures may change before release.
- Navigation v2 was described as in development (N7590, T17749), not shipped; treat its memory figure as a target.
- Where CCL v2 is available, the 2026-10 advice (T17744) supersedes older Humanoid-default NPC guidance for large NPC counts.
- T17751 (Roblox Creator Docs [OFFICIAL], 2026-10) is the newest item in this batch. It is the current platform guidance for reserved server GUID generation.
- New items span 2026-08 to 2026-10. Camera and character-startup tips (T17879–T17882, T17884, T17890) are from 2026-09 and are the most recent guidance on this topic.
- GetPivot (T17882, 2026-09) is presented as preferred over reading HumanoidRootPart; this is newer than the older root-part habit and is consistent with the existing PivotTo rule.
- Camera distances and tuned values (N7651–N7654) are from one 2026-09 tutorial and are game-specific. Do not treat them as defaults for current Roblox camera behaviour.
- T17793 and T17975 are marked low-advice; weight them below multiple-creator and official-style guidance.
- Older teleport tutorials (2021-2022) use TeleportToPlace and similar; these are consolidated into TeleportAsync (T18031, 2022-08). Treat pre-2022 teleport code as stale.
- OverlapParams Whitelist/Blacklist is deprecated per SimTek (T18312, 2025-12); use Include.
- Studio cannot reserve servers (T18042, 2022-08); recheck this against current Studio behaviour.
- The GetAsync rate limit (N7740) comes from a 2022 video; verify against current DataStore docs.
- Emancyphur tutorials (2021) predate newer APIs; prefer newer creators' guidance where they conflict.
- AI-generated code advice (2025) reflects current LLM-assisted workflows; the warning about deprecated methods still applies.
- TheDevKing items (2019-11) predate current Actor and Parallel Luau tooling; the gamepass pcall tip is consistent with current practice, but the character-wait tip duplicates an existing rule. Treat the 2019 items as low weight.
- Stewiepfing (2024-05) and Crusherfire (2024-05) Parallel Luau and tooling tips are marked low-advice. Verify current ConnectParallel and task.desynchronize behavior in official docs before relying on it.
- janisjanis01 (2025-12) post-launch leak note is a single anecdote; the general memory-leak rule already exists.
- New items span 2024-03 to 2025-10 and come from one indie creator (self-reported). Treat them as anecdotes, not platform guidance.
- Q1942 (2024-03) is a bare quote with no detail, so it adds no actionable rule.
- As of 2026-11, check the tagged-part and pathfinding patterns against current Roblox docs before relying on them.
- Most new items are 2018–2022 beginner tutorials from AlvinBlox and GnomeCode; the APIs remain valid but were not verified against the 2026 engine.
- T19022 (2024-06) is the most recent item; where it overlaps older advice on .Changed, prefer GetPropertyChangedSignal.
- N8188 (256 track limit) is from 2022; treat it as a reference point to re-check.
- T18990 (2020) uses Touched for player collection. The existing rule prefers OverlapParams for ability hits, so keep Touched for simple pickups only, with a debounce.

## Instructions for an agent using this file
- If a feature grants currency/items, then implement and validate on the server and never trust client values.
- If handling a purchase, then use ProcessReceipt with PurchaseId dedupe, return PurchaseGranted only after saving, NotProcessedYet if player absent.
- If writing DataStore code, then key by UserId, pcall, save on PlayerRemoving and BindToClose.
- If building a competitive or character-movement game, then recommend server authority mode and bind to simulation; replace old anti-fly or fake-projectile hacks.
- If optimising mobile, then check in-camera triangles (~500k), draw calls (~500), memory (~1.3 GB on 2 GB) on a real low-end device.
- If a script runs every frame, then convert to events or spread across frames, and avoid RenderStepped unless needed.
- If frame drops are reported, then triage: real hardware, frame rate, stats, Microprofiler, compare dumps.
- If many parts or NPCs exist, then use StreamingEnabled, anchor/disable idle NPC animators, avoid server-only attributes on parts.
- If asked about input hints, then use PreferredInput and input actions; if building React UI, use cleanup, dependency arrays and bindings.
- If an indie claim conflicts with official mechanics, then prefer the official source.
- If a frame or CPU problem is reported, check the 16 ms budget on client and server first, then profile with MicroProfiler before changing code.
- If a server crashes shortly after startup, look for uncleaned startup clones and memory spikes before anything else.
- If a config or badge call is needed, run it on the server inside pcall with a fallback value.
- If a config value changes, refresh the snapshot on UpdateAvailable rather than reading it once.
- If error logs are noisy, strip unique IDs first, then add ordered Group or Ignore rules, testing each regex before saving.
- If a VR build uses a custom camera, merge current VR camera fixes or replace the camera with the default.
- If a rewarded ad or Robux transfer is involved, store the returned IDs and correlate them with ProcessReceipt.
- If a UI looks wrong on phones, scale strokes and sizes with viewport height.
- If code uses task.desynchronize or ConnectParallel, check each API member's thread-safety tier and wrap every write in task.synchronize().
- If a signal handler depends on a value set by a callback, set SignalBehavior to Deferred explicitly and yield before reading.
- If a cache is keyed by Roblox Instances, do not assume weak tables collect them; hold a strong reference and release it on Parent=nil.
- If a Touched or ProximityPrompt handler can fire repeatedly, add a debounce flag or attribute cooldown before doing work.
- If you claim an optimisation helps, profile before and after with os.clock or the Script Profiler; keep it only if it shows gains.
- If a DataStore or Open Cloud call happens in bulk, check GetRequestBudgetForRequestType, count UpdateAsync as two requests, and keep GetAsync caching on.
- If a remote sends numeric data at high frequency, choose the smallest fixed-width type or a buffer, and send only fields the client needs.
- If a source conflicts with 2026-10 official docs, follow the official doc and note that the older advice is superseded.
- If gameplay or reward logic runs on the client, move it to ServerScriptService and validate it server-side.
- If many objects share one behaviour, use a CollectionService tag and one central script rather than duplicated scripts.
- If code uses Motor6D C0, C1, Part0 or Part1 and the target place has AnimationConstraints, migrate to Attachments before editing.
- If writing a raycast with filters, use ExcludeInstances or IncludeInstances, not the older FilterDescendantsInstances.
- If a remote fires many small events per frame, batch them into one per frame; keep unreliable remotes for event spam only.
- If an NPC or projectile needs collisions, use physics; if it only moves cosmetically on many parts, benchmark CFrame vs physics first.
- If a feature spends currency or grants rewards, add a server-side in-progress flag and validate every input.
- If a path is needed, wrap ComputeAsync in pcall, check Status, and fall back safely.
- If a Store asset is being built, remove require(id), LoadAsset, LoadString and getfenv/setfenv.
- If a claim is a single speaker's benchmark, treat it as unverified and measure in MicroProfiler on a midrange device before adopting it.
- If bulk-loading thousands of instances, yield on a time budget rather than after each item.
- If zones overlap, bind them to a one-at-a-time group and disconnect listeners on removal.
- If an object is required, use WaitForChild with an explicit timeout; if optional, use FindFirstChild with a conditional.
- If PathfindingService fails on stairs, add custom waypoints; precompute hot routes into a module.
- If doors block NPC paths, build a PathfindingLink and return true from PathfindingLinkReached.
- If you need a world-wide scan every second, replace it with events or a central manager.
- If you use FindPartsInRegion3, switch to GetPartBoundsInBox.
- If you need hit or projectile physics for many objects, prefer closed-form math on the server over replicated parts.
- If you need a bigger zone count, benchmark QuickZone against your current library at real scale.
- Treat all indie figures (QuickZone, Rover, safe-zone 30%, springs) as self-reported; verify on your own hardware before relying on them.
- If a project needs many NPCs and CCL v2 is available in the user's Studio build, prefer it over Humanoid and benchmark it; otherwise keep Humanoid and the existing pathfinding rules.
- If the user is building drop tables, odds or pricing, put the logic in a pure module that takes data as arguments, and suggest command-line unit tests.
- If the user asks for a car, boat, drone or helicopter, check Roblox's pre-built physics behavior scripts before generating custom code.
- When destroying a character or animator, disconnect its animation and character signals first.
- Do not present navigation v2 memory savings as shipped; say it was described as in development.
- Label beta and speaker-stated figures (CCL v2 2-4x, nav v2 up to 90%) as unverified when citing them.
- If code creates a reserved server identifier, generate it on the server with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false), not on the client.
- If a reserved-server flow needs an ID, have the server create it and hand it to clients only as needed, rather than letting clients choose it.
- Treat [OFFICIAL] Roblox Creator Docs as platform ground truth when it conflicts with indie advice.
- If writing camera code, place it in a LocalScript under StarterPlayerScripts and loop until Workspace.CurrentCamera exists.
- If reading the character on join or spawn, call Player.CharacterAdded:Wait() first, then WaitForChild the Humanoid and Head before iterating parts.
- If a rotation, zoom, or other stepped motion runs in Heartbeat or RenderStepped, multiply the step by delta time.
- If you need the character's CFrame, use character:GetPivot() in new code.
- Default to CFrame for orientation; introduce quaternions only when combining rotations or blending orientations.
- Do not copy tuned camera numbers (60, 12, -1, 3.25, 1.5) into other games; tune per game and check for end-of-zoom jolt.
- Keep camera and visual logic on the client; server-side code should not drive camera state.
- Treat low-advice sources (T17793, T17975) as optional guidance, not as required practice.
- If the task involves teleporting, use TeleportAsync inside pcall and do not suggest deprecated teleport methods.
- If the task involves recursion, require a base case and a MAX_DEPTH counter before generating structures.
- If the task involves grid building, place snapping math in a shared ReplicatedStorage module and use // with one GRID_SIZE constant.
- If placement or occupancy checks are needed, use OverlapParams Include lists with a box shrunk about 0.2 studs per axis.
- If generated code is involved, test each system separately and profile FPS after each addition.
- If an instance may exist at startup and also be added later, handle both paths.
- If writing per-frame smoothing, use alpha = 1 - math.pow(smoothness, dt).
- If a required value is missing, fail loudly with assert(condition, message) instead of continuing silently.
- If a pickup or Touched event is involved, set CanCollide false and award once with disconnect.
- If the source is indie, label figures as self-reported and verify against official docs before presenting them as platform fact.
- If per-frame CPU work is heavy and cores are idle, use ConnectParallel inside an Actor with task.desynchronize; otherwise keep normal connections.
- Before enabling any parallel Luau, profile with the Microprofiler to confirm the bottleneck and the thread layout.
- If a require path is built through variables, rewrite it as a literal full path so Studio tooling can resolve it.
- When calling UserOwnsGamePassAsync, wrap it in pcall and warn on failure rather than letting the error break the script.
- After a live release, re-check memory and button logic with real players, since leaks can appear only under real load.
- If an animation script for tagged parts does nothing, first check that the parts exist in Workspace and that the tag query returns them.
- If only the first tagged part moves, give each part its own thread.
- If a part gets animated more than once, add an active-animation table and skip duplicates.
- If item transfers can be duplicated, move the logic to the server and add dupe tests before launch.
- If error counts spike, find the single repeating line before changing other code.
- If pathfinding fails, start from a tested tutorial rather than adapting untested AI output.
- If a Touched handler collects or awards something, resolve the player with GetPlayerFromCharacter(hit.Parent), return early on nil, and debounce.
- If an animation plays repeatedly, look up an existing track by name in GetPlayingAnimationTracks() before calling LoadAnimation.
- If watching one property, connect to GetPropertyChangedSignal instead of .Changed.
- If a loop over character children changes properties, filter with IsA('BasePart') first.
- If a service, folder, or stat fails to resolve, check exact spelling and capitalisation first and confirm nothing was renamed.
- If a leaderboard number is wrong, update the IntValue's Value rather than renaming objects.
- Before moving an object that must reset later, cache its original Position.

## Not covered here (research elsewhere)
- No coverage of Luau type-checking strictness, --!strict, or native codegen details.
- No deep treatment of DataStore limits, session locking, or ProfileService/ProfileStore.
- No concrete anti-exploit patterns beyond validation (rate limits, sanity checks, remote security audit).
- No guidance on Parallel Luau/Actors or memory profiling beyond F9.
- No details on object pooling, task library vs wait, or Janitor/Maid cleanup patterns.
- Release status and API names of 2026 features (CCL, Slim, device integrity) need verifying in current docs.
- No details on exact Developer Console or Creator Dashboard error-rule UI workflow beyond the listed rules.
- No guidance on the internals of sub-step collision checks or how many steps to use.
- No details on Extended Services for Compute pricing or setup.
- No specifics on Right to Erasure template configuration steps or which data stores are supported.
- No hardware targets for mobile phones beyond the 2 GB memory guideline.
- No coverage of ExperimentService setup details or how to choose experiment cohorts.
- No guidance on rate limits for RankProductsAsync, GetUsersPriceLevelsAsync or GetBadgeInfoAsync beyond the rate-limit warning.
- No coverage of the ad reward flow timing or fill rates for rewarded video.
- No measured mobile-device frame times for Actor-based parallel Luau; evidence is one desktop-style test (T5886).
- No numeric DataStore per-minute budget formulas beyond throttle and failure thresholds (N3132, N3133); check the DataStore budget docs.
- No anti-exploit detection heuristics or rate-limiting numbers for remotes beyond validation and per-player state; research exploit-detection practice separately.
- No guidance on strict mode, Luau type checking or lint configuration.
- No benchmark with clear units for BulkMoveTo versus individual CFrame sets; the source reports ~0.49 ms vs 0.255 ms ambiguously (T6257).
- No MicroProfiler guidance specific to Actor and parallel phases beyond general profiling (T8254, T8853).
- No data-session locking, migration or ProfileService-style patterns for DataStore safety.
- No StreamingEnabled tuning thresholds or streaming-radius numbers for scripting performance.
- No measurements on real low-end phones or 2 GB devices beyond general claims; test your own build.
- No official benchmarks for Packet, BridgeNet or buffer serialisation; most figures are one creator's tests.
- No guidance on anti-exploit detection or rate-limit tuning for specific remotes beyond validation and cooldowns.
- Pathfinding and flowfield performance numbers are missing, as are NPC budgets per server.
- Parallel Luau and Actor performance numbers are thin and mostly from one post.
- Memory budgets per device class and GC tuning beyond table pooling are not covered.
- No coverage of server heartbeat, server CPU per-player scaling, or Open Cloud quota planning beyond one note.
- Terrain generation and map-building advice is not scripting and was not merged.
- No [OFFICIAL] sources in this batch; API claims (AnalyticsService funnel, Scythe, Timer, Color/NumberSequence limits, Lighting tiers, Run Context) need verification against current docs.
- No exploit or security content in the new items; the security rules need separate sources.
- No mobile-specific numbers for zones, pathing or accessories; the mobile budget rules rely on older sources.
- Scythe and Commander are third-party libraries; check their licence and maintenance before adopting.
- No hardware specs for the QuickZone benchmark; repeat on your target devices.
- No data on DataStore, Open Cloud or MessagingService budgets in this batch.
- No official agent or pathing limits; the cap on pathfinding agents is unquantified.
- Accessory cost, layered clothing and Humanoid:AddAccessory performance at scale are not covered.
- No independent benchmarks for CCL v2 or navigation v2; all figures are Roblox- or speaker-stated.
- No API details, code samples or exact names for CCL v2, the vehicle behavior scripts, or the command-line test setup.
- No detail on how to run the command-line test harness outside Studio or which test framework is used.
- No guidance on which animation or character connection types leak, or how to detect the leak (MicroProfiler or memory tags).
- No information on customising or licensing the pre-built vehicle behavior scripts.
- Low-advice world-model figures (N7467, N7468: 16 FPS, context window drops with players) were excluded as unrelated to Luau scripting or performance.
- The source does not explain how reserved servers are created or joined, or how the GUID is used afterward.
- No details are given on whether the GUID needs to be stored, how long it stays valid, or how to protect its transport to clients.
- The source does not say whether the false argument (no braces) matters for downstream parsing.
- No official Roblox documentation is cited for the camera, quaternion, or delta-time items; verify against the Camera and RunService docs.
- Does not cover the default camera zoom limits (for example CameraMaxZoomDistance or CameraMinZoomDistance) or whether custom zoom should replace them.
- Does not explain whether WaitForChild on Humanoid and Head is actually required given Roblox character load order (for example CharacterAppearanceLoaded).
- Does not cover the performance cost of RenderStepped on low-end mobile devices or the cost of per-frame camera updates.
- Does not cover quaternion math details, interpolation for orientation blending, or CFrame.Lerp versus tween approaches.
- Does not cover the cleanup loop for despawning objects past an end zone in depth (partly covered by existing projectile cleanup rule).
- None of these sources cite official Roblox docs or DevForum posts directly; verify TeleportAsync, OverlapParams, GetAsync limits and the Studio reserved-server limit on creator.roblox.com.
- Security and exploit handling for grid placement (reach limits, permissions, server-side validation of each placed block) is not covered.
- Team loadout security (preventing clients from requesting tools they should not have) is only lightly covered.
- Mobile performance of placement systems, typewriter UI, and per-frame visual effects is not covered.
- Data persistence for leaderstats values and team loadouts (DataStore integration) is not covered here.
- Localisation and rich text for typewriter dialogue is not covered.
- Accessibility and skip behaviour details beyond a click-to-finish option are not covered.
- No benchmark data on how much ConnectParallel or Parallel Luau improves frame time or server CPU in these sources.
- No fallback policy for UserOwnsGamePassAsync failures (e.g., retry count or default ownership value).
- No steps for diagnosing post-launch leaks or button-logic bugs beyond the general memory-leak guidance.
- No official-source confirmation of the 2024 ConnectParallel behavior; these items are indie self-reported.
- No cooldown durations or animation counts were given, so there are no performance benchmarks for tagged-part animation.
- The source does not show how the dupe bug was fixed or what server checks prevent item duplication.
- The 51,000-error source does not identify which line caused the errors.
- The pathfinding source names no tutorial or pattern, and the AI-code distrust quote (Q1942) has no context.
- Nothing here covers the official status of the tagged-part, animation, or pathfinding APIs in 2026.
- No current (2026) guidance on replacing Touched-based pickups; check the existing OverlapParams and CollectionService rules.
- The 256 Animator track limit is from a 2022 warning; verify the current limit on the official Creator Hub before relying on it.
- No mobile or performance benchmarks for Touched event cost or animation track overhead.
- No coverage of server-side security for pickups or leaderboard values beyond the existing rules.
- No guidance on ViewportFrame cleanup performance or cost beyond clearing children (T18993).
- Indie figures (62 Touched firings, 2-second delay) are demo-specific and not platform benchmarks.

## Quotes (near-verbatim)
- "Wouldn't sending server updates every render step end up using a decent amount of traffic on the server and on the client?" ([Roblox DevForum 2018-08 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434))
- "It's not good practice to put the player's name in the DataStore; it's best to use their user ID." ([AlvinBlox 2018-08 5:12](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=312))
- "When a function references a variable from an outside scope it holds a strong reference to that value!" ([Roblox DevForum 2019-10 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954))
- "Use the dot product instead of magnitude to avoid unnecessary square root calculations, which are expensive." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-06 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943))
- "i'm not a big fan of loops running all the time in the background it generally implies some sort of bad programming practice" ([Last Level Studios 2020-08 30:33](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1833))
- "Sine is closer to the Linear EasingStyle than any other EasingStyle." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-easing-styles/937281))
- "Quint is the fastest, Quart is the second fastest, and Quad is the slowest." ([Roblox DevForum 2020-12 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-easing-styles/937281))

- "The loop style is extremely inefficient and will cause major lag throughout your game." ([Roblox DevForum 2021-09 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/open-source-lock-on-combat-in-3d/1458506))

- "Server sided VFX not only increases server load but also increases ping tremendously." ([Roblox DevForum 2022-05 ](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487))

## Official 2026 engine and API changes

_Chunk 12 (2026-11-01). Labels: OFFICIAL = Roblox Creator Docs release notes or staff DevForum posts. Status is as stated by the source (Pending = listed in release notes but not yet live). Items marked DEPRECATED name a deprecated, removed or sunset API/behaviour. Dates are post or release dates; 'Release N ~date' = Studio/engine release N, date approximate from the DevForum release-notes post._

- **Rejected engine yield no longer resumes unrelated thread** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 744 ~2026-10-22): Fixes an issue where an engine API whose yield was rejected could later resume the same thread at an unrelated suspension point. Build impact: Removes a source of hard-to-trace thread resumption bugs in yielding code. Source: Creator Docs release notes 744.
- **Parallel and animation-phase fixed-frequency binds** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 743 ~2026-10-15): RunService:BindToAnimation() runs fixed frequency callbacks prior to animation updates. RunService:BindToSimulationParallel and RunService:BindToAnimationParallel are parallel Luau equivalents of the non-parallel binds. Build impact: Parallel variants are available for fixed-frequency logic that should run in parallel Luau. Source: Creator Docs release notes 743.
- **TeleportService reserved-server APIs deprecated** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 743 ~2026-10-15): TeleportService:ReserveServer(), ReserveServerAsync(), TeleportToPrivateServer(), TeleportOptions.ReservedServerAccessCode and TeleportOptions.ShouldReserveServer are deprecated. Build impact: Use TeleportService:TeleportAsync() with TeleportOptions.ReservedServerId or VipServerId. **DEPRECATED:** TeleportService:ReserveServer(), ReserveServerAsync(), TeleportToPrivateServer(), TeleportOptions.ReservedServerAccessCode, TeleportOptions.ShouldReserveServer Source: Creator Docs release notes 743.
- **Luau table and metatable performance gains** (OFFICIAL, Live (table.find change pending), Release 743 ~2026-10-15): Dynamic-key access t[x] is 10-18% faster to read and 18-25% faster to write, with faster rehash. Metamethod lookup is greatly cheaper for table.freeze'd metatables. table.find without __eq becomes 2-3x faster (pending). Build impact: Freezing metatables with table.freeze benefits metamethod-heavy code. Source: Creator Docs release notes 739, 743.
- **CollectionService collections API** (OFFICIAL, Live (GetCount change pending), Release 742 ~2026-10-08): CollectionService:CreateCollection() is a new entry point, and CollectionService:GetCollections(Instance) returns all collections an Instance belongs to. Pending: collections provide GetCount() instead of GetSize() and no longer expose Id, behind FFlagCollectionTopLevelAccessors (off by default). Build impact: Prefer GetCount() over GetSize() and avoid relying on Id. **DEPRECATED:** GetSize, Id (Collection) Source: Creator Docs release notes 742.
- **WorldRoot scriptable physics stepping** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 737 ~2026-09-03): Adds WorldRoot:Simulate() for scriptable physics stepping, WorldRoot.SimulationRate for scaling simulation rate, and WorldRoot.AutoSimulate to control continuous physics stepping. Build impact: Allows manually driving physics for a world instead of continuous stepping. Source: Creator Docs release notes 737.
- **Instance:QueryDescendants selector query** (OFFICIAL, Live, Release 737 ~2026-09-03): QueryDescendants looks up descendants via a selector string. Later added :not(...), :has(...), [$AttributeExists], enum property string matching and comparison operators. Release 699 made it faster; [ClassName=name] is 2x faster. Build impact: Use it as a faster, more expressive alternative to GetDescendants() loops. Source: Creator Docs release notes 697, 699, 700, 712, 737.
- **Improved AnimationConstraint rollout** (OFFICIAL, Pending, 2026-09-15): Opt in with Workspace.ImprovedAnimationConstraint = Enabled; AngularStrength, AngularDamping, LinearStrength and LinearDamping replace the PD controller when IsKinematic = false. Phase 2 defaults to Enabled; Phase 3 applies to all games with no opt-out. Build impact: Test ragdoll/animation-driven physics now; the opt-out disappears in Phase 3. **DEPRECATED:** Workspace.ImprovedAnimationConstraint (removed in Phase 3); legacy PD-controller response for IsKinematic = false Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4700211).
- **Collision group APIs moved to WorldRoot** (OFFICIAL, Live, Release 732 ~2026-07-29): Collision group APIs move from PhysicsService to WorldRoot so each WorldModel can register its own groups. Enable WorldModel.UseWorkspaceCollisionGroups to use Workspace collision groups instead. Build impact: Update code that calls PhysicsService collision group APIs. **DEPRECATED:** PhysicsService collision group APIs (moved to WorldRoot) Source: Creator Docs release notes 732.
- **Ordered data store BatchGetAsync** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 730 ~2026-07-14): Adds BatchGetAsync to ordered data stores, retrieving up to 100 string keys in one request as a key-to-value dictionary. Gated by DataStoreEnableOrderedBatchGetAsync. Build impact: Can replace per-key reads with a single batched request once enabled. Source: Creator Docs release notes 730.
- **Scene Analysis tool** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-07-10): Real-time treemap views for script memory, unparented instances, audio and animation memory, instance composition and triangles, plus programmatic access via SceneAnalysisService. Out of beta on 2026-07-10. Build impact: Use it to find memory and instance usage hotspots. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4620678).
- **LibMP MicroProfiler Luau API** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-06-25): Exposes client, server and Studio performance data for custom widgets, analytics and snapshots. Update Oct 2026: enabled in all live games; SetMemTargetMB() sets memory budget. Build impact: Enables custom performance tooling in scripts. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4703136).
- **Real-time performance alerts** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-06-25): Alerts use static thresholds or period-over-period detection, up to 20 per game, including data store metrics. Available to experiences with 100+ DAU and delivered via new game-level webhooks. Build impact: Set up alerts for performance and data store regressions. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4703283).
- **Studio Script Sync** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-06-17): Edit Script, LocalScript, ModuleScript and Folder instances as files in external editors, syncing both ways via a single Sync to action. Deletions affect only Luau scripts and supported files; a conflict dialog lists changes. Build impact: Supports external editor workflows safely. **DEPRECATED:** Multiple right-click sync menu options; generic overwrite prompt; deleting non-Luau files during sync Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4688454).
- **ImprovedPhysicsReplication opt-in and Disabled option** (OFFICIAL, Deprecation, 2026-06-16): Workspace.ImprovedPhysicsReplication gives eventual consistency so updates are not lost to packet drops. A Disabled option is a short-term opt-out; its removal is delayed until bugs are fixed, then a two-week validation window. Build impact: Test now; physics network stats are replaced by Instance State Replication stats. **DEPRECATED:** Workspace.ImprovedPhysicsReplication 'Disabled' option (removal delayed); physics network stats Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4675512).
- **Input Action System fully released** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-06-11): Hardware bindings and action types are available to all games. Default player scripts migrate to IAS, enabled via Workspace.PlayerScriptsUseInputActionSystem. Release 735 adds require("@rbx/PlayerModule") when opted in. Build impact: Audit custom input logic. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4678416).
- **Structured logging in LogService** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-06-04): LogService gains Output(), Info(), Warn(), Error() and Log() accepting a message template and context table, shown in F9 console and Output. Errors group by template in Creator Analytics. LogService:Error() inside pcall no longer prints. Build impact: print, warn and error remain available. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4668415).
- **Extended Services for Compute** (OFFICIAL, Live, 2026-06-02): Opt-in feature that more than doubles per-player server CPU resources. Limits are unchanged for non-opted-in games. Server Performance Observability adds a CPU core utilization graph and insights. Build impact: Consider opting in for CPU-bound servers. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4665165).
- **Advanced Network Simulation** (OFFICIAL, Live, 2026-06-02): Studio Settings > Network simulates per-packet latency, jitter and packet loss in Test, Test Here, Team Test and Server & Clients, scriptable via NetworkSettings. Client-side only. Recommended over legacy Incoming Replication Lag; enabling both stacks effects. Build impact: Use Network Simulation, not both settings together. **DEPRECATED:** IncomingReplicationLag (recommended to switch to Network Simulation) Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4665487).
- **GroupService:GetRolesInGroupAsync** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 723 ~2026-05-27): Adds GroupService:GetRolesInGroupAsync(userId, groupId), returning all roles a user holds in a group, supporting multi-role assignment. Build impact: Migrate away from single-role group checks. **DEPRECATED:** Player:GetRankInGroupAsync(), Player:GetRoleInGroupAsync() Source: Creator Docs release notes 723.
- **Luau export keyword for variables and functions** (OFFICIAL, Live, Release 723 ~2026-05-27): The export keyword now supports variables and functions, building an export table returned from the module to expose a stable module API. Build impact: Lets modules declare a stable public API. Source: Creator Docs release notes 723.
- **Server Authority and Next Generation Replication in Client Beta** (OFFICIAL, Beta, 2026-04-30): Server Authority can be published for live testing; APIs are locked, mispredictions reduced, rollback faster. Next Generation Replication is opt-in. Adds Animator:GetTrackByAnimationId and Adaptive Animation compatibility. Build impact: Test Server Authority games live; use BindToSimulation for predictive Instance.new. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4606949).
- **Fixed-rate simulation: BindToSimulation and UseFixedSimulation** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-04-29): RunService:BindToSimulation(callback, frequency) runs logic at fixed Enum.StepFrequency rates (Hz1 to Hz60), default 30 Hz. UseFixedSimulation syncs animation, Humanoid, ControllerManagers and sensors to a fixed rate; opt-in, opt-out stage undated. Build impact: Only properties with the Simulation Access attribute can be modified in callbacks. Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4605516).
- **ClientAnimatorThrottling always on** (OFFICIAL, Full release, 2026-04-29): Phase 3 is an always-on engine feature, no longer toggle-able in Workspace, with 25-30% animation CPU savings in crowded scenes. Animator.PreferLodEnabled = false hints against throttling; local characters are now throttled too. Build impact: Don't rely on toggling it or on local-character exemption. **DEPRECATED:** Toggling ClientAnimatorThrottling in Workspace; local-character throttling exemption Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4568218).
- **ExcludeInstances and IncludeInstances for spatial queries** (OFFICIAL, Pending, 2026-04-07): RaycastParams and OverlapParams gain ExcludeInstances and IncludeInstances, allowing simultaneous lists with log(n) lookups. Up to 10x faster than FilterDescendantsInstances with 10,000 excluded instances. Build impact: Prefer them for large filter lists. **DEPRECATED:** FilterDescendantsInstances and FilterType (to be deprecated eventually, still supported) Source: [source](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/4563575).
- **Yielding methods renamed with Async suffix** (OFFICIAL, Pending, Release 700): Deprecates nearly all yielding methods that did not end with Async and provides equivalent methods with Async in the name. Build impact: Plan to migrate yielding calls to the Async equivalents. **DEPRECATED:** Yielding methods not ending in Async (nearly all of them) Source: Creator Docs release notes 700.

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2015-01-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-cframes/11743): Use CFrame:components() to read raw values when doing custom math; it is faster than repeated CFrame operations in hot code.
- 2015-01-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-cframes/11743): Remember the Right, Top, Back vectors are directions (unit length), not face-center positions.
- 2015-04-24 TheNexusAvenger [18:12](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=1092): Convert RGB 0-255 values to 0-1 when using Color3 in ColorSequence scripts.
- 2015-04-24 TheNexusAvenger [18:55](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=1135): Size values in NumberSequence are capped at 10 on the actual instance; keypoints need time 0 to 1.
- 2015-04-24 TheNexusAvenger [22:23](https://youtu.be/4hZo6m1N2-w?t=1343): Parent scripted emitters to a named folder in workspace so the script can find the part reliably.
- 2016-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017): Use Terrain:FillBlock(part.CFrame, part.Size, material) in the command bar to fill a part's volume with smooth terrain.
- 2016-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017): Use Terrain:FillBall(position, radius, material) to create spherical terrain; the docs example uses part.Size.Y/2 as radius.
- 2016-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017): Use Terrain:FillRegion(region:ExpandToGrid(4), 4, material) to fill a Region3; ExpandToGrid aligns it to the 4-stud voxel grid.
- 2016-07-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756): Profile before and after optimizing; the author warns some changes may have no effect, since the newer Luau VM already handles some optimizations.
- 2016-07-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756): Precompute constant values like CFrame.new(0,1,0) outside loops instead of recreating them every iteration.
- 2016-07-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756): Cache indexed methods and constructors (e.g. Workspace.FindPartOnRay, Vector3.new) in locals to reduce repeated __index calls inside loops.
- 2016-09-05 TheNexusAvenger [4:12](https://youtu.be/T5T9RMbjHBU?t=252): Sync visuals to audio by computing frame index = playback time × recording FPS (30 default), not by stepping through the table in a loop; the loop drifts when frames are dropped.
- 2016-09-05 TheNexusAvenger [4:12](https://youtu.be/T5T9RMbjHBU?t=252): Update bar sizes every render step (about 1/60 s) from the computed frame, rather than on a fixed timer.
- 2016-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442): Place main code in ModuleScripts, require them, then delete the modules; code keeps running while the requiring script exists. Obfuscation adds further protection but is not absolute.
- 2017-10-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-scripting-bots-javascript-tutorial-funcaptcha-and-new-host-info/58201): Use a unique password for the bot's Roblox account, separate from your main account.
- 2018-02-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082): Call :Destroy() on Instances you no longer need; Destroy recursively breaks their event connections so they can be garbage collected.
- 2018-02-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082): Store connection objects and call :Disconnect() on them when done; a connection that is never disconnected keeps the closure and its upvalues alive.
- 2018-02-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082): Prioritise leak checks on the server, since long-running server processes accumulate leaks while short client sessions rarely hit a problematic level.
- 2018-07-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-quaternions/143295): When blending rotations, pass unnormalized component weights to CFrame.new(0,0,0,X,Y,Z,W); it normalizes them onto the 4D unit sphere for you.
- 2018-07-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-quaternions/143295): For custom slerp, choose the twin with positive dot product between start and end quaternions so the path is the shortest rotation.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [18:31](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1111): For 1v1 duels, use fixed animation timing (e.g. 700ms heavy attack) and a non-physics range/time check so timing is consistent for players.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [19:12](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1152): Favor the active attacker with client-driven logic for responsiveness, accepting some desync; keep slow attacks so desync is a small share of duration.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [27:26](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1646): For competitive games, move hit logic to the server to resist exploiters, accepting some loss of client responsiveness and latency-related misfires.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [36:05](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=2165): For fast projectiles, set network ownership to the player with SetNetworkOwnership and enlarge projectile colliders to counter discrete-physics tunneling.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [22:09](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1329): Disable auto-localize on GUIs you handle in scripts, and localize on the client; send event info over RemoteEvents, not localized text.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [4:06](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=246): Frame budget is 15-30 ms per frame (60-30 FPS). Watch worst-case frames, not just average; isolated spikes feel far worse than steady slightly slower frames.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [8:46](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=526): Join time is mostly a function of instance and voxel count; use streaming. Avoid blocking server calls in character loading, e.g. third-party analytics, which caused 30 s waits when the service went down.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [19:23](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1163): Prefer event-based code over running every frame. Slice long-running work into small per-frame pieces to avoid single-frame spikes.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [20:46](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1246): Heartbeat, Stepped and RenderStepped all run every frame; RenderStepped runs before rendering and inflates render time, so prefer Heartbeat or Stepped.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [21:46](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1306): Cache property reads and object creation in Lua; use Roblox built-in APIs (animation, UI layout) instead of rewriting them, and request missing features instead.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [22:28](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=1348): Collection Service tags are efficient even at about a thousand tags. Use multiple remote events for large data volumes; one event suits low traffic.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [14:20](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=860): Instancing does not support outlines or Humanoids at the time; disable outlines when not needed and keep Humanoids for characters only.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [11:33](https://youtu.be/rBrILsiVdtQ?t=693): Simplify mesh triangle counts and combine meshes where possible; splitting one high-poly mesh into many smaller ones does not speed rendering.
- 2018-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434): Compute aim-down-sights offset as Handle.CFrame:inverse() * Aim.CFrame and set joint C1 by lerping, so the camera centres on the sight rather than the handle.
- 2018-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434): Update arm shoulder C1 values each frame in RenderStepped so arms stay attached to the weapon while aiming down sights.
- 2018-08-07 AlvinBlox [2:31](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=151): Name the folder exactly 'leaderstats' (lowercase l, correct casing) or the leaderboard will not appear in the top-right corner.
- 2018-08-07 AlvinBlox [5:12](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=312): Use the player's UserId in the DataStore key, not their name, and never rename the DataStore after publishing or player data gets wiped.
- 2018-08-07 AlvinBlox [9:17](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=557): Wrap DataStore GetAsync in pcall so a DataStore error is handled instead of breaking the whole script.
- 2018-08-07 AlvinBlox [12:58](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=778): Save player data in PlayerRemoving and kick all players in BindToClose with a short wait, so the last player's data is saved before the server shuts down.
- 2018-08-07 AlvinBlox [20:37](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=1237): Enable Studio Access to API Services in game config to test DataStores in Play Solo; note Studio writes to the live DataStore used by real servers.
- 2018-08-07 AlvinBlox [31:31](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=1891): In ProcessReceipt, store each PurchaseId in a DataStore and check it before granting, so a purchase is never granted twice; return PurchaseGranted only after rewards are saved.
- 2018-08-07 AlvinBlox [35:45](https://youtu.be/h92s4Xn26oc?t=2145): If the buyer has left before ProcessReceipt fires, return NotProcessedYet so the reward is granted when they rejoin.
- 2018-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677): Use x(t) = 0.5*g*t^2 + v0*t + x0 to compute launch velocity v0 = (target - x0 - 0.5*g*t^2)/t for a chosen flight time t.
- 2018-11-06 ContextLost [0:40](https://youtu.be/fNf3JtvrzRg?t=40): Check the read-only Active property on joints (returned by GetJoints) to see which redundant welds Roblox actually uses; delete inactive ones that contribute nothing.
- 2018-11-06 ContextLost [2:44](https://youtu.be/fNf3JtvrzRg?t=164): Welds forming a cycle are arbitrarily ignored by the spanning tree; if you need predictable behaviour, break cycles yourself rather than relying on the engine's choice.
- 2018-11-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509): Use RunService:IsServer() inside a shared class so one module handles both server and client logic without duplicated code.
- 2018-12-08 AlvinBlox [10:54](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=654): Luau is strict about spelling and capitalisation; double-check code against the output window when an error appears.
- 2018-12-08 AlvinBlox [11:30](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=690): Name the leaderstats folder exactly 'leaderstats' in lowercase, or the leaderboard will not appear.
- 2018-12-08 AlvinBlox [13:20](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=800): Change IntValue Value to update leaderboard numbers; renaming the value object does not change the displayed stat name.
- 2018-12-08 AlvinBlox [18:02](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=1082): Use WaitForChild for services' folders and values, since server scripts can run before objects have replicated.
- 2018-12-08 AlvinBlox [19:25](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=1165): Add a wait inside while loops so the game loop does not run every frame and waste server performance.
- 2018-12-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733): Run the position check as a server Script in the character's StarterCharacterScripts loop with wait(checktime), storing the last CFrame each tick.
- 2019-03-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754): Refresh leaderboard fetches on a long interval (tutorial uses 120 seconds) to limit DataStore and thumbnail request load.
- 2019-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253): Tween the root's CFrame, not its Position; welds only propagate CFrame changes to other parts.
- 2019-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253): Use TweenService directly for model tweens; avoid CFrameValue proxies with SetPrimaryPartCFrame, which add overhead and fail without a PrimaryPart.
- 2019-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253): Avoid SetPrimaryPartCFrame for repeated moves: floating-point offset errors accumulate and tear models apart over time (deprecated; use Pivot API).
- 2019-08-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-couple-of-advanced-cframe-tricks/337682): Use the quaternion trick for rotation between vectors: add [1+dot, cross] and let the CFrame constructor normalize; cheaper and more robust than acos/fromAxisAngle.
- 2019-08-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-couple-of-advanced-cframe-tricks/337682): Guard the opposite-vector case (dot < -0.99999) separately with a backup axis; the quaternion sum is zero there and cannot be normalized.
- 2019-08-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-couple-of-advanced-cframe-tricks/337682): Measure spin around an axis with swing-twist decomposition on the delta CFrame rather than the part's delta CFrame; this avoids errors from wobbling parts.
- 2019-08-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-a-maid-class-on-roblox-to-manage-state/340061): Disconnect signals and destroy objects when state changes or objects are removed; a Maid centralises this cleanup so nothing is forgotten.
- 2019-09-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-requests-simple-powerful-http-in-roblox/355294): Use configurable rate limiting to balance your game's HTTP usage so requests stay within Roblox and third-party API limits.
- 2019-09-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-requests-simple-powerful-http-in-roblox/355294): Enable local or global response caching to avoid repeating identical HTTP requests.
- 2019-09-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484): Set RequireHandles to OFF on the Tool, otherwise the custom grip setup will not work in game.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Disconnect event connections and destroy instances when done; a connected function keeps every upvalue it references alive indefinitely.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Set local variables to nil when finished with them; a referenced table is never collected while any strong reference remains.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Wrap temporary values in do blocks so they go out of scope and become collectable without waiting for the thread to end.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Use setmetatable with __mode set to "k", "v", or "kv" for caches so entries can be garbage collected automatically.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Clear or weak-reference tables you still hold but no longer use; a table keeps its values alive until the table itself is collected.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Clean up callbacks like Bindable/RemoteFunction OnInvoke handlers, since they hold references to captured variables just like connections.
- 2019-10-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954): Clean up never-ending threads (e.g. coroutine.yield loops) by scoping temporaries in do blocks or clearing them, because such threads never collect their root values.
- 2019-11-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801): Before blaming Humanoid, analyze your own NPC code and ask Scripting Support to review it; the author says it usually can be improved for significant gains.
- 2019-11-22 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792): For constantly changing UI like inventories or player lists, wrap the CanvasSize update in a function and call it whenever content changes.
- 2019-11-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687): Pass the method as pcall(DataStore.GetAsync, DataStore, "key") rather than wrapping in an anonymous function; avoids creating a closure each call.
- 2019-11-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687): Pcall StarterGui:SetCore in a retry loop with wait(1), since SetCores aren't registered until CoreScripts initialise.
- 2019-11-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687): Do not use ypcall; pcall can yield in modern Luau, so ypcall gives no benefit.
- 2019-11-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687): Do not yield (wait) inside xpcall; it cannot yield. Use xpcall with debug.traceback as handler to get stack traces on errors.
- 2019-11-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618): Learn Luau fundamentals (print, variables, functions, loops, tables) before tackling advanced game systems; the guide says it provides the basics needed to follow advanced tutorials.
- 2019-11-27 TheDevKing [8:00](https://youtu.be/3hMmhmC18RA?t=480): Wrap MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync in pcall so an API error does not break the script; warn on failure.
- 2019-11-27 TheDevKing [26:55](https://youtu.be/3hMmhmC18RA?t=1615): In server scripts, don't use player.Character right after PlayerAdded; wait for the character to load (e.g. CharacterAdded or waiting for workspace child) before parenting tools.
- 2019-12-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dragging-objects-with-the-mouse/403766): Set mouse.TargetFilter to the dragged target so mouse.Hit ignores it, and only move the part while Button1 is held.
- 2019-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672): Run the terrain conversion script in the Studio command bar, not in Test Mode, or the terrain may not save.
- 2019-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996): Put the bar math in a function (CurvedBarResize) and reuse it per segment instead of duplicating code for each corner.
- 2020-01-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374): Replicate instances to only the players who need them (e.g. a player's interior) instead of ReplicatedStorage, to save bandwidth, CPU and memory, especially on mobile.
- 2020-01-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374): Store instances as table keys to prevent garbage collection while they are held in a pending state, rather than parenting them early.
- 2020-02-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/consume-everything-how-greedy-meshing-works/452717): Extend greedy meshing to 2D by expanding the 1D run across the other axis while every block in that direction can be included, then emit one cuboid per region.
- 2020-02-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/consume-everything-how-greedy-meshing-works/452717): Mark blocks as visited so each block is included in exactly one cuboid; repeat for every remaining block. Extending the same idea to 3D follows the 2D pattern.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584): Clamp health with math.clamp between 0 and MaxHealth so values cannot go negative or exceed max after heal/attack.
- 2020-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584): Disconnect event connections and destroy the battle area when a battle ends to avoid leaked connections.
- 2020-02-20 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/about-our-upcoming-global-compliance-system/461447): Use GetPolicyInfoForPlayerAsync to check a player's region policy info so game features can comply with local rules.
- 2020-03-01 Last Level Studios [23:08](https://youtu.be/4fz4fpJLUSQ?t=1388): Use a command framework like Commander so argument parsing (e.g. converting names to Player objects) is handled before your command code runs.
- 2020-03-20 AlvinBlox [15:19](https://youtu.be/jOFrwMkl8LI?t=919): Use game:GetService for every service call as a consistent habit so you never have to remember which services need it.
- 2020-03-20 AlvinBlox [18:15](https://youtu.be/jOFrwMkl8LI?t=1095): Do not rename built-in services; renaming breaks GetService lookups and confuses anyone reviewing your code.
- 2020-03-23 Last Level Studios [19:14](https://youtu.be/n0ODJH9EgWc?t=1154): Build utilities that solve a wide-audience problem (e.g. rotated region checks) rather than one-off effects; they get reused across many projects.
- 2020-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318): Multiply procedural movement by deltaTime (e.g. *deltaTime*60) so FPS-unlocked clients (100-300 FPS) don't run animations faster.
- 2020-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318): Use TweenService for aim in/out transitions and lerp between idle and aim CFrame offsets via a NumberValue.
- 2020-04-02 GnomeCode [3:38](https://youtu.be/KZVpkWwz3A4?t=218): Loop GetChildren() on a character and check IsA('BasePart') or MeshPart before changing properties; many children are not parts.
- 2020-04-05 AlvinBlox [23:48](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=1428): Check that a touched part belongs to a player (game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(hit.Parent)) before running collection logic.
- 2020-04-05 AlvinBlox [26:34](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=1594): Destroy the touched egg after collection and clear the viewport frame's children before adding the next egg preview.
- 2020-04-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683): Errors inside a coroutine do not crash the main thread; check coroutine.resume's returned success value to handle failures.
- 2020-05-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329): Roblox math.sin/cos/tan take radians; convert degrees with math.rad(). Inverse functions return radians; convert with math.deg().
- 2020-05-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329): Use Vector3:Dot(other) divided by magnitudes to get the cosine of the angle between two vectors (|a||b|cos(Θ)).
- 2020-05-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329): Law of Cosines only applies when the known angle sits between the two known sides; for SAS, compute the missing side first, then Law of Sines.
- 2020-05-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329): In SSA/Law of Sines cases, solve for the angle opposite the smaller known side first to avoid picking the wrong angle.
- 2020-05-02 GnomeCode [4:42](https://youtu.be/uF-BGGv6otQ?t=282): Wait on Humanoid.MoveToFinished before issuing the next move; wrap loops in a wait so the while loop does not spin unbounded.
- 2020-05-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587): Route external HTTP calls through a queue instead of raw HttpService so rate-limited (429) requests are retried automatically rather than lost.
- 2020-05-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587): Set maxSimultaneousSendOperations no higher than the service's rate limit, per the author's guidance.
- 2020-05-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587): Use retryAfter cooldown matching the service's rate-limit window (example: Trello 10 requests/10 seconds, cooldown 10).
- 2020-05-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587): Use HttpRequestPriority.Prioritary or First sparingly; default first-come-first-serve covers most cases.
- 2020-05-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587): Append a trailing slash to URLs when using query options; library normalizes this from v1.0.0 onward.
- 2020-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782): Tween a CFrameValue and apply its Changed value to the joint's C0; TweenService can only tween values like CFrameValue, not CFrame properties directly.
- 2020-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782): Save a base C0 (savedBaseCFrame) at start and multiply offsets by it instead of using the current CFrame to avoid odd drift.
- 2020-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782): Reuse one TweenBase by cancelling it, editing goal.Value, and recreating the tween per keyframe rather than creating many tweens.
- 2020-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782): Use C0 of a Motor6D so procedural offsets can run alongside regular animation tracks, enabling augmentation such as character tilt.
- 2020-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782): CFrame.Angles takes radians; wrap degree values in math.rad().
- 2020-05-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/perspective-object-dragging-like-superliminal/566200): Anchor picked-up parts and zero Velocity/RotVelocity before dragging; an anchored part with velocity still acts as a conveyor.
- 2020-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791): Keep projectiles client-side for responsiveness and replicate them, rather than server-only bullets which freeze and cost more to create.
- 2020-06-01 AlvinBlox [8:18](https://youtu.be/spFZ44yqIII?t=498): Countdown to an event: subtract os.time() from the start timestamp to get seconds remaining.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Avoid plain os.time() for cross-server sync; author reported servers drifting 3-5 minutes apart. Fetch time externally via the Google HTTP Date header.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Seed Random.new() with the day number so every server generates the same shop items without communicating.
- 2020-06-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401): Floor the day value so the seed only changes when a full day boundary passes.
- 2020-06-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943): Use TweenInfo reverse and repeat flags instead of separate return tweens; this cut the bobbing script from ~156 lines to ~87.
- 2020-07-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collectionservice-in-a-nutshell/668984): Use CollectionService tags to drive one script over many instances instead of duplicating scripts; fixing one script fixes all tagged parts (e.g. 300 killing bricks).
- 2020-07-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collectionservice-in-a-nutshell/668984): Use GetInstanceAddedSignal and GetInstanceRemovedSignal to react to instances gaining or losing a tag at runtime.
- 2020-07-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753): Leave TargetFPS at 60, as the author recommends.
- 2020-08-02 Emancyphur [0:58](https://youtu.be/ScdTM3ju-yk?t=58): Set Lighting Technology to Future in Properties; players need 5+ graphics bars (or Studio quality level 10+) to see it, others fall back to ShadowMap/Voxel.
- 2020-08-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/table-to-string-converter/712610): Escape control characters (\n, \r, \0) with string.gsub and a lookup table; avoid %q when you also need custom escaping.
- 2020-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-raycasting-gun/723716): Use the new Workspace:Raycast API instead of outdated tutorial methods; old guides broke after filtering enabled was enforced.
- 2020-08-17 Last Level Studios [21:38](https://youtu.be/0RRGneqSZRY?t=1298): When initially processing hundreds of thousands of parts, yield periodically using a time budget rather than after every part, or the load takes unacceptably long.
- 2020-08-19 Emancyphur [1:04:19](https://youtu.be/LjETISdUBp8?t=3859): Route the frame's parent name to the server to decide which slot (primary/secondary) an item fills, rather than sending the item alone.
- 2020-08-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastore-tutorial-for-beginners/733973): Put datastore code in a server Script, not a LocalScript.
- 2020-08-24 Last Level Studios [4:29](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=269): Treat track as blocks with entry and exit sensors; track trains in an in-block table, adding on enter and removing on exit or despawn, instead of GetTouchingParts.
- 2020-08-24 Last Level Studios [30:33](https://youtu.be/uYIE3IVmKY8?t=1833): Avoid per-object background loops; use one centralised table/manager for hundreds of entities and yield to keep the server near 60 fps.
- 2020-08-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207): Give static accessories with Humanoid:AddAccessory(accessory:Clone()) inside CharacterAdded so they reapply on every respawn.
