- 2020-09-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/concepts-object-oriented-programming-in-roblox/761267): Put shared methods on a prototype table with Prototype.__index = Prototype and setmetatable on instances to avoid duplicating functions per object (DRY, less memory).
- 2020-09-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/concepts-object-oriented-programming-in-roblox/761267): Set __index to a table, not a function, to keep Luau's fast method calls; avoid __index functions and deep __index chains.
- 2020-09-27 B Ricey [1:46](https://youtu.be/4fJBWFz9I-E?t=106): Use workspace:Raycast(origin, direction, params) instead of the deprecated FindPartOnRay family; pass origin and direction directly rather than building a Ray object.
- 2020-09-27 B Ricey [4:02](https://youtu.be/4fJBWFz9I-E?t=242): Always check that the RaycastResult is non-nil before reading it, since a raycast into empty space returns nil.
- 2020-09-27 B Ricey [8:37](https://youtu.be/4fJBWFz9I-E?t=517): Use RaycastParams.FilterType Blacklist or Whitelist with FilterDescendantsInstances to skip or restrict hit objects.
- 2020-09-27 B Ricey [9:32](https://youtu.be/4fJBWFz9I-E?t=572): Use RaycastParams.CollisionGroup to control which parts rays hit; note rays can hit parts in groups that are set to collide with each other.
- 2020-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-scripting-roadmap-learning-resource-list/808430): Learn Luau basics (variables, functions, loops, tables, conditionals, debugging tools) before taking on any commissions or paid scripting work.
- 2020-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-scripting-roadmap-learning-resource-list/808430): Start with YouTube tutorials for beginner Luau, then move to the official Roblox DevHub docs as skills grow.
- 2020-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-scripting-roadmap-learning-resource-list/808430): Follow the official Roblox scripting style guides to keep code consistent and readable.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Keep data sent between server and client minimal; large tables in remote arguments cause latency-like lag.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Run visual effects (tweens, particles) on the client and replicate only when needed, rather than on the server.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Use built-in events (.Changed, GetPropertyChangedSignal) instead of while loops that poll, e.g. for updating a TextLabel.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Parent instances last, after setting all properties, so changes replicate once instead of many times.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Destroy unused instances and disconnect connections to prevent memory leaks; consider Quenty's Maid class.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499): Use PartCaching/PartPooling to reuse parts instead of constantly creating new ones.
- 2020-10-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013): Access Lighting via game:GetService("Lighting") rather than game.Lighting, the recommended form.
- 2020-10-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182): Consider AeroGameFramework for new projects to avoid manually handling RemoteEvents/RemoteFunctions; it bundles modules and ProfileService.
- 2020-11-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/melee-hitboxes-what-is-the-best-solution-and-is-the-touched-event-really-that-bad/867477): GetTouchingParts only detects CanCollide parts; Region3 is costly and unrotated; raycasting is lightweight and gives hit position, but needs a module for ease of use.
- 2020-11-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/generating-equidistant-points-on-a-sphere/874144): Use a golden-angle spiral (azimuth increment = 2π × golden ratio, incline = acos(1 - 2·i/n)) to get evenly distributed points on a sphere instead of random sampling, which avoids clumps and line artifacts.
- 2020-11-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/generating-equidistant-points-on-a-sphere/874144): Precompute sphere points once and store them in a table (self._points) so they can be reused without recalculating each frame.
- 2020-11-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/generating-equidistant-points-on-a-sphere/874144): Use Vector3 tables for sphere directions when doing raycasts for vision cones; limiting the number of rays simulates a limited field of view and keeps cost low.
- 2020-12-11 SimTek Game Development [6:26](https://youtu.be/8QUJ_Qc6JTs?t=386): Check for a Humanoid and then GetPlayerFromCharacter before handling a touch, so NPCs/zombies with humanoids are ignored.
- 2020-12-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-easing-styles/937281): Elastic can make objects pass through targets if too fast; slow the tween duration (author raised it from 1s to 2-3s) to avoid overshooting through colliders.
- 2020-12-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-ownership-for-beginners-how-to-handle-physics-on-the-client/947029): Hand physics of many short-lived, frequently spawned parts (e.g. cannonballs) to the firing player so the server doesn't simulate every one, avoiding lag under spam.
- 2021-01-04 B Ricey [6:51](https://youtu.be/xwaCg7SWUhc?t=411): Use a Maid utility to collect event connections and instances, then call destroy once to clean them all up instead of manual connection tables.
- 2021-01-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pid-control-pt-1-proportional-control/968881): Write the controller as a generic function taking kP, set point and process value; it only needs subtraction and multiplication, so it works for numbers, Vector2, Vector3.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [4:04](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=244): Access attributes with instance:GetAttribute(name) and SetAttribute(name, value), not instance.name; attributes are not properties.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [6:10](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=370): Store attribute name strings in constants at the top of scripts; a typo silently creates a new attribute instead of erroring.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [7:32](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=452): Setting an attribute to nil deletes it.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [7:32](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=452): Use GetAttributeChangedSignal(name) to react to attribute changes, replacing clutter like extra NumberValues.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [13:45](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=825): Keep JSON-encoded table data to numbers, strings, booleans and nested tables only; functions cannot be encoded.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [16:28](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=988): Prefer a few JSON-encoded attributes over hundreds of individual attributes for bulk data.
- 2021-01-21 GameMakers [32:05](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=1925): Learn Lua; Luau is backward compatible with Lua and adds strict typing, so Lua knowledge carries over.
- 2021-01-23 TechSpectrum [6:31](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=391): Use the Microprofiler (Ctrl+F6 style toggle in Developer Stats) and pause at the biggest spike; dump 32-64 frames to inspect later.
- 2021-01-23 TechSpectrum [9:28](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=568): A large RayCast bar in the Microprofiler suggests too many raycasts; reduce how often or how many you cast.
- 2021-01-23 TechSpectrum [15:26](https://youtu.be/-PPg4N5sKXE?t=926): Check the Network tab for frequent service calls (FriendService, MarketplaceService, GamePassService) and reduce them on the server.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [0:31](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=31): Learn by picking apart free models and open-source code to see why developers made their scripting decisions, not just copying them.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [0:31](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=31): When stuck, google the exact error message or API name instead of blindly following tutorials, so you learn how the code works.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [2:24](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=144): Read every line of a reference script and look up each unfamiliar call in the API reference until you understand what it does.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [3:45](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=225): Before using a new API or mechanic, find a practical example of someone else using it, then check the API docs with that context.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [1:14](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=74): Learn what code elements do rather than only how to make one thing, so you can plan new scripts yourself.
- 2021-01-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074): Use the Microprofiler at peak spikes, zoom in, and identify which tasks consume time in each frame.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [1:37](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=97): Name the tool's main part exactly 'Handle' (capital H); tools will not recognize any other name.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [3:12](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=192): Use raycasting (FastCast) instead of velocity-based projectiles; velocity is laggier and less accurate with .Touched.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [9:12](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=552): Set the cast origin to the attachment's world position (not .Position), and use a unit vector for direction.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [12:09](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=729): Filter the raycast with RaycastParams ignoring the shooter's character and the bullets folder; set IgnoreWater true for guns.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [12:53](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=773): Rebuild the filter list on Equipped so the tool's parent character is ignored; set AutoIgnoreContainer to false to avoid redundancy.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [14:29](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=869): Create the bullets folder in code if missing so the gun stays standalone and portable across projects.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [19:48](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1188): Offset the bullet by its segment length so it stops behind walls instead of phasing through them.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [21:57](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1317): Use a starting velocity around 1000 for realistic gun speed with visible travel time and drop.
- 2021-01-24 B Ricey [24:01](https://youtu.be/mJZWY3G7wa4?t=1441): Clean up bullets with Debris:AddItem so spent bullets do not persist in the world.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [4:00](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=240): Store team loadout tools in ServerStorage folders named exactly as each Team, and validate folder names against Teams before granting items.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [43:28](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=2608): Use a short delay before destroying a tool that a player should not have (the creator used one second) to avoid errors when unequipping and removing at once.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [16:03](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=963): Use dictionaries mapping tool name to team name for fast lookup of whether a tool belongs to a team.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [5:07](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=307): Hook Players.PlayerAdded plus a loop over existing players on server start, so the script works both in Studio tests and live.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [28:25](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=1705): Listen to Teams.ChildAdded and each team's PlayerAdded so teams added at runtime still apply the correct loadouts.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [5:49](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=349): Check that a player is a Player and that Character and Backpack exist before accessing them, to avoid runtime errors.
- 2021-02-12 Emancyphur [1:37](https://youtu.be/0B1Q2IZSlU8?t=97): Follow Roblox's Lua Style Guide from the start so code stays readable and editable and bad habits don't slow development later.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259): Follow the Roblox Lua Style Guide on GitHub from the start to avoid bad habits that compound and make code harder to modify and debug.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259): Use Lua 5.1 Reference Manual and Programming in Lua to strengthen base-language fundamentals; Luau builds on Lua 5.1.
- 2021-02-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078): Don't loop over and destroy many instances individually. Destroy the whole container folder and create a new empty one.
- 2021-02-15 SimTek Game Development [8:21](https://youtu.be/_ANWj69QDms?t=501): Ensure the stat name in the zombie script (e.g. 'Kills') exactly matches the leaderstats value name; mismatches silently fail.
- 2021-02-15 SimTek Game Development [7:01](https://youtu.be/_ANWj69QDms?t=421): For player characters, connect the same creator/death logic in CharacterAdded so it applies to all players, not just NPCs.
- 2021-02-15 SimTek Game Development [0:44](https://youtu.be/_ANWj69QDms?t=44): Use Ctrl+Shift+F to search the weapon system for 'take damage' and find where damage is applied before adding tags.
- 2021-02-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/efficient-object-oriented-programming-tutorial/1061153): Put methods on the class table and use __index metatables, not per-object closures; each object otherwise allocates duplicate functions, wasting memory.
- 2021-02-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/efficient-object-oriented-programming-tutorial/1061153): Declare default property values in the class table (Rcade.class{name='NO NAME'}) rather than using 'or' in __init, so setting a field to nil still falls back to the default.
- 2021-03-22 GnomeCode [2:36](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=156): Use events like ball.Touched:Connect(fn) to react to in-game changes in real time instead of polling or hardcoding when things happen.
- 2021-03-22 GnomeCode [5:02](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=302): Touched passes the touching part as a parameter; name it in the function to inspect which part hit the object.
- 2021-03-22 GnomeCode [10:54](https://youtu.be/94MtCzTo85k?t=654): Save an object's original Position in a variable before moving it so it can be reset later, e.g. after a goal.
- 2021-03-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/fps-using-viewmodels-the-improved-version-parts-2-out-of-3/1129877): Drive ViewModel animations manually each RenderStepped by copying TimePosition, IsPlaying and WeightCurrent from the character track; use Animator, not Humanoid, for animations.
- 2021-03-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developing-expert-developers-advice-from-all-around-the-world/1134437): Use a proven admin system (Cmdr, HD Admin, Adonis, etc.) unless you want full control; scripting your own gives a unique, customizable result.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [18:24](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=1104): Use Humanoid:AddAccessory rather than parenting accessories to the character; it ensures the accessory works correctly.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [20:15](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=1215): Clone a 'real' accessory from ServerStorage per player so one hat can be granted to many players; check the character doesn't already have it.
- 2021-04-23 Emancyphur [22:22](https://youtu.be/DmsbnwtaVi0?t=1342): Use CollectionService tags to loop over accessories anywhere in the game, so new hats need only be tagged, not moved into a folder.
- 2021-05-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208): Destroy projectiles once they exceed a max travel distance to avoid leaking instances.
- 2021-05-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507): Do not pass damage as a client parameter in production; validate hits server-side with a second raycast, since this tutorial simplifies for beginners.
- 2021-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842): Replace while true do loops with .Changed, GetPropertyChangedSignal, or RunService events where possible.
- 2021-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842): Use task.wait() instead of wait(); it is safe and equivalent to RunService.Heartbeat:Wait().
- 2021-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842): Set Instance parent on a separate line (instance.Parent = x) instead of Instance.new(class, parent); the latter is slower.
- 2021-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842): Store expensive results like GetChildren() or threshold calculations outside loops instead of recomputing per iteration.
- 2021-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastores-beginners-to-advanced/1275421): Pass the function, then self, then arguments to pcall (pcall(dataStore.GetAsync, dataStore, key)) rather than wrapping in an anonymous function.
- 2021-06-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datastores-beginners-to-advanced/1275421): Use ipairs for arrays and Players:GetPlayers() rather than GetChildren on the Players service.
- 2021-06-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/be-careful-when-using-assert-and-why/1289175): Don't put string concatenation or tostring() in assert's message argument; Lua evaluates it on every call even when the condition passes.
- 2021-06-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/be-careful-when-using-assert-and-why/1289175): Use an if-statement with error() for hot-path argument validation so the message is built only when the check fails.
- 2021-06-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/be-careful-when-using-assert-and-why/1289175): Watch the Script Performance view in Studio; a sudden rise in script activity can come from costly assert message formatting.
- 2021-07-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-custom-loadinghealth-bars/1323459): Guard the 1/ratio calculation against zero (use sizeRatio > 0 check) to avoid dividing by zero.
- 2021-07-04 SimTek Game Development [5:32](https://youtu.be/lJktfRT2pbg?t=332): Compute waypoints dynamically with PathfindingService:CreatePath and ComputeAsync instead of hardcoding waypoint folders; hardcoded points break when the map changes.
- 2021-07-04 SimTek Game Development [5:32](https://youtu.be/lJktfRT2pbg?t=332): Check path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success before reading waypoints; otherwise handle the 'no path' case to avoid nil errors when the NPC is boxed in.
- 2021-07-04 SimTek Game Development [7:37](https://youtu.be/lJktfRT2pbg?t=457): Drive movement with Humanoid:MoveTo one waypoint at a time, advancing the index in MoveToFinished only when reached is true.
- 2021-07-04 SimTek Game Development [8:58](https://youtu.be/lJktfRT2pbg?t=538): Honour waypoint.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump by calling Humanoid.Jump; the default action is walk.
- 2021-07-04 SimTek Game Development [14:46](https://youtu.be/lJktfRT2pbg?t=886): Connect path.Blocked and recompute the path only if the blocked waypoint index is ahead of the current index; ignore blockages behind the NPC.
- 2021-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/comprehensive-beginners-guide-to-cframes-and-how-to-use-them-cframe-guide/1334085): Use CFrame.lookAt(pos, target) to position and aim cameras or parts in one line instead of manual rotation math.
- 2021-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/comprehensive-beginners-guide-to-cframes-and-how-to-use-them-cframe-guide/1334085): CFrame.Angles takes radians and applies rotations in Z, Y, X order; Orientation on parts uses degrees and Y, X, Z order. Don't mix them up.
- 2021-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/comprehensive-beginners-guide-to-cframes-and-how-to-use-them-cframe-guide/1334085): Rotation order matters: rotating by the same angles in a different order gives a different orientation.
- 2021-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/comprehensive-beginners-guide-to-cframes-and-how-to-use-them-cframe-guide/1334085): Use CFrame:Lerp(otherCFrame, fraction) to smoothly interpolate between two positions and orientations.
- 2021-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/comprehensive-beginners-guide-to-cframes-and-how-to-use-them-cframe-guide/1334085): Use CFrame:Inverse() to undo a transform; cf:Inverse() * (cf * v) returns v.
- 2021-07-18 SimTek Game Development [0:49](https://youtu.be/NsnoDX2dEY8?t=49): Make HTTP calls from a server Script, not a LocalScript; HttpService requests only run on the server, so pass results to clients via a RemoteEvent.
- 2021-07-18 SimTek Game Development [0:18](https://youtu.be/NsnoDX2dEY8?t=18): Enable 'Allow HTTP Requests' under game Settings > Security before using HttpService, or requests will fail.
- 2021-07-18 SimTek Game Development [6:46](https://youtu.be/NsnoDX2dEY8?t=406): Print the decoded HTTP response and inspect it before parsing; API payloads often nest fields (e.g. fact and length) you must index explicitly.
- 2021-07-18 SimTek Game Development [8:09](https://youtu.be/NsnoDX2dEY8?t=489): Wrap the parsed value with tostring() before firing it through a RemoteEvent so the client receives a string for the label.
- 2021-08-09 B Ricey [19:06](https://youtu.be/TgZI48DTNqE?t=1146): Create unlockables before replaying saved unlocks, otherwise publishes are missed; call LoadUnlocks after the unlock setup in init.
- 2021-08-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcall-xpcall-and-ypcall-tutorial-how-do-they-work/1417471): Wrap DataStore GetAsync/SetAsync calls in pcall() since they are network calls that can error.
- 2021-08-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcall-xpcall-and-ypcall-tutorial-how-do-they-work/1417471): Check the first pcall return value (bool) before using the second value; on false, the second value holds the error message.
- 2021-09-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/open-source-lock-on-combat-in-3d/1458506): Replace loop-based attack cooldowns with task.delay() or multithreading; loop-style cooldowns are very inefficient and can cause major lag.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [32:36](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1956): For mobile, cap 3D model triangle counts and brick counts; prioritise smooth performance over fine detail.
- 2021-10-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-procedural-mountains-a-fractal-noise-tutorial/1494362): Store colour and layer settings in a table and loop over it instead of long if-else chains for maintainability.
- 2021-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nature2d-2d-physics-engine-for-ui-elements/1510935): Nature2D runs every RenderStepped; it holds 60fps with about 100 collisions per frame, but frames drop with many bodies.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [19:50](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1190): Keep tech requests from cadence small; limit them to light hooks like quest triggers so major updates are not delayed.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [44:22](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2662): Use embedded variables in quest text (e.g. amount) so rebalancing does not require rewriting or re-localising strings.
- 2021-11-10 Suphi Kaner [29:57](https://youtu.be/JyMxrcqEzu8?t=1797): Set AgentRadius, AgentHeight, AgentCanJump and Costs (e.g. Water = 20) in CreatePath; enable the Pathfinding Modifiers beta feature to use Costs.
- 2021-11-10 Suphi Kaner [43:17](https://youtu.be/JyMxrcqEzu8?t=2597): Wrap ComputeAsync in pcall and check status == Success; otherwise fall back to teleporting the NPC to the target.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [4:37](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=277): Use variables in quest text instead of hard-coded values, sourcing numbers from the quest balance spreadsheet to avoid re-editing and relocalizing text.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [10:52](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=652): Hide event code in ServerStorage until launch, then copy it to ReplicatedStorage on the countdown event so exploiters cannot read it beforehand.
- 2021-12-09 Last Level Studios [16:19](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=979): Roblox's default VR GUI behaves oddly; building a custom GUI system was easier than working around it.
- 2021-12-10 Suphi Kaner [15:52](https://youtu.be/bk8UVm-gxBs?t=952): Roblox now makes identical functions raw-equal; avoid capturing per-instance variables in methods so closures stay identical and shared in memory.
- 2021-12-14 GnomeCode [4:29](https://youtu.be/DanjB0cTfw0?t=269): Anchor waypoint parts, disable their collision and make them semi-transparent so enemies don't bump into them.
- 2021-12-14 GnomeCode [5:44](https://youtu.be/DanjB0cTfw0?t=344): Loop over waypoints with a numbered for-loop using waypoints:GetChildren() and #waypoints to drive movement.
- 2021-12-14 GnomeCode [6:24](https://youtu.be/DanjB0cTfw0?t=384): Call Humanoid:MoveTo then Humanoid.MoveToFinished:Wait() so each enemy reaches a waypoint before heading to the next.
- 2021-12-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710): Store RBXScriptConnections in a Janitor and disconnect them when no longer needed to avoid leaks and stray threads.
- 2021-12-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710): Always pass the cleanup method name explicitly when adding Instances or custom objects (e.g. "Destroy", "DisconnectAll") for consistent habits.
- 2021-12-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710): Use janitor:LinkToInstance(instance) so the Janitor cleans up automatically when that instance is destroyed.
- 2021-12-28 Suphi Kaner [25:32](https://youtu.be/SFAwwmb8G6o?t=1532): Guard speed calculation with math.max(delta, 0.01) to avoid division by zero producing NaN.
- 2021-12-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949): Store per-player anti-cheat state in a table keyed by player and clean it up when players leave.
- 2022-01-04 GnomeCode [1:48](https://youtu.be/de5-o6euwJE?t=108): Before loading an animation, check animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() for an existing track by name and reuse it; loading per call hits the 256-track-per-animator limit.
- 2022-01-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/canvasdraw-a-fast-and-powerful-graphics-library-draw-pixels-lines-triangles-readmodify-image-data-and-much-more/1624633): Set Canvas.AutoRender = false when using multiple canvases, then call Canvas:Render() only when needed to avoid per-frame EditableImage updates.
- 2022-01-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/canvasdraw-a-fast-and-powerful-graphics-library-draw-pixels-lines-triangles-readmodify-image-data-and-much-more/1624633): Use SetRGB(x, y, r, g, b) instead of SetPixel with Color3 for faster pixel writes.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [28:34](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1714): For FPS hit detection, client-side hit detection with server checks (ammo, state, position, geometry) favors feel; check known exploits server-side and monitor exploit hubs monthly.
- 2022-02-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-cos-and-sin/1659301): Roblox cos and sin take radians, not degrees; convert degrees with math.pi*2 / 360 scaling before calling them.
- 2022-02-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-cos-and-sin/1659301): Use math.acos and math.asin to recover an angle in radians from a value between -1 and 1.
- 2022-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-entity-component-system/1664447): Run ECS systems through Matter.Loop bound to a signal such as RunService.Heartbeat, scheduling all systems in one place.
- 2022-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-entity-component-system/1664447): Consider Jecs if raw performance matters; the author says its query iterators are as fast as direct array iteration.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basics-of-cframe-multiplication-and-weldsmotor6dsjoint-instances/1682079): When rotating a part in world space, rotate at origin first then add position back: part.CFrame = rotation.Rotation + part.Position, to avoid orbital motion.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basics-of-cframe-multiplication-and-weldsmotor6dsjoint-instances/1682079): Modify Motor6D joints by changing C0 with Part0.CFrame:Inverse() * targetWorldCFrame * C1, keeping original C0 position, so limbs stay attached.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basics-of-cframe-multiplication-and-weldsmotor6dsjoint-instances/1682079): Use CFrame.lookAt-style world CFrames for head look-at and arm pointing by converting them into joint C0 space, which is useful for IK.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basics-of-cframe-multiplication-and-weldsmotor6dsjoint-instances/1682079): Prefer CFrame.fromOrientation over CFrame.Angles when working with Studio's orientation values, since Angles does not equal orientation.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/my-intro-to-raycasting-what-why-and-how/1681977): Pass a RaycastParams object as the third argument to workspace:Raycast to control which instances are hit.
- 2022-02-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/my-intro-to-raycasting-what-why-and-how/1681977): Set RaycastParams.FilterType to Whitelist with FilterDescendantsInstances to hit only specific objects, or Blacklist to ignore them.
- 2022-04-14 Suphi Kaner [33:28](https://youtu.be/FVOowr2DXbM?t=2008): Disconnect the seat.Changed connection when the driver exits, or handlers stack (each event fires once more per re-entry).
- 2022-04-14 Suphi Kaner [1:06:53](https://youtu.be/FVOowr2DXbM?t=4013): Structure car logic as: sit script (prompt), network owner server script, and drive local script connected to Seated; rename scripts by role.
- 2022-04-16 B Ricey [9:08](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=548): Use MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase for gamepasses; PromptProductPurchase silently fails for them.
- 2022-04-16 B Ricey [10:31](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=631): Handle both CollectionService GetTagged and GetInstanceAddedSignal so buttons added later or loaded in a different order still work.
- 2022-04-16 B Ricey [12:17](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=737): Keep a server module mapping gamepass ID to a handler function; add new passes by adding entries without changing the player-join code.
- 2022-04-16 B Ricey [13:39](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=819): Default the multiplier with 'or 1' when the attribute is nil so non-owners get unchanged values.
- 2022-04-16 B Ricey [15:03](https://youtu.be/TmiU5CY6BW0?t=903): Check ownership on the server with MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync when the player joins and apply the perk there, not on the client.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487): Replicate only VFX to clients via FireAllClients; keep hitboxes and damage on the server, since client-side hitboxes are vulnerable to exploiters.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487): Server-sided VFX increases server load and ping, especially with 10+ players using moves at once; render effects on clients instead.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-replication-101-the-guide-to-replicating-effects-to-clients/1789487): Use Debris:AddItem to clean up cloned effects and anchor them with CanCollide false so they don't interfere with physics.
- 2022-05-28 B Ricey [3:39](https://youtu.be/T0xqkYODIl0?t=219): Call BadgeService:AwardBadge with player.UserId, not the Player object, when awarding a badge; passing the Player is a common mistake.
- 2022-05-28 B Ricey [5:06](https://youtu.be/T0xqkYODIl0?t=306): Keep badge logic separate from core mechanics so changes to gameplay don't require editing badge code.
- 2022-05-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simple-pathfinding-ai/1815347): Re-path inside RunService.Heartbeat only if needed; the author re-computes every frame, which is simple but costly. Consider throttling path recomputation.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [1:00:44](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=3644): Measure FPS and memory and optimise so the game runs on old low-end devices, widening accessibility.
- 2022-06-16 Roblox Creators [1:02:50](https://youtu.be/hCT9zCJ1M5c?t=3770): Avoid unnecessary network requests and design new meshes and assets with performance on every device in mind.
- 2022-06-19 Suphi Kaner [0:07](https://youtu.be/BbIPalpAfaI?t=7): Open MicroProfiler with Ctrl+Alt+F6 and pause with Ctrl+P to inspect frame timing per thread and per script.
- 2022-06-19 Suphi Kaner [3:33](https://youtu.be/BbIPalpAfaI?t=213): Use debug.profilebegin/debug.profileend labels to see exactly which chunks of script code cost frame time in MicroProfiler.
- 2022-06-19 Suphi Kaner [8:21](https://youtu.be/BbIPalpAfaI?t=501): RenderStepped runs on the main thread; Stepped and Heartbeat can run on worker threads when connected with ConnectParallel inside an Actor.
- 2022-06-19 Suphi Kaner [13:39](https://youtu.be/BbIPalpAfaI?t=819): Put scripts that should run in parallel inside separate Actors; scripts in the same Actor still run one after another.
- 2022-06-19 Suphi Kaner [14:59](https://youtu.be/BbIPalpAfaI?t=899): Use task.desynchronize() for heavy computation on worker threads and task.synchronize() before touching the DataModel or Instances.
- 2022-06-19 B Ricey [7:19](https://youtu.be/TeMGAqzZrDc?t=439): Don't run a round loop as a bare while-true; a single runtime error breaks the whole loop. Chain stages with promises so errors are caught and cleanup still runs.
- 2022-06-28 Roblox [39:18](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=2358): Abstract low-level complexity out of exposed APIs; exposing internals let the community misuse them, forcing a relaunch of the facial API.
- 2022-06-28 Roblox [17:43](https://youtu.be/SOoe0E1Ql5M?t=1063): Build generic primitives (e.g. sorted map, priority queue) rather than bespoke services; developers then build matchmaking and other systems on top.
- 2022-06-30 Suphi Kaner [26:23](https://youtu.be/B446FyN1xtc?t=1583): Validate on the server before changing values: check the player's data folder exists and the apple count is below storage capacity before spawning more.
- 2022-07-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779): Wrap path:ComputeAsync in pcall with a retry loop (MAX_RETRIES=5, RETRY_COOLDOWN=5s) since it makes network calls and can fail.
- 2022-07-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779): Call pcall(path.ComputeAsync, path, start, goal) rather than wrapping in an anonymous function, to avoid unnecessary closure creation.
- 2022-07-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779): Follow waypoints with humanoid.MoveToFinished rather than a for loop; the event fires per waypoint and lets you handle blocking and timeouts.
- 2022-07-13 Suphi Kaner [0:41](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=41): Prefer physics-driven movement (AssemblyLinearVelocity/anchored parts) over manually setting CFrame each Heartbeat; benchmark showed ~50% lower script cost at 1,000–2,000 parts.
- 2022-07-13 Suphi Kaner [6:19](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=379): Don't rely on Touched or query events for CFrame-moved parts; they did not fire when CFrame was set manually, while physics-moved parts triggered them.
- 2022-07-13 Suphi Kaner [7:00](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=420): CFrame-moved parts do not collide with other parts; physics parts do. Choose physics if collisions matter.
- 2022-07-13 Suphi Kaner [10:04](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=604): Manual CFrame updates on the server replicate far more data (~271 KB/s vs ~40 KB/s for 100 parts in the test); physics replication is throttled by distance and saves bandwidth.
- 2022-07-13 Suphi Kaner [11:26](https://youtu.be/thfUspopuGE?t=686): Roblox's physics replication reduces update rate for parts far from the camera, scaling much better than CFrame updates when part counts grow.
- 2022-07-25 Emancyphur [9:28](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=568): Bind zones to a group 'EnterOnlyOneZoneAtATime' to stop overlapping zones from fighting over Atmosphere changes.
- 2022-07-25 Emancyphur [10:52](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=652): Clean up zone connections on removal and disconnect/nil them so removed zones stop listening.
- 2022-07-25 Emancyphur [3:30](https://youtu.be/UYnn9JF8qCc?t=210): Run Atmosphere zone changes in a LocalScript for per-player visuals; server changes affect everyone.
- 2022-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/bridgenet-insanely-optimized-easy-to-use-networking-library-full-of-utilities-now-with-roblox-ts-v199-beta/1909935): Batch multiple remote calls into one remote call per frame; BridgeNet reports this avoids ordering stalls caused by many individual RemoteEvents.
- 2022-08-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/bridgenet-insanely-optimized-easy-to-use-networking-library-full-of-utilities-now-with-roblox-ts-v199-beta/1909935): Compress data before sending using dictionary-to-table conversion and compressed ID strings instead of sending verbose keys each call.
- 2022-08-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-best-practices-how-to-keep-your-games-ping-low/1913475): Send only the fields the client needs (e.g. item name and class), not full tables with history or damage data.
- 2022-08-09 Suphi Kaner [7:18](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=438): Use TeleportAsync instead of the deprecated teleport methods (TeleportToPlace etc.), which the docs say are consolidated into it.
- 2022-08-09 Suphi Kaner [9:21](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=561): Wrap TeleportAsync in pcall; it can fail, and it only works in published games, not Studio.
- 2022-08-09 Suphi Kaner [21:17](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=1277): Studio cannot reserve servers; test by publishing and read the access code from a TextBox, since the developer console does not allow selecting text.
- 2022-08-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732): Load data for players already in the server with task.spawn, in case they joined before the script ran.
- 2022-08-21 B Ricey [2:08](https://youtu.be/2Msrw5w5cTs?t=128): Forgotten connections and parts keep running after an object is done, causing leaks; clean up every connection, event and part when the object is destroyed.
- 2022-09-19 TheNexusAvenger [4:12](https://youtu.be/C4ASAayPpkY?t=252): IK Control needs an Animator (AnimationController or Humanoid) present; without it, it silently fails with no error.
- 2022-09-23 GnomeCode [18:57](https://youtu.be/aFKaY-PmR08?t=1137): Read event and method docs for hidden behaviour. For example, Humanoid:MoveTo stops after about 8 seconds and needs handling for long moves.
- 2022-11-17 XTB7 [2:56](https://youtu.be/k2p9KMrlSAA?t=176): Use humanoid.MoveToFinished:Wait() before stopping the animation so the animation stops when the character arrives.
- 2022-11-22 Roblox Creators [35:49](https://youtu.be/xXIc-p9hatU?t=2149): Write code efficiently; small inefficiencies add up across many complex systems and hurt FPS.
- 2022-11-23 Dev Total [22:48](https://youtu.be/0K-5sifYhxs?t=1368): Precompute and hardcode frequently used path positions from PathfindingService output into a module instead of calling PathfindingService every time, for speed.
- 2022-11-23 Dev Total [24:52](https://youtu.be/0K-5sifYhxs?t=1492): Use PathfindingService material cost settings (e.g., set a material to 0 to make it preferred) to control enemy routes.
- 2022-12-04 SimTek Game Development [2:06](https://youtu.be/BiaMIbrzo0Q?t=126): Cap raycast distance (e.g. 300 studs) so shots have a range limit; raycasts otherwise reach infinitely, unlike click-based damage.
- 2022-12-04 SimTek Game Development [4:57](https://youtu.be/BiaMIbrzo0Q?t=297): Use RaycastFilterType Blacklist with a table of excluded instances (own character, accessories) so the ray ignores them.
- 2022-12-04 SimTek Game Development [8:10](https://youtu.be/BiaMIbrzo0Q?t=490): When a raycast hits an accessory, add it to the filter and recast recursively until a body part or non-accessory is hit.
- 2022-12-08 GnomeCode [2:43](https://youtu.be/PNYtTkMidE8?t=163): Default Roblox animate script is ~800 lines; a trimmed custom version (~228 lines) can be used instead, and it fixes the run/walk blend glitch at changed speeds.
- 2022-12-10 SimTek Game Development [5:34](https://youtu.be/mrmB0hQGCXE?t=334): Use workspace:Raycast from above the map (e.g. Y=300) straight down to find ground height instead of hard-coded Y values.
- 2022-12-10 SimTek Game Development [6:58](https://youtu.be/mrmB0hQGCXE?t=418): Pass RaycastParams (or nil for defaults) as third raycast argument; use ignore lists to skip water or other non-ground objects.
- 2022-12-10 SimTek Game Development [6:58](https://youtu.be/mrmB0hQGCXE?t=418): Always check that the raycast returned a result, since it can miss (e.g. off the edge of the map).
- 2022-12-10 SimTek Game Development [7:40](https://youtu.be/mrmB0hQGCXE?t=460): Return the full hit position from a cast function so callers get X, Y, Z and can offset Y as needed.
- 2022-12-15 GnomeCode [2:29](https://youtu.be/R-xurNJtx7M?t=149): Use CFrame:Lerp(goal, alpha) to move parts; it gives control over partial movement without hand-calculating positions and rotations.
- 2022-12-15 GnomeCode [4:08](https://youtu.be/R-xurNJtx7M?t=248): Drive per-frame movement with RunService.Heartbeat rather than a for-loop with task.wait, and use deltaTime to track elapsed time.
- 2022-12-15 GnomeCode [6:09](https://youtu.be/R-xurNJtx7M?t=369): Compute alpha as elapsed time divided by total duration, then disconnect the Heartbeat connection once alpha reaches 1.
- 2022-12-22 GnomeCode [1:34](https://youtu.be/Wl3mXFlRPo4?t=94): Raycast direction is target.Position minus origin.Position; Workspace:Raycast takes origin, direction, and optional RaycastParams.
- 2022-12-22 GnomeCode [5:27](https://youtu.be/Wl3mXFlRPo4?t=327): Use RaycastParams with FilterType Blacklist and FilterDescendantsInstances to ignore transparent parts like windows instead of hitting them.
- 2022-12-25 SimTek Game Development [8:28](https://youtu.be/c1jFXwmRtzI?t=508): Use Workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox instead of the deprecated Region3 API to find parts inside a box; pass nil for OverlapParams since they gave no optimisation benefit here.
- 2022-12-25 SimTek Game Development [9:50](https://youtu.be/c1jFXwmRtzI?t=590): Detect a player inside a region by checking if a part's parent name matches the player's character name, then break the loop early once found.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [0:28](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=28): Put input-handling code in a LocalScript (e.g. StarterPlayerScripts); UserInputService and ContextActionService only work on the client.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [1:08](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=68): Use UserInputService.InputBegan with the gameProcessed boolean to ignore inputs already handled by the game, such as UI clicks.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [4:20](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=260): Name ContextActionService actions descriptively (e.g. 'change part colour'), not after the key used to trigger them.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [5:43](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=343): BindAction accepts multiple KeyCodes or UserInputTypes, so one action can respond to several inputs.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [6:25](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=385): ContextActionService fires on Begin, Change and End states; check inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin to act once per press.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [9:19](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=559): Call ContextActionService:UnbindAction(name) to stop an action once it is no longer needed; unbound actions cost nothing when inputs occur.
- 2023-01-07 XTB7 [12:24](https://youtu.be/DIz1Nk7YOUo?t=744): Prefer ContextActionService over UserInputService for input only valid in certain states; UserInputService handler runs on every InputBegan, hurting performance as logic grows.
- 2023-01-07 SimTek Game Development [7:22](https://youtu.be/zmTsh66uxsI?t=442): Run the infinite queue-processing loop in task.spawn so it does not block the rest of the script.
- 2023-01-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/movement-system/2135683): Use a while loop checking arrival at destination; after the loop, stop the unit and remove its beam, so cleanup always runs.
- 2023-02-03 SimTek Game Development [2:45](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=165): Guard knockback with nil and existence checks on both characters' HumanoidRootParts before applying forces to avoid errors when players are respawning.
- 2023-02-03 SimTek Game Development [3:26](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=206): Normalize the direction vector with .Unit and add a small upward component so knocked targets lift off the ground.
- 2023-02-03 SimTek Game Development [5:28](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=328): Set VectorForce RelativeTo to World or Attachment and tune force magnitude (example 10000) by testing values; the result varies a lot.
- 2023-02-03 SimTek Game Development [6:49](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=409): Set Humanoid.PlatformStand to true during knockback so the character stays in a free-falling state, then reset it after about 2 seconds.
- 2023-02-03 SimTek Game Development [8:10](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=490): Unanchor test rigs before testing knockback, since anchored HumanoidRootParts won't move.
- 2023-02-03 SimTek Game Development [10:15](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=615): Set AngularVelocity MaxTorque to math.huge so the spin is applied reliably.
- 2023-02-03 SimTek Game Development [11:37](https://youtu.be/-sQHMwuVAu4?t=697): Clean up temporary constraints and attachments with Debris:AddItem after about 0.2 seconds so they don't accumulate.
- 2023-02-11 XTB7 [1:23](https://youtu.be/0TpwPpULXxU?t=83): Use RunService:BindToRenderStep with Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1 (or 201) so your camera changes aren't overridden by Roblox.
- 2023-02-11 XTB7 [2:50](https://youtu.be/0TpwPpULXxU?t=170): Set camera.CameraType to Scriptable, otherwise custom camera changes won't take effect.
- 2023-02-11 XTB7 [3:31](https://youtu.be/0TpwPpULXxU?t=211): Combine CFrames with multiplication (*), not addition; adding offsets every frame with + causes runaway drift.
- 2023-02-11 XTB7 [4:32](https://youtu.be/0TpwPpULXxU?t=272): Put the camera LocalScript in StarterCharacterScripts so it doesn't error when the character dies and no character exists.
- 2023-02-11 XTB7 [8:01](https://youtu.be/0TpwPpULXxU?t=481): CFrame.Angles axes are not intuitive: X tilts up/down, Y turns left/right, Z tilts/rolls; use math.rad to convert degrees.
- 2023-02-13 Suphi Kaner [4:35](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=275): IKControl chain: set ChainRoot and EndEffector to define which Motor6Ds rotate; Transform type matches the target CFrame, Position matches location only.
- 2023-02-13 Suphi Kaner [11:26](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=686): Set IKControl.Pole via command bar (not properties, it is hidden) to control elbow/bend direction.
- 2023-02-13 Suphi Kaner [47:32](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=2852): Add a task.wait before creating IKControl on character added to avoid rare float bugs; destroy IKControl on Humanoid.Died to avoid the neck IK crash.
- 2023-02-13 Suphi Kaner [54:38](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=3278): Set IKControl Priority to 1 for hand IKs so they solve after the torso IK, otherwise hands drift off the steering wheel when the head turns.
- 2023-02-13 Suphi Kaner [15:57](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=957): For a steering wheel, set HingeConstraint ActuatorType to Servo with infinite MaxTorque and drive TargetAngle from VehicleSeat.SteerFloat times 45.
- 2023-02-13 Suphi Kaner [23:45](https://youtu.be/8fV-XOOj6O4?t=1425): Run IK-holding logic on the server so IK replicates to all clients; enable hand IKs on Humanoid.Seated and disable them on unseat.
- 2023-02-17 Suphi Kaner [11:03](https://youtu.be/Ng1uYpFcpOA?t=663): Scripts, local scripts and modules created or edited on the client do not replicate to the server; keep authoritative logic on the server.
- 2023-02-21 Suphi Kaner [7:57](https://youtu.be/hr_8svt1n1E?t=477): Handle death server-side: listen to Humanoid.HealthChanged, return if health > 0, then re-enable Dead state, set state to Dead and break joints.
- 2023-02-27 Suphi Kaner [0:00](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=0): Server-side tweens replicate every property change to all clients; this cost showed as network incoming rising from ~0.16 to ~1.66 KB/s in the video's test.
- 2023-02-27 Suphi Kaner [13:13](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=793): Create TweenInfo once at the top of the script and reuse it rather than rebuilding it on every trigger.
- 2023-02-27 Suphi Kaner [15:18](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=918): Skip server-side property setting when TweenInfo.Reverses is true, since the instance returns to its original state; return early when RepeatCount is -1 (infinite).
- 2023-02-27 Suphi Kaner [16:41](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=1001): Total tween duration = (DelayTime + Time) * (RepeatCount + 1); use this to schedule the server-side state sync with task.delay.
- 2023-02-27 Suphi Kaner [18:02](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=1082): After the tween duration, set the final properties on the server so server and client state match once the tween ends.
- 2023-02-27 Suphi Kaner [19:25](https://youtu.be/ZJnMimf9n5Y?t=1165): task.delay cannot be cancelled by a new tween on its own; store the returned thread per instance and cancel it before starting a new tween to avoid stale property sets.
- 2023-03-08 Emancyphur [0:56](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=56): Read the error's Script path and line number first; the error names the missing child and the parent it was searched in.
- 2023-03-08 Emancyphur [2:18](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=138): Check exact spelling and capitalization against Explorer or Properties; Roblox names and properties are case-sensitive (BrickColor, not Brickcolor).
- 2023-03-08 Emancyphur [3:43](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=223): Use the full path through nested folders (e.g. Workspace.Furniture.Table); a search result showing an object elsewhere does not make a dot-path find it.
- 2023-03-08 Emancyphur [6:08](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=368): Use :FindFirstChild() with a conditional when an object may not exist yet, so the script does not error immediately.
- 2023-03-08 Emancyphur [6:49](https://youtu.be/lZrfA9Ogv2M?t=409): Use :WaitForChild(name, seconds) when you are sure an object will appear; it yields the code below it, so put it in a separate script or reorder if needed.
- 2023-04-14 B Ricey [9:32](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=572): Set AlignPosition MaxVelocity to a finite value (e.g. 10); default is Infinity, which flings attached objects off.
- 2023-04-14 B Ricey [11:04](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=664): Use a task.wait loop for infrequent spawn checks; for time-critical logic prefer RunService events instead.
- 2023-04-14 B Ricey [24:30](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=1470): Set the Model PrimaryPart before calling GetPivot, or GetPivot will error.
- 2023-04-14 B Ricey [14:12](https://youtu.be/jaitqSU2HIA?t=852): Tag packages with an attribute (e.g. Target) rather than relying on names to identify them.
- 2023-04-15 Roox4 [3:15](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=195): When PathfindingService fails on stairs, add custom waypoints for that section and have enemies follow them instead of default path waypoints.
- 2023-04-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-custom-chat-tag/2302562): Check msg.TextSource before modifying the prefix so system messages without a source are not altered.
- 2023-06-10 B Ricey [4:30](https://youtu.be/BBoJiE4IkSg?t=270): Use HingeConstraints with ActuatorType Servo, high speed (~5) and very large MaxTorque (~50,000) so the turret moves properly under physics.
- 2023-06-10 B Ricey [10:24](https://youtu.be/BBoJiE4IkSg?t=624): Create an invisible anchored reference part at the turret's rotation fulcrum; use its CFrame to convert world targets to local space so aiming works in any orientation.
- 2023-06-10 B Ricey [12:46](https://youtu.be/BBoJiE4IkSg?t=766): Update hinge TargetAngle in RunService.Stepped (runs before physics each frame) so hinges are set before simulation.
- 2023-06-10 B Ricey [14:47](https://youtu.be/BBoJiE4IkSg?t=887): Flatten the local direction by multiplying by Vector3.new(1,0,1) and use Vector3:Angle with the Y axis for signed 360-degree yaw; convert with math.deg.
- 2023-06-10 B Ricey [18:44](https://youtu.be/BBoJiE4IkSg?t=1124): If aim is backwards or off by 90 degrees, flip the direction sign, negate the degrees, or swap the Z axis for X axis; debug yaw first, then pitch.
- 2023-06-10 B Ricey [19:36](https://youtu.be/BBoJiE4IkSg?t=1176): For pitch, get a perpendicular axis via cross product of local Y axis and flat direction, then use Vector3:Angle against the flat direction to set pitch TargetAngle.
- 2023-06-15 Suphi Kaner [25:14](https://youtu.be/UAdE8-AfuMo?t=1514): Read the live state from the DataStore object, not a cached variable captured earlier, since the cached value never updates after open.
- 2023-06-15 Suphi Kaner [0:21](https://youtu.be/UAdE8-AfuMo?t=21): Set ProximityPrompt hold duration and disable requires line of sight for quick NPC interactions like selling.
- 2023-06-22 Suphi Kaner [37:14](https://youtu.be/ARPfIt16sXI?t=2234): Schedule timers with task.delay and cancel them with task.cancel; use os.clock for drift-free intervals.
- 2023-07-07 XTB7 [0:00](https://youtu.be/WfZhGsivpBM?t=0): Always check that Raycast returned a result before indexing it; an unsuccessful raycast returns nil and will error otherwise.
- 2023-07-07 XTB7 [1:22](https://youtu.be/WfZhGsivpBM?t=82): Raycast direction must be destination minus origin (target position - origin), not the target position itself.
- 2023-07-07 XTB7 [5:30](https://youtu.be/WfZhGsivpBM?t=330): Use RaycastParams FilterDescendantsInstances with Exclude to ignore parts; be careful not to filter out things the ray should hit.
- 2023-07-07 XTB7 [7:33](https://youtu.be/WfZhGsivpBM?t=453): Set CanQuery to false on a part to make raycasts ignore it; simpler than using RaycastParams RespectCanCollide.
- 2023-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-realistic-npc-eyesight-system/2456522): Run each NPC's sight check in its own coroutine on a loop with task.wait(1) rather than every frame to limit cost.
- 2023-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-realistic-npc-eyesight-system/2456522): Use a raycast from the NPC head to block sight through walls; exclude the NPC and all character models from RaycastParams filter.
- 2023-07-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-realistic-npc-eyesight-system/2456522): Check field of view with the dot product of the direction-to-target and head LookVector, and cheap distance check before the raycast to avoid unnecessary raycasts.
- 2023-07-30 GnomeCode [2:04](https://youtu.be/UsPYgIW3rv8?t=124): Use CollectionService tags to find all instances of a type; tag models with a plugin such as Tag Editor instead of hardcoding references.
- 2023-07-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650): Organise shared modules in a Libs folder in ServerScriptService and keep a Manager module as the single access point for player data.
- 2023-07-31 Amora [2:15](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=135): Set a Script's Run Context to Client so it runs inside a part; regular LocalScripts only run in specific containers like backpack, character, PlayerGui, or PlayerScripts.
- 2023-07-31 Amora [3:57](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=237): Compute Region3 corners from part.Position ± part.Size/2 instead of placing manual corner parts.
- 2023-07-31 Amora [6:21](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=381): Use OverlapParams with FilterType Include and the character as the filter descendant so only the player's own parts are detected.
- 2023-07-31 Amora [7:44](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=464): Use workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox instead of the deprecated FindPartsInRegion3.
- 2023-07-31 Amora [5:39](https://youtu.be/KPK86YQ6t-Y?t=339): Reset the inRegion flag each frame before the check so music stops if the player leaves the zone.
- 2023-08-03 Amora [8:33](https://youtu.be/vr1fae2qROM?t=513): Handle players already in the server by looping over Players:GetPlayers() with task.spawn(playerAdded) alongside the PlayerAdded connection.
- 2023-08-18 SimTek Game Development [8:05](https://youtu.be/U22nZqDmTXI?t=485): Check ownership with MarketplaceService:UserOwnsGamePassAsync inside pcall on join, since it is asynchronous and can fail.
- 2023-08-18 SimTek Game Development [10:10](https://youtu.be/U22nZqDmTXI?t=610): Connect PromptGamePassPurchaseFinished to grant the benefit right after purchase; it also fires on cancel, so check the purchased flag.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Adopt good coding practices early; avoid hard-coded logic to reduce bugs and post-launch patching.
- 2023-08-29 Suphi Kaner [9:49](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=589): Sync cosmetic movement across clients by computing position from workspace:GetServerTimeNow() instead of local time; network traffic stays near zero.
- 2023-08-29 Suphi Kaner [27:01](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=1621): Parameterise path movement by arc length (magnitude between points divided by speed) so constant speed doesn't depend on point spacing.
- 2023-08-29 Suphi Kaner [36:22](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=2182): Destroy client-side instances when a streamed model is removed, otherwise re-streaming creates duplicates.
- 2023-08-29 Suphi Kaner [33:37](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=2017): When listening for streamed-in models, loop existing children and call the handler once before connecting ChildAdded so nothing is missed.
- 2023-08-29 Suphi Kaner [4:39](https://youtu.be/OAZvYqbzOWI?t=279): Use FloatCurve keys with tangents to shape smooth motion; leave tangents unset to let Roblox auto-calculate them.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Replace deprecated wait() with task.wait(); the author says it is more stable and about 2x faster.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Use RunService.Heartbeat instead of while wait() loops for per-frame code.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Connect functions to events once at startup rather than creating connections repeatedly at runtime.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Set all properties on an Instance before parenting it to the game, so the engine does not process changes repeatedly.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Use modules to store functions and tables so one require serves many calls instead of repeated lookups.
- 2023-09-12 janisjanis01 [4:31](https://youtu.be/9aQpeTCZ7yM?t=271): Check for typos first when a previously working script suddenly breaks; the video's bugs were often single-character typos.
- 2023-09-12 janisjanis01 [5:11](https://youtu.be/9aQpeTCZ7yM?t=311): Checkpoints made from a plain part spawned the player in the wrong place on reset; a dedicated checkpoint design fixed it.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [5:56](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=356): Prioritise performance for all audiences, including high-FPS hardcore players and mobile; understand every code segment's performance.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [24:02](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=1442): For mobile, use proper techniques and clean topology, and plan a mobile-friendly version alongside a custom LOD system for large maps.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [29:16](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=1756): For racing, use a server-authoritative networking model with full control of physics and car positions for smoothness.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [25:54](https://youtu.be/V1uFFUUCLpo?t=1554): Roblox pre-warms servers for anticipated join spikes; plan for crowd joins, and note a join queue now lets players wait for space.
- 2023-09-24 VuukStudios [12:50](https://youtu.be/dcJtZnan7i8?t=770): Detect movement via Humanoid.Running (speed > 0) rather than checking root part magnitude; the latter failed in testing.
- 2023-09-30 SimTek Game Development [5:36](https://youtu.be/6sgx1elbhwU?t=336): Check each part's orientation indicator (Show Orientation Indicator) before using CFrame.UpVector; a rotated part will make the elevator move in the wrong direction.
- 2023-10-20 B Ricey [0:53](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=53): Every recursive function needs an explicit base case (an if-return at the top) or it recurses forever and causes a stack overflow.
- 2023-10-20 B Ricey [6:51](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=411): Use a depth counter with a MAX_DEPTH constant when recursing over branching structures, since fractal-style recursion is otherwise infinite.
- 2023-10-20 B Ricey [16:19](https://youtu.be/T2V_NiMP5dk?t=979): Clone the template part per branch and parent it to Workspace; anchor parts so the generated structure stays fixed.
- 2023-10-30 XTB7 [4:31](https://youtu.be/o0S_8fFCsfA?t=271): Do not store sensitive data (e.g. currency) in teleport settings; they are client-accessible and can be manipulated by exploiters. Validate on the server.
- 2023-10-31 XTB7 [8:26](https://youtu.be/EpF5oWNS-Pk?t=506): Wrap all MemoryStore async calls in pcall in production; they fail more often than DataStore calls.
- 2023-11-17 TheDankDeveloper [2:21](https://youtu.be/fqMj_b2m-Ik?t=141): Study open-source or uncopylocked games in Studio: read their scripts to see how features are built, as a learning method.
- 2023-11-19 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983): Prevent duplicate queue entries by setting and checking a player attribute (e.g. IsInQueue) before adding them to the queue.
- 2023-11-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-luau-buffer-type-beta/2724894): Use buffers instead of string.unpack or large number arrays for compact binary data; buffers are faster to read and cheaper for GC.
- 2023-11-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-luau-buffer-type-beta/2724894): Use 0-based offsets with buffer read/write functions, and scale floats to integers (e.g. degrees*100 in i16) to save bytes.
- 2023-12-12 TheDankDeveloper [0:00](https://youtu.be/1jRaSYhrEcU?t=0): Building all player values on the client makes them easy to exploit; move value and currency logic to the server for a real game.
- 2023-12-16 ByteBlox [11:58](https://youtu.be/KbW_p_83hgQ?t=718): Server Scripts only see the server's workspace; if a part exists only on a client, FindFirstChild/direct access fails. Use LocalScripts for client-only objects.
- 2023-12-17 ByteBlox [0:00](https://youtu.be/9YwYDuIuih4?t=0): Use breakpoints instead of print statements for debugging; prints only show what you anticipated and require editing code to change what you check.
- 2023-12-30 ItsZyonic [2:09](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=129): Raise rendering settings only as needed; oversized assets and maxed rendering made Studio laggy and unresponsive.
- 2023-12-30 ItsZyonic [10:09](https://youtu.be/C1wSHlu8vVM?t=609): Add a debounce to slap/ability remotes so actions cannot be spammed; the speaker noted this was needed.
- 2023-12-30 SimTek Game Development [1:16](https://youtu.be/eDTbzVDm1aY?t=76): Use a Beam between two Attachments on the head to visualize raycasts during development; raycasts themselves are invisible.
- 2023-12-30 SimTek Game Development [12:45](https://youtu.be/eDTbzVDm1aY?t=765): Build RaycastParams with FilterType Exclude and add the character's descendants to FilterDescendantsInstances so the character doesn't block its own ray.
- 2023-12-30 SimTek Game Development [16:56](https://youtu.be/eDTbzVDm1aY?t=1016): Tag objects with CollectionService (e.g. 'exclude Ray') and loop over tagged instances to fill the raycast filter table.
- 2023-12-30 SimTek Game Development [21:14](https://youtu.be/eDTbzVDm1aY?t=1274): When a raycast hits an accessory handle, add it to the filter list and recast immediately so hats don't block headshots.
- 2023-12-30 SimTek Game Development [23:58](https://youtu.be/eDTbzVDm1aY?t=1438): Sweep rays in nested loops (5-degree steps across a ±45° horizontal and ±15° vertical range) to scan a cone; convert degrees to radians with math.rad.
- 2023-12-30 SimTek Game Development [24:39](https://youtu.be/eDTbzVDm1aY?t=1479): Expect a sweep scan to be resource intensive; sample a few random points inside the cone instead of a full grid when performance matters.
- 2024-01-05 ByteBlox [1:18](https://youtu.be/yKIZWvhabmA?t=78): Put click-to-raycast logic in a LocalScript (StarterPlayerScripts) rather than sending mouse data to the server via RemoteEvent; it is more efficient.
- 2024-01-05 ByteBlox [4:13](https://youtu.be/yKIZWvhabmA?t=253): Use UserInputService:GetMouseLocation() and Camera:ViewportPointToRay(x, y) to build the ray from camera through the mouse.
- 2024-01-05 ByteBlox [5:22](https://youtu.be/yKIZWvhabmA?t=322): Use RaycastParams with FilterType Exclude and FilteredDescendantsInstances set to the player's Character so the ray passes through the player.
- 2024-01-05 ByteBlox [9:20](https://youtu.be/yKIZWvhabmA?t=560): Multiply the ray direction by a length (e.g. 10 studs for melee, a large number for near-infinite) to control raycast range.
- 2024-01-05 ByteBlox [10:06](https://youtu.be/yKIZWvhabmA?t=606): Check the gameProcessed flag in InputBegan and return early if true so clicks on chat or UI do not fire weapons.
- 2024-01-05 ByteBlox [13:36](https://youtu.be/yKIZWvhabmA?t=816): Read RaycastResult.Instance, .Position, .Material, .Normal and .Distance; Instance is nil when the ray hits nothing (e.g. sky).
- 2024-01-05 ByteBlox [17:16](https://youtu.be/yKIZWvhabmA?t=1036): Raycasts are cheap; the speaker heard a game can fire around 6,000 raycasts per frame without issue (unverified claim).
- 2024-01-06 B Ricey [5:34](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=334): Raycast with workspace:Raycast from the camera ray and exclude the player's character via CastParams AddToFilter.
- 2024-01-07 Suphi Kaner [9:53](https://youtu.be/A14qFAyFYhQ?t=593): Clear per-player tables once they are empty so no data lingers and memory doesn't leak.
- 2024-01-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lets-teach-an-ai-to-catch-exploiters/2783460): Round Vector3 values to 2 decimals before storing to shrink the saved data size.
- 2024-01-18 Crusherfire [0:41](https://youtu.be/7tzy1DuPcBQ?t=41): Don't use WaitForChild on server scripts; you almost never need it there.
- 2024-01-18 Crusherfire [6:29](https://youtu.be/7tzy1DuPcBQ?t=389): Don't write GUI:WaitForChild('Frame') for children of a ScreenGui in StarterGui; index them directly.
- 2024-01-18 Crusherfire [12:15](https://youtu.be/7tzy1DuPcBQ?t=735): Remove 'if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait()' checks at the top of LocalScripts in StarterPlayerScripts/StarterGui; they are unnecessary.
- 2024-01-20 B Ricey [5:16](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=316): Put shared snapping logic in a ModuleScript in ReplicatedStorage so client preview and server validation use identical math.
- 2024-01-20 B Ricey [5:16](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=316): Use floor division (//) on Luau numbers to snap to grid instead of divide-then-math.floor; handles negatives correctly.
- 2024-01-20 B Ricey [5:57](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=357): Store GRID_SIZE as a single constant matching your block size so snapping logic can be changed in one place.
- 2024-01-20 B Ricey [17:18](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=1038): Use math.sign on the relative snapped component to pick the placement direction, with a negative flip, so one code path handles all wall faces.
- 2024-01-23 Crusherfire [12:20](https://youtu.be/xz_G2n1Q8-U?t=740): For cleanup that needs the instance still in the world, do it before triggering the removal rather than in a removing/destroying callback.
- 2024-02-01 Crusherfire [0:00](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=0): Use Parallel Luau only for heavy, frame-time-eating work (AI pathfinding, raycasting, terrain generation); skip it if your game already runs fine serially.
- 2024-02-01 Crusherfire [11:10](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=670): Heartbeat/RenderStepped connections run serially on one worker thread; many scripts connected to them stack up and inflate frame time.
- 2024-02-01 Crusherfire [16:46](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=1006): Put scripts that should run in parallel under an Actor instance; scripts in the same Actor still share one thread, so split workloads across multiple Actors.
- 2024-02-01 Crusherfire [17:21](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=1041): Replace :Connect with :ConnectParallel on the signal to run the callback in parallel immediately upon firing.
- 2024-02-01 Crusherfire [22:47](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=1367): Check each API member's thread-safety level in the docs; most instance properties are read-only in parallel, so wrap writes like CFrame or MoveTo in task.synchronize().
- 2024-02-01 Crusherfire [24:51](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=1491): Converting 72 AI deer from serial to parallel Luau (with task.synchronize for writes) dropped average frame time from about 50ms to about 16ms.
- 2024-02-01 Crusherfire [31:28](https://youtu.be/MyM3JkHEPmA?t=1888): BindableEvent:Fire is read-and-write safe, so it can be called from parallel code to communicate between threads without errors.
- 2024-02-10 SimTek Game Development [6:02](https://youtu.be/V65oLE0Wbuc?t=362): Enable 'Allow HTTP Requests' in Game Settings > Security before using HttpService; Studio blocks external calls otherwise.
- 2024-02-10 SimTek Game Development [9:18](https://youtu.be/V65oLE0Wbuc?t=558): HttpService cannot call Roblox API domains directly from Studio; test endpoints with curl to confirm data is reachable.
- 2024-02-10 SimTek Game Development [17:39](https://youtu.be/V65oLE0Wbuc?t=1059): Public proxies may go down or get shut down; do not rely on them in production games.
- 2024-02-17 B Ricey [12:13](https://youtu.be/FaEMgVDlaNQ?t=733): Use table.remove(list) to pop and return the last element in one call when lending out pooled resources.
- 2024-02-19 SimTek Game Development [5:48](https://youtu.be/q-kUXooTRd8?t=348): If printing a table shows only a table address, turn off Output log mode (the three-dot menu) to see the contents.
- 2024-02-26 ByteBlox [6:06](https://youtu.be/VitH_k_-AZU?t=366): Unreliable events save performance because the engine doesn't track delivery; reserve them for event spam where dropped messages are harmless.
- 2024-02-28 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/discovery-on-roblox-past-present-and-future-vision/2859111): Keep join times low: server pre-warming makes ~70% of joins that would have waited instant, improving conversion from recommendations.
- 2024-03-02 B Ricey [11:09](https://youtu.be/SVBmZ3yIMRo?t=669): Store bind and action names in constants so the same string is used to bind and unbind, and typos are caught by the compiler.
- 2024-03-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/emilios-ragdoll-module-easy-ahh-ragdolls-r15-and-r6/2865426): Wrap Touched handlers in a debounce flag and check Humanoid.Health > 0 before ragdolling to avoid repeated triggers.
- 2024-03-08 janisjanis01 [1:24](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=84): Run each tagged part's animation in its own function or thread; a single loop may animate only the first part.
- 2024-03-08 janisjanis01 [2:46](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=166): Check that parts exist in Workspace before animating them. Procedurally generated parts may not be there yet, so the script silently does nothing.
- 2024-03-08 janisjanis01 [3:29](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=209): Keep a table of parts already animating so the same part doesn't get multiple loops, and add a cooldown to avoid lag.
- 2024-03-08 janisjanis01 [3:29](https://youtu.be/IBswE0CyjA8?t=209): Use CollectionService tags to find all parts that need spinning or moving instead of hardcoding references.
- 2024-03-09 SimTek Game Development [13:58](https://youtu.be/H7sk_T1zrD8?t=838): Lowercase the word list and the incoming message (string.lower) so capitalization doesn't cause missed matches.
- 2024-03-09 SimTek Game Development [16:03](https://youtu.be/H7sk_T1zrD8?t=963): Check string.match result with truthiness (nil means not found) rather than treating it as a boolean.
- 2024-03-09 SimTek Game Development [18:50](https://youtu.be/H7sk_T1zrD8?t=1130): Validate chat keywords on the server and fire the result to all clients via RemoteEvent, keeping game logic server-authoritative.
- 2024-03-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/howto-physics-based-tweenservice/2873727): Step the platform on RunService:BindToRenderStep at priority 99 (RenderStepped), not Heartbeat, so physics solves with the new velocities that frame.
- 2024-03-27 Crusherfire [1:38](https://youtu.be/kDCLGPMpLl0?t=98): Prompt purchases with MarketplaceService:PromptGamePassPurchase(player, gamePassId) from a LocalScript triggered by a button click.
- 2024-03-29 janisjanis01 [4:08](https://youtu.be/9gBS7nhaC3c?t=248): When AI-generated scripts move models incorrectly, fix it manually: use a union and set the part's position and rotation directly.
- 2024-03-30 B Ricey [14:20](https://youtu.be/1XJRZ1x2x4g?t=860): Use OverlapParams with an include filter on plot objects and workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox to detect collisions, ignoring players and debris.
- 2024-03-30 ByteBlox [0:51](https://youtu.be/lJ_ZwfNHkQU?t=51): Detect players via other.Parent:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') before acting on touched parts, so non-character collisions are ignored.
- 2024-03-30 ByteBlox [2:12](https://youtu.be/lJ_ZwfNHkQU?t=132): When parenting an Explosion to a character, set its Position to HumanoidRootPart.Position, since parenting alone doesn't place it there.
- 2024-04-05 ByteBlox [25:38](https://youtu.be/cWfsm8rwgDg?t=1538): TweenService only animates numeric properties (position, size, transparency, color); it cannot tween strings like material.
- 2024-04-06 ByteBlox [2:05](https://youtu.be/7NRp4BEYrsQ?t=125): Call MarketplaceService:PromptSubscriptionPurchase from a server Script, passing the player and subscription ID.
- 2024-04-14 ByteBlox [5:34](https://youtu.be/RWl6zQYanOA?t=334): Build instances fully (name, value, parent) before parenting the folder to the Player so everything appears at once.
- 2024-04-14 ByteBlox [6:57](https://youtu.be/RWl6zQYanOA?t=417): Use WaitForChild when fetching parts or click detectors so the script does not error if the object has not loaded yet.
- 2024-04-23 Stewiepfing [10:50](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=650): Spawn popup orbs on the client with a LocalScript so only the player sees them; setting the count too high can crash the game.
- 2024-04-23 Stewiepfing [5:15](https://youtu.be/qMdRT1xf9GM?t=315): Run each orb in its own task.spawn thread so the loop doesn't block.
- 2024-05-10 ByteBlox [0:48](https://youtu.be/D6DwmHcmwwQ?t=48): Parent cloned or spawned instances into a dedicated folder (e.g. spawnedParts) rather than directly into Workspace so they are easy to track and clean up.
- 2024-05-10 ByteBlox [2:10](https://youtu.be/D6DwmHcmwwQ?t=130): Destroy spawned instances after a timeout (e.g. Destroy after 3 seconds) so the count stays steady instead of growing every frame.
- 2024-05-10 ByteBlox [4:35](https://youtu.be/D6DwmHcmwwQ?t=275): Prevent leaks by always pairing creation with cleanup: every time you create an instance, make sure its destruction path exists.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/viewportframe-masking/2964839): ViewportFrames do not support EditableMeshes, so per-player masks cannot be created dynamically; force a fixed rig such as a blocky character instead.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543): Use useMemo to cache expensive computations, with the input values listed as dependencies so they recompute only on change.
- 2024-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543): Never mutate state tables in place; build a new table (or use join from Cryo/Dash) and pass it to the setter, since reference equality drives re-renders.
- 2024-05-11 ByteBlox [1:37](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=97): Spawn test objects with a loop (task.wait 0.1) rather than manual clicks, but remove or raise the wait if it causes lag; spawning every frame clogs Workspace.
- 2024-05-11 ByteBlox [2:17](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=137): Parent spawned clones to a dedicated folder (e.g. spawnedParts) instead of Workspace root to keep Workspace organised.
- 2024-05-11 ByteBlox [7:53](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=473): Return immediately once the cumulative weight reaches the random roll so the loop exits early instead of checking every rarity.
- 2024-05-11 ByteBlox [9:54](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=594): Do not assume pairs() iterates in insertion order; table iteration is unordered, so the weighted selection must not depend on order when the rarities are read.
- 2024-05-11 ByteBlox [10:35](https://youtu.be/UUgj-hmX2LM?t=635): Pick the random roll first and then accumulate weights; this avoids biased results from order-dependent comparisons.
- 2024-05-18 Crusherfire [2:45](https://youtu.be/OksI7HpEd6w?t=165): For distance queries over thousands of objects, use a spatial structure (octree) instead of looping all objects and checking distance each time.
- 2024-05-18 Crusherfire [2:45](https://youtu.be/OksI7HpEd6w?t=165): Use Quenty's octree module (Octree/radiusSearch API) rather than writing your own spatial partitioning from scratch.
- 2024-05-18 Crusherfire [3:27](https://youtu.be/OksI7HpEd6w?t=207): Tag objects with CollectionService and collect them with GetTagged to bulk-insert them into the octree at startup.
