- 2024-05-18 Crusherfire [5:30](https://youtu.be/OksI7HpEd6w?t=330): Benchmark with os.clock/tick start and end timestamps to compare approaches; measure execution time excluding any intentional delays.
- 2024-05-22 Crusherfire [4:46](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=286): Wrap only the code that actually yields or can error (async calls like HasBadgeAsync) in pcall, not unrelated lines.
- 2024-05-22 Crusherfire [5:26](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=326): Set humanoid health via player.Character and a direct humanoid reference instead of wrapping simple property sets in pcall.
- 2024-05-22 Crusherfire [6:48](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=408): Define named handler functions once and connect them, rather than creating a new lambda per player/character, to save memory.
- 2024-05-22 Crusherfire [7:29](https://youtu.be/NsPL5yDY5cE?t=449): Avoid WaitForChild in server scripts when the child is already guaranteed to exist; index it directly.
- 2024-05-28 Stewiepfing [6:53](https://youtu.be/0gOLJ4-ea8w?t=413): Use ConnectParallel on RunService events to split demanding work like terrain generation across CPU cores; task.spawn and coroutines do not run in parallel.
- 2024-05-28 Stewiepfing [7:34](https://youtu.be/0gOLJ4-ea8w?t=454): ConnectParallel requires the script to be inside an Actor; call task.desynchronize inside parallel code to switch to parallel execution.
- 2024-05-28 Stewiepfing [8:15](https://youtu.be/0gOLJ4-ea8w?t=495): Learn Actors, the Microprofiler, and how threads map to CPU cores before experimenting with parallel Luau.
- 2024-05-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-health-and-damage/2994963): Do all damage and status calculations in a server Script; ModuleScript state does not replicate between client and server, so keep authoritative stats on the server.
- 2024-05-30 Crusherfire [2:10](https://youtu.be/yncfwrBAWVw?t=130): Use Studio's native Go To Declaration (Ctrl+click) on functions; it works across modules, but not on require() lines for ModuleScripts.
- 2024-05-30 Crusherfire [3:32](https://youtu.be/yncfwrBAWVw?t=212): Write require paths as literal full paths (e.g. ReplicatedStorage.Modules.X); the plugin cannot resolve paths built through intermediate variables.
- 2024-06-02 AlvinBlox [2:42](https://youtu.be/paRHxtuk_Mc?t=162): Use GetPropertyChangedSignal for a single property instead of .Changed, since each listener adds processing cost every time any property changes.
- 2024-06-03 PineappleDev [5:12](https://youtu.be/BOMfj0bSjf0?t=312): Check that the data table value exists before assigning it to a leaderstat value to avoid nil errors for new players.
- 2024-06-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930): GetPartsInBox supports varied shapes but costs more than magnitude checks; use it for wider or box-shaped attacks.
- 2024-06-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930): Raycast between last-frame and current positions of attachments on the weapon for precise swings; note it requires animation-tied sizing.
- 2024-06-04 AlvinBlox [2:32](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=152): Process purchases on the server in MarketplaceService.ProcessReceipt because server handling is authoritative and cannot be exploited by clients.
- 2024-06-04 AlvinBlox [4:31](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=271): Return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted only after delivering goods; return NotProcessedYet if the player is missing so the purchase is retried next join.
- 2024-06-04 AlvinBlox [7:17](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=437): Check the player exists with Players:GetPlayerByUserId before granting items, since they may have left mid-purchase.
- 2024-06-04 AlvinBlox [8:36](https://youtu.be/5YNFRCD0pd0?t=516): Store one handler function per product ID in a table and dispatch by ID, so adding products means adding a table entry instead of more if/elseif branches.
- 2024-06-07 ByteBlox [7:43](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=463): Set a model's PrimaryPart and use PivotTo to position cloned rooms, rather than moving each part manually.
- 2024-06-07 ByteBlox [10:37](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=637): Store Touched connections in a variable and disconnect them after the first trigger to avoid duplicate room spawns.
- 2024-06-07 ByteBlox [10:37](https://youtu.be/s2XUzTyCdK4?t=637): Check for a Humanoid on the touching part's parent so only players, not arbitrary parts, trigger room generation.
- 2024-06-08 AlvinBlox [3:52](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=232): Wrap progress updates in a function like updateProgressBar(percent) for cleaner code, though inlining the size assignment also works.
- 2024-06-08 AlvinBlox [3:52](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=232): Drive the bar from leaderstat GetPropertyChangedSignal('Value') so it updates automatically when the stat changes.
- 2024-06-08 AlvinBlox [4:40](https://youtu.be/-Da8-Sv54_Q?t=280): Clamp the ratio with math.clamp(n, 0, 1) so the fill never overflows the bar when the value exceeds the target.
- 2024-06-09 AlvinBlox [5:38](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=338): Add a task.wait() delay after CharacterAdded before parenting, since the head may not exist yet and the GUI can fail to load.
- 2024-06-10 ByteBlox [2:53](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=173): Use Players' Humanoid HealthChanged via GetPropertyChangedSignal('Health') to react to damage. Use GetDescendants() instead of GetChildren() when you need nested parts.
- 2024-06-10 ByteBlox [4:56](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=296): Reset Humanoid.Health to MaxHealth inside the health-changed handler, with an early return when the pool is depleted. Health events fire before the death check, so this keeps characters alive while the pool drains.
- 2024-06-10 ByteBlox [14:01](https://youtu.be/_rksdF5JoHo?t=841): Keep Studio scripts small and test each change in play mode; the speaker shows that client and server behave differently for the same code.
- 2024-06-19 Roox4 [2:44](https://youtu.be/DPpAsjUr278?t=164): Set IK target parts to CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery off and Massless on; weld raycast parts to HumanoidRootPart and keep them unanchored.
- 2024-06-19 Roox4 [4:35](https://youtu.be/DPpAsjUr278?t=275): Use a raycast params filter of Exclude with the whole model so leg raycasts don't hit the character itself.
- 2024-06-23 janisjanis01 [14:35](https://youtu.be/e725ByFD8EI?t=875): Set a server player count limit before launch; the creator forgot it and servers were overwhelmed at around 300 players.
- 2024-06-24 ByteBlox [4:19](https://youtu.be/ekM2z9FMhZc?t=259): Clone a template from ReplicatedStorage and scale values per tier (e.g. cost x5, cash x2) in a loop to generate many upgrade items quickly.
- 2024-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332): Move non-physics projectiles in a client-side RenderStepped loop for smoothness; use one shared connection for all active projectiles instead of one per projectile.
- 2024-06-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332): Run hit detection on an interval (e.g. every 0.1s) rather than every frame to save cost.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [6:49](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=409): Check that the Humanoid exists before using it, and wait for CharacterAdded, to avoid errors in speed potion scripts.
- 2024-07-25 Roox4 [2:42](https://youtu.be/OYfrdTxNdjA?t=162): When offsetting viewmodels/cframes, check whether offsets are in object space or world space; mixing them broke the scope alignment until made relative to the camera.
- 2024-08-06 Suphi Kaner [21:52](https://youtu.be/DTEraIOfoS0?t=1312): Roblox signals now defer by default; mirror that in custom event classes with task.defer, since spawn/direct calls break when connecting or disconnecting mid-fire.
- 2024-08-06 Suphi Kaner [1:23:19](https://youtu.be/DTEraIOfoS0?t=4999): Recycle coroutine threads: keep finished threads in a pool and resume them with task.spawn to avoid creating a new thread per event fire.
- 2024-08-06 Suphi Kaner [1:41:16](https://youtu.be/DTEraIOfoS0?t=6076): Wrap a yielded thread's resume args with a function-call helper so varargs pass through without table.pack/unpack overhead.
- 2024-08-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400): Do not use tick() as the Z argument of math.noise; Roblox clamps Z to 0-1, so use a Random-generated seed offset instead.
- 2024-08-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400): Generate offsets with Random.new(Seed):NextNumber(-100000, 100000); the author says larger offsets distort the terrain.
- 2024-08-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400): Clamp the math.noise result to 0-1 but multiply by Amplitude afterward; clamping after amplitude removes all hills.
- 2024-08-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400): Add a task.wait() per outer loop iteration to avoid crashing Studio when generating large maps.
- 2024-08-22 PineappleDev [8:21](https://youtu.be/5wudgfIoTwo?t=501): Add a debounce flag to touch handlers so a player touching a hitbox doesn't join the queue multiple times.
- 2024-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934): Use immutable cached data and autosaves to avoid bugs from commits happening too early or accidental mutations.
- 2024-09-05 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934): Inject a MockDataStore for testing data code without hitting live DataStores.
- 2024-09-06 BubuTheDev [2:45](https://youtu.be/oYQ5jIZKE8s?t=165): Add a short wait (about 0.25s) before the death fade so it doesn't play instantly on death.
- 2024-09-06 BubuTheDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/oYQ5jIZKE8s?t=0): Cache a service-level local for character debugging: store Humanoid via character:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid') and check it exists before connecting Died.
- 2024-09-08 BubuTheDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/zc7vWpLOejE?t=41): Count frames in RunService.RenderStepped and update the label once per second (update rate = 1) instead of every frame to keep the readout stable and cheap.
- 2024-09-19 GnomeCode [1:15](https://youtu.be/oEWoyT-GBZY?t=75): Use ALL_CAPS names for constants like intermission and round time that you won't change at runtime.
- 2024-09-19 GnomeCode [7:57](https://youtu.be/oEWoyT-GBZY?t=477): Skip characters that haven't loaded yet (player.Character nil) when teleporting, rather than erroring.
- 2024-09-20 GnomeCode [10:19](https://youtu.be/SUZPuQrKJuU?t=619): Handle no-vote and invalid-name cases by falling back to a random map; a winner of nil must not be cloned from ReplicatedStorage.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [17:07](https://youtu.be/DeWXxpnoe4U?t=1027): Use the get image for key code API to show the correct PlayStation or Xbox button icons; use haptic feedback APIs for immersion.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [14:02](https://youtu.be/LMUQpm0HpEQ?t=842): Use audio API to customize voice: toggle push-to-talk via audio device input muted property, or replace default 3D audio with direct device routing for team voice.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [10:52](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=652): Optimize for low-end Android devices: Android dominates most markets outside Japan. Keep a budget Android device to test updates.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [27:19](https://youtu.be/T7w3Q54JOPE?t=1639): Give the team a budget for low-end Android and iPhones and test on-device; hide models instead of reparenting them for load performance (Clipit tip).
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [5:59](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=359): Budget in-camera scene triangles, not total triangles: aim for about 500k on phone targets. Check with Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+F2 rendering stats.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [10:39](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=639): Keep scene draw calls around 500 for phone targets; every draw call has a fixed cost even with one triangle. Subtract UI draw calls from the budget.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [12:43](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=763): Reuse the same MeshId and material to batch draw calls. Color3 and SurfaceAppearance property changes batch well; transparency, decals and textures add draw calls.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [18:56](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1136): Use transparent and decal-heavy elements sparingly; they break batching and raise draw calls. Re-check the scene draw-call budget as you build.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [21:29](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1289): Test CPU on real low-end phones: a 10 ms PC frame can become ~40 ms on phone. Target under 16.66 ms per frame for 60 FPS.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [25:25](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1525): Spread raycasts and heavy loops across frames; use the Microprofiler (Ctrl/Cmd+F6) to find script hotspots.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [27:36](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1656): Target 2 GB devices. Ejections rise around 66% of device memory (~1.3 GB client memory). Check with F9 on device, not in Studio.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [30:49](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=1849): Limit instances sent to the client with StreamingEnabled; each part costs roughly 1 KB of client memory.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [34:15](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=2055): Limit server-owned moving assemblies to about 50 at a time (~1 KB/s each). Watch the recv stat (Ctrl/Cmd+F7).
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [20:46](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1246): Don't quadruple lights for global illumination; add a few bounce lights in key spots, since too many lights hurt optimization and can crash the game.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [31:19](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=1879): Add lights as needed, then toggle them off and on to check which are actually necessary; use the lighting editor plugin by woot3 to audit light count and radius.
- 2024-09-24 ByteBlox [10:02](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=602): Use a loop over the parts folder to connect Touched events instead of wiring each part manually; name-independent and scales when parts are added.
- 2024-09-24 ByteBlox [14:09](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=849): Filter Touched events by checking for a Humanoid in the hit's parent, and debounce with a flag so one touch doesn't fire repeatedly.
- 2024-09-24 ByteBlox [15:34](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=934): Store per-part state (e.g. an IsCleared attribute) on the part so the progress and highlight logic can check it without extra variables.
- 2024-09-24 ByteBlox [19:18](https://youtu.be/Issrodr_wK8?t=1158): Run per-player animations on the client via a RemoteEvent, not on the server, so each player sees their own state.
- 2024-10-10 Suphi Kaner [7:01](https://youtu.be/ZPviBfpPloE?t=421): Add an animation event (e.g. 'pause') in the Animation Editor and connect GetMarkerReachedSignal to control timing from script; set AnimationTrack.Speed to 0 to pause.
- 2024-10-12 ByteBlox [13:03](https://youtu.be/MmYAacyHaSw?t=783): When moving scripts to ServerScriptService, replace script.Parent references with explicit paths like workspace.DoorName.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [7:45](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=465): Set track speed to 1 / desired seconds (e.g. 1/10 for a 10-second cycle) so the timing is easy to tune.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [18:57](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=1137): A paused track and a stopped track cost the same CPU (~6 ms in the test), so pausing is not a performance optimisation.
- 2024-10-13 Suphi Kaner [46:07](https://youtu.be/AkF-4x7qfzM?t=2767): Client-side animation logic can be moved to the server if you need authoritative door state; the client version is shown for lower cost.
- 2024-10-18 Crusherfire [0:35](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=35): Use weak-key tables (__mode='k') as caches keyed by objects; entries are auto-removed when the key object is no longer strongly referenced, avoiding manual cleanup and leaks.
- 2024-10-18 Crusherfire [5:33](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=333): Don't store the weak key inside the table's values under a strong reference; a value referencing its own key keeps it alive. Use __mode='kv' to avoid this.
- 2024-10-18 Crusherfire [6:54](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=414): For per-entity caches (e.g. AI raycast params), key by the entity so cache entries are collected when the entity is destroyed.
- 2024-10-18 Crusherfire [10:22](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=622): Don't rely on weak tables to weakly hold Roblox Instances; the docs say instances are never weak, and behavior is inconsistent. Test before depending on it.
- 2024-10-18 Crusherfire [13:44](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=824): Workaround for instances: hold a strong reference in a table, and release it when the part's Parent changes to nil, disconnecting the connection.
- 2024-10-18 Crusherfire [18:23](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=1103): A closure must reference the object (e.g. in a Destroying listener) to keep it alive; otherwise it may be collected even when the weak table should hold it.
- 2024-10-18 Crusherfire [19:32](https://youtu.be/K6TryCAqFbs?t=1172): DescendantAdded on game is a possible way to track instances globally, but it adds a script execution per instance; be cautious on mobile with large batches.
- 2024-10-24 ByteBlox [5:43](https://youtu.be/dd9QvPp6HQI?t=343): Rebuild ViewportFrame children each frame in a task.wait() loop, clearing old children except UIGradients, and set view.CurrentCamera to workspace.CurrentCamera.
- 2024-10-24 ByteBlox [13:24](https://youtu.be/dd9QvPp6HQI?t=804): Update the cloned character at 0.05-0.1 second intervals to produce a natural low-frame-rate look instead of animation tricks.
- 2024-11-01 Roox4 [1:20](https://youtu.be/LC8AV8Yy6Z4?t=80): Humanoid can override applied forces (rocket jump momentum bled away mid-air). If physics-driven movement matters, build a custom character controller instead.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [13:22](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=802): Don't attach touch events to too many parts; use one buffer part per wall to limit how often the event fires.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [5:55](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=355): Use CollectionService tags (e.g. 'solid') to mark bounceable objects, so one script handles all tagged parts instead of per-object code.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [16:46](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=1006): Use a flag instance on the HumanoidRootPart plus Debris:AddItem to debounce repeated touch events so forces aren't applied multiple times.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [18:50](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=1130): Set VectorForce and AngularVelocity RelativeTo Attachment0 on the HumanoidRootPart so the push direction follows the character, not world orientation.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [20:13](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=1213): Destroy VectorForce and AngularVelocity constraints after ~0.1s with Debris so the forces are short impulses, not continuous.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [22:52](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=1372): Tune knockback by adjusting VectorForce Z magnitude (thrust) and AngularVelocity X (spin) values; the video used 10,000 force and 50 spin for stronger effect.
- 2024-11-02 SimTek Game Development [11:30](https://youtu.be/zimfV0QYV2w?t=690): Connect CharacterAdded per player so touch detection is reapplied after respawns, not just on first join.
- 2024-11-03 ByteBlox [4:02](https://youtu.be/4yIjd9Xn9sE?t=242): Guard currency deductions with a check (if cash < 1 then return) to prevent negative balances.
- 2024-11-03 ByteBlox [11:58](https://youtu.be/4yIjd9Xn9sE?t=718): Use task.spawn for each dropped item so all drops appear at once and despawn together, instead of a loop that waits per item.
- 2024-11-13 Roblox Creators [1:00](https://youtu.be/qfWKYgO63OI?t=60): Set up a RemoteEvent in ReplicatedStorage so the server can notify the client when a player joins via a referral link.
- 2024-11-17 ByteBlox [20:06](https://youtu.be/1KKQ6YrktTk?t=1206): Detect platform with UserInputService: KeyboardEnabled, MouseEnabled, TouchEnabled combinations identify PC vs mobile.
- 2024-11-20 ByteBlox [4:46](https://youtu.be/Jl0SdXksQsI?t=286): Check the isPurchased flag from PromptProductFinished and return early if false. Players can close the purchase prompt without buying.
- 2024-11-20 ByteBlox [14:20](https://youtu.be/Jl0SdXksQsI?t=860): Reset camera type to Custom at script start and after a failed purchase. Otherwise a rejected prompt can leave the camera stuck in Scriptable forever.
- 2024-11-27 Possible Panda [6:30](https://youtu.be/OxGa3kVcfMY?t=390): Put server-side scripts in ServerScriptService; they run automatically when the game starts.
- 2024-11-28 Suphi Kaner [46:24](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=2784): Prefer clone inheritance over metatable inheritance; deep __index chains hurt function-call performance, per Roblox's fast method calls guidance.
- 2024-11-28 Suphi Kaner [1:12:12](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=4332): Use thread recycling (Global.spawn / Fred) instead of task.defer; the speaker claims about 2x performance versus creating new threads each time.
- 2024-12-06 Roox4 [6:14](https://youtu.be/_E1stqb2fNw?t=374): Raycasts have a 15,000-stud max range; at extreme distances, chain multiple raycasts to hit nearby targets.
- 2025-01-05 ByteBlox [11:23](https://youtu.be/K-KvVwt_Y4I?t=683): Tweak Atmosphere Density in small increments (about 0.05 per step) for subtle fog build-up instead of large jumps that look obvious.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Replace every wait() with task.wait(); wait() is deprecated and can be unoptimized.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Avoid while true do loops; use event-driven code unless no other option exists.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Use one central script in ServerScriptService to control many similar parts (e.g. streetlights) instead of one script per part.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Keep the number of Heartbeat connections as low as possible.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Skip FindFirstChild when the child is guaranteed to exist.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Do not use anti-lag scripts; the guide says they do the opposite of what they claim.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Set all RaycastFilterTypes to Include or Exclude rather than deprecated options.
- 2025-01-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345): Store rarely client-needed assets in ServerStorage instead of ReplicatedStorage to reduce client load.
- 2025-01-17 Crusherfire [4:18](https://youtu.be/yevAvHU3ewo?t=258): In signal modules, check coroutine.status(thread) == "suspended" before task.spawn; otherwise a cancelled waiting thread throws 'cannot spawn nonsuspended coroutine with arguments'.
- 2025-01-22 ByteBlox [16:34](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=994): Use RunService.RenderStepped to read workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame every frame, then move a tiny invisible anchored part to the camera to detect camera-in-volume.
- 2025-01-22 ByteBlox [19:58](https://youtu.be/29B9AV_xM_k?t=1198): Touched doesn't fire between two anchored parts; use workspace:GetPartsInPart on a small probe part to detect overlap instead.
- 2025-01-24 PineappleDev [5:09](https://youtu.be/2HRX5AqBfNA?t=309): Put the viewmodel controller in a LocalScript under StarterCharacterScripts so it runs on each spawn and only handles the local player.
- 2025-01-24 PineappleDev [5:52](https://youtu.be/2HRX5AqBfNA?t=352): Clone the viewmodel into workspace.CurrentCamera so only the local player sees it, and start it below view to fake pulling the item out of a pocket.
- 2025-01-24 PineappleDev [7:13](https://youtu.be/2HRX5AqBfNA?t=433): Clean up on unequip by destroying the cloned viewmodel and clearing the reference, so no stale viewmodels remain on the camera.
- 2025-01-24 ByteBlox [7:11](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=431): Don't assume AI-generated code works; test each system (coins, checkpoints) separately. AI script's coin listener only fired for future-added coins, so existing coins never worked.
- 2025-01-24 ByteBlox [12:28](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=748): Attach Touched handlers directly where objects are created instead of routing through a folder-added listener; simpler and avoids missed events.
- 2025-01-24 ByteBlox [13:09](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=789): Use Touched:Once-style connection (disconnect after first hit) so multiple body parts touching a pickup don't award it twice.
- 2025-01-24 ByteBlox [14:33](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=873): Set pickup CanCollide to false so players don't physically bump coins; they still fire Touched.
- 2025-01-27 ByteBlox [4:06](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=246): Use AlignPosition with two Attachments (player root, pet part) for smooth following instead of anchoring/teleporting the character.
- 2025-01-27 ByteBlox [7:07](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=427): Use TweenService on a hidden owner part's rotation to animate a parent model; attached followers move with it.
- 2025-01-27 ByteBlox [10:07](https://youtu.be/RkgxOf3iZfA?t=607): Spawn many collectibles by cloning one template from ReplicatedStorage in a loop with random positions, keeping Y fixed.
- 2025-01-29 JustDucksey [2:09](https://youtu.be/jKyzG9HPrRM?t=129): Roblox Touched events can be unreliable for pickups or tools; expect to debug them and consider alternative detection methods.
- 2025-01-31 ByteBlox [8:01](https://youtu.be/mpv1SyfcpGA?t=481): Build dialogue as a reusable say(message) function: clear the label, loop over message:split(""), append each letter with a short task.wait().
- 2025-02-03 ArTheDev [1:00](https://youtu.be/twXk5B5G5dA?t=60): Create leaderstats as a Folder under the Player and add IntValue/NumberValue children so the built-in leaderboard displays them.
- 2025-02-06 ArTheDev [4:34](https://youtu.be/JtZ8RzQaQco?t=274): On the server, check UserOwnsGamePassAsync first and only call PromptGamePassPurchase if the player doesn't own it, with a pcall to catch invalid IDs.
- 2025-02-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advanced-scripting-tutorial/3511460): Call :Destroy() on instances and :Disconnect() on connections, and clear all table references to them, so the garbage collector can free memory and avoid leaks.
- 2025-03-09 Suphi Kaner [0:40](https://youtu.be/BRYp2-rdpXQ?t=40): Motor6D Transform updates are batched in a parallel job before physics, so they are cheaper than many immediate C0/C1 changes.
- 2025-03-09 Suphi Kaner [9:38](https://youtu.be/BRYp2-rdpXQ?t=578): Use TweenService on Motor6D.Transform with a client Script for smooth door animation, and set Transform instantly on the server to save work.
- 2025-03-09 Suphi Kaner [18:34](https://youtu.be/BRYp2-rdpXQ?t=1114): Motor6D was roughly 2-3 ms faster in the author's test than BulkMoveTo with 2000-scale loops; benefit is small but real.
- 2025-03-20 ArTheDev [10:54](https://youtu.be/5QZA1_0L9PU?t=654): Store party membership as a player attribute holding the leader's name; this makes group checks simple loops over Players without a separate table.
- 2025-03-20 ArTheDev [12:15](https://youtu.be/5QZA1_0L9PU?t=735): On each membership change, delete and recreate all member UI templates rather than patching them; simpler and avoids ordering bugs.
- 2025-03-21 PineappleDev [2:29](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=149): Disable or delete the item spawning script if you don't want items spawning continuously; it just clones items into Workspace.
- 2025-03-21 PineappleDev [2:29](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=149): Tune InteractableHandler client values: range (pickup distance), carry distance, max carry distance (auto-drop when too far), and carry smoothness (lower = laggier, higher = snappier).
- 2025-03-22 Crusherfire [2:41](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=161): Use task.spawn to run a function immediately in a new thread so yielding calls (e.g. PlayerService async web requests) don't halt your main script.
- 2025-03-22 Crusherfire [12:38](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=758): Prefer task.defer over task.spawn when the work doesn't need to run immediately; it queues to the end of the current resumption cycle for predictable ordering.
- 2025-03-22 Crusherfire [17:49](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=1069): task.delay(0) inside Run service RenderStepped-style events runs at the next Heartbeat, which may be in the same frame; inside Heartbeat it runs next frame.
- 2025-03-22 Crusherfire [24:08](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=1448): task.wait() with no argument behaves like task.delay(0): it resumes on the next Heartbeat, so use it as a yield, not as a precise timer.
- 2025-03-22 Crusherfire [25:01](https://youtu.be/0jAZOpNHqv8?t=1501): Store the thread returned by task.defer/delay and call task.cancel on it when the work is no longer needed to prevent it running.
- 2025-03-22 CallMeDogey [12:11](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=731): Make weapon hitboxes generous so combat feels fair to players facing tough enemies.
- 2025-03-26 Suphi Kaner [9:19](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=559): Pick the smallest numeric type that fits your range (U8 over any/F16) since each type's byte size is set by its declared type; the any type adds an extra byte.
- 2025-03-26 Suphi Kaner [31:09](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=1869): Use the characters type for restricted-alphabet strings (6 bits/char for 64-char set vs 8 for string); use static type for predefined value sets (1 byte total).
- 2025-03-26 Suphi Kaner [36:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2202): Create custom static types for fixed error messages or enumerations so each send costs one byte.
- 2025-03-26 Suphi Kaner [44:28](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2668): Enable dynamic type checking only when not relying on strict mode; it gives clearer errors but costs runtime work.
- 2025-03-26 Suphi Kaner [49:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2982): Batch Packet fires into one buffer per frame: 1000 fires went from 14 KB (RemoteEvent) to 1 KB; batching is the largest saving.
- 2025-03-26 Suphi Kaner [49:42](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=2982): Don't batch unreliable events: they cap at 1000 bytes and lost batches drop many events; use Packet only for serialization/compression there.
- 2025-03-26 Suphi Kaner [1:08:49](https://youtu.be/WoIElUdj64A?t=4129): Combine batching with smaller types: array of 4 smallest C-frames sent 6x per frame gave 323 bytes vs 576 for RemoteEvents.
- 2025-03-29 PineappleDev [3:31](https://youtu.be/ntHgQBEVERE?t=211): Define per-item data by duplicating the test data module in the item data template; defaults are value 5, sellable true, usable false, fuel 5, so override only what differs.
- 2025-03-29 PineappleDev [4:55](https://youtu.be/ntHgQBEVERE?t=295): On the server, require the item data module, call setup with the item's data module and model, then clone the model into Workspace to spawn items.
- 2025-04-11 PineappleDev [1:25](https://youtu.be/kVtEBqvw5F4?t=85): Delete the plugin's default item setup script and replace it with the custom one so money bags and coal get set up correctly.
- 2025-04-11 PineappleDev [3:15](https://youtu.be/kVtEBqvw5F4?t=195): To sell an item, set its Sellable attribute/value to true and give it a value; sold items drop a money bag worth that value.
- 2025-04-11 PineappleDev [4:34](https://youtu.be/kVtEBqvw5F4?t=274): Buy-table purchase names must exactly match the item name in ReplicatedStorage, and the shop data entry key must match too.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [17:08](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=1028): Pick the smallest fixed-width type that fits the value range (u8, u16, etc.); an 8-bit int sends ~3 bytes vs ~9 bytes for a 64-bit number over remotes.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [17:49](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=1069): Buffers have no built-in cursor; manually track the byte offset and advance it by each value's byte width after every write/read.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [21:27](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=1287): Map 0-1 floats to 8-bit ints by multiplying by 255 and rounding, then divide by 255 on read; trades precision for size.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [8:00](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=480): Use writebits/readbits (0-32 bit width) to pack values smaller than a byte, such as 0-10, and several values per byte.
- 2025-04-19 Crusherfire [23:30](https://youtu.be/M2YKE07cUOc?t=1410): Serializing a Color3 into 3 bytes instead of 13 bytes over remotes cuts data by over half; only do this if the game's throughput truly needs it.
- 2025-04-27 Stewiepfing [0:19](https://youtu.be/e48wgHQll2Y?t=19): Scale UI strokes with viewport height: store thickness at 1080p baseline and set thickness = viewportSize.Y × (originalThickness/1080) so borders look the same on phones.
- 2025-04-27 Stewiepfing [4:30](https://youtu.be/e48wgHQll2Y?t=270): Use WaitForChild on required modules in replicated scripts; otherwise code runs before replication and errors on load.
- 2025-05-02 Duckable [4:19](https://youtu.be/Wc8wjiy1oYk?t=259): Don't be stuck on a fighting/weapon mechanic you haven't built before; rewrite the script from scratch if it stalls, as the speaker did for the slap pan.
- 2025-05-03 LovJak [1:12](https://youtu.be/M-o5hLZbjGY?t=72): Use WaitForChild() (e.g. PlayerGui:WaitForChild) for GUIs and parts that may not load in time, to avoid errors.
- 2025-05-12 JustDucksey [1:23](https://youtu.be/tSWHABNX2bU?t=83): Stop animating distant objects: freeze wind-shake on far props using a configurable range; the creator says a test with many objects stayed performant.
- 2025-05-19 Leif [1:09](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=69): Write code outside Studio in VS Code with the Luau language server, Selene linter and StyLua auto-format on save for richer IntelliSense and cleaner code.
- 2025-05-19 Leif [7:11](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=431): Enable the Luau language server auto-import setting so services and module require paths are suggested and written automatically.
- 2025-05-21 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/math-for-roblox-developers-1-vectors/3659072): Use Vector3/Vector2 with built-in methods (:Dot, :Cross, .Unit, :Lerp) rather than hand-computing the math; the APIs cover it.
- 2025-05-21 JustDucksey [0:42](https://youtu.be/qVkYbVHWI1I?t=42): Set starting cash for new players in the player-join server script so it is easy to tune.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [2:39](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=159): Test physical item interactions early; Dev 2's dropped pickaxe was lost, so validate that tools and placed objects persist and can be recovered.
- 2025-05-24 Crusherfire [8:52](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=532): Use the T runtime type-checking library (recommended in Roblox docs) to validate values instead of repeated type() comparisons, especially for remote arguments.
- 2025-05-24 Crusherfire [10:56](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=656): Build T.interface schemas for complex tables and nest interfaces or tuples to validate data passed through RemoteEvents at runtime.
- 2025-05-24 Crusherfire [13:03](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=783): Wrap sound or effect triggers in a throttle(id, delay, fn) helper so bulk child-added events play only once within the delay window.
- 2025-05-24 Crusherfire [16:34](https://youtu.be/Nqtpzrkwm1Q?t=994): Implement waitWithTimeout with a coroutine plus task.delay race so yields on RBXScriptSignals can time out instead of hanging forever.
- 2025-05-25 GnomeCode [3:39](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=219): Pair CollectionService GetInstanceAddedSignal with a GetTagged loop so tagged parts that exist at startup are also set up.
- 2025-05-25 GnomeCode [4:19](https://youtu.be/2jcCCIcxvdk?t=259): For dynamically added tagged parts, check they are inside Workspace so copies stored in ServerStorage are ignored.
- 2025-06-16 ArTheDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/2M8r1oU2k3U?t=41): Create leaderstats folder and values on PlayerAdded; name the folder exactly 'leaderstats' and the value 'coins' (lowercase L) so it shows in the top-right.
- 2025-06-16 ArTheDev [9:39](https://youtu.be/2M8r1oU2k3U?t=579): Handle purchases server-side: check currency >= price and unlock flag is false before subtracting currency and setting the flag to true.
- 2025-06-16 ArTheDev [21:27](https://youtu.be/2M8r1oU2k3U?t=1287): Use the HumanoidRootPart (or FindFirstChild fallback) as the teleport target and set CFrame to the destination part's CFrame.
- 2025-06-19 Roox4 [0:42](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=42): Don't replicate every projectile as a physical part over the network; network bandwidth becomes the bottleneck. Simulate them with math instead.
- 2025-06-19 Roox4 [2:46](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=166): Make bullets purely visual on clients and have the server simulate them from start position, direction and velocity; this reduces each object to a few numbers.
- 2025-06-19 Roox4 [3:29](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=209): For non-linear projectiles like grenades, use a closed-form ballistic equation to compute position at any time instead of simulating physics objects.
- 2025-06-19 Roox4 [5:34](https://youtu.be/vBZMscweRDk?t=334): Avoid rotating turrets with CFrame updates, animation rigs or mechanical constraints; they were too expensive at scale.
- 2025-06-24 SimTek Game Development [0:00](https://youtu.be/4tGM1Ac5fcI?t=0): Remove debug print statements from the output window once the system works; spammed prints bury real errors.
- 2025-07-13 ArTheDev [5:18](https://youtu.be/WImDkrf3sAo?t=318): Auto-expire states with a delayed task after the duration, and nil the state when it ends, so you never need manual cleanup logic.
- 2025-07-13 ArTheDev [5:18](https://youtu.be/WImDkrf3sAo?t=318): Guard state setters with type checks on duration and existence checks on player tables to avoid errors from typos or missing entries.
- 2025-07-13 janisjanis01 [9:21](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=561): Make gifting and trading server-validated; the creator found a dupe bug in stealing and gifting that must be fixed before launch.
- 2025-07-15 PineappleDev [1:33](https://youtu.be/QRkRk-FHN4s?t=93): Clamp the scaled roll to [0, totalWeight] so a roll can never fall outside the valid weight range.
- 2025-07-16 ArTheDev [9:54](https://youtu.be/csxJTfPVW8U?t=594): Use Debris to destroy animation tracks after their length plus one second so they are cleaned up automatically.
- 2025-07-16 ArTheDev [16:14](https://youtu.be/csxJTfPVW8U?t=974): Guard playback with an is-playing check so spamming an input does not overlap the same animation.
- 2025-07-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/more-creators-can-now-use-rewarded-video-ads/3838678): Create the reward dev product in the same universe as the place; the server calls CreateAdRewardFromDevProductId and ShowRewardedVideoAdAsync, then grants the reward in ProcessReceipt.
- 2025-07-25 ArTheDev [7:35](https://youtu.be/Pi8rQ9gGTKE?t=455): Bind R for block rather than Mouse Button 2 with ContextActionService, since MB2 interferes with camera movement.
- 2025-07-25 ArTheDev [36:18](https://youtu.be/Pi8rQ9gGTKE?t=2178): Gate every action with a shared can't-do-anything state so players cannot attack, block or evade while stunned, attacking or blocking.
- 2025-07-25 Crusherfire [1:42](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=102): Open F9 developer console, Memory tab, sort by high usage; check Physics Parts and Sounds categories to find memory hogs.
- 2025-07-25 Crusherfire [12:34](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=754): Set Workspace PlayerCharacterDestroyBehavior to Enable to avoid leaked connections on character models and players.
- 2025-07-25 Crusherfire [27:51](https://youtu.be/YWyJHwynM1M?t=1671): Use Workspace:BulkMoveTo with BulkMoveMode FireCFrameChanged for many moving parts; it took ~0.49ms vs 0.255ms in his test, and individual CFrame sets were slower.
- 2025-07-28 SimTek Game Development [1:20](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=80): Put literals like walk speeds, animation IDs, detection radius and sight distance in a config ModuleScript instead of hardcoding them across scripts.
- 2025-07-28 SimTek Game Development [36:27](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=2187): Use a RaycastParams exclusion list containing the NPC's own model so line-of-sight rays don't hit the zombie itself.
- 2025-07-28 SimTek Game Development [40:35](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=2435): Use a random wander delay (min to max seconds) and a task.wait loop of about 0.5s for AI decisions to keep state changes varied and cheap.
- 2025-07-28 SimTek Game Development [53:36](https://youtu.be/9rzgsXFPTjY?t=3216): Check that HumanoidRootPart and target character exist before using them; WaitForChild on the Animator avoids nil errors when spawning NPCs.
- 2025-07-31 ArTheDev [11:35](https://youtu.be/_6F6vRt_9sM?t=695): Store last-used timestamps and compare elapsed time at check time instead of looping or polling for cooldown checks.
- 2025-08-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566): Use Parallel Luau for expensive operations such as raycasting; for simple tasks the actor overhead can make it slower.
- 2025-08-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566): Clamp the worker count to the row count (max one worker per row) and sanitise inputs before creating Actors.
- 2025-08-10 Stewiepfing [7:34](https://youtu.be/THpSS73tveg?t=454): Format numbers inside the display module, not before sending them, because adding 1k to a number fails if the number is already a string.
- 2025-08-10 Crusherfire [9:18](https://youtu.be/dJjZ7ml9bSo?t=558): Never yield inside initialize or start; yielding stalls the loader for every other module. Loader warns after a yield threshold is exceeded.
- 2025-08-10 Crusherfire [21:14](https://youtu.be/dJjZ7ml9bSo?t=1274): Parallel modules run in separate actors with separate Luau VMs, so require caching is not shared; do not expect shared state across them.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [24:40](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1480): Call get ad availability before showing the watch button, and again after each watch; calling once per session yields only one ad.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [25:21](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1521): Call availability close to when the player can actually press the button; calling too early lowers impression rate and EPM.
- 2025-08-29 Roblox Creators [27:09](https://youtu.be/7AtvvUxvCaU?t=1629): Never show ad prompts to ineligible (under-13) users; check the Eligibility API separately if not using availability checks.
- 2025-08-29 Duckable [7:09](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=429): Test AI-generated code immediately; it may use deprecated Roblox methods or introduce errors that only show at runtime.
- 2025-08-29 Duckable [10:49](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=649): Profile performance after adding systems; AI-written code dropped one test game from about 100 to 18 FPS when buying many upgrades.
- 2025-08-29 Duckable [10:49](https://youtu.be/Em5H7sNWN7I?t=649): Clean up deleted objects from tracking tables; AI code kept references to deleted fireworks, risking server memory growth and crashes.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [2:31](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=151): Write services as singleton module tables, not constructors; add an init function for object creation and non-yielding startup code.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [2:31](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=151): Use dot-call with explicit self parameter and type annotation instead of colon syntax so the Luau type checker understands self.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [3:54](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=234): Never create RemoteEvents/RemoteFunctions manually; generate them in code via a small remote wrapper (under 50 lines) to avoid messy hierarchies and name dependencies.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [5:39](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=339): Have clients request actions (e.g. 'can I build here?') and validate on the server; the speaker's example enforces a 5-second cooldown between builds.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [7:24](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=444): Group helper modules into clear categories such as core, game, math and platform, and keep each module small and focused.
- 2025-09-01 PineappleDev [4:03](https://youtu.be/gXKoxaK6Ufg?t=243): Fetch product info with MarketplaceService for the next tier's price using a retry loop so the displayed Robux price updates reliably.
- 2025-09-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628): Set Instance parent last after configuring properties, especially for Attachments whose WorldPosition changes when parented.
- 2025-09-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628): Compute point velocity as AssemblyLinearVelocity + AssemblyAngularVelocity:Cross(point - part.Position) since built-in GetVelocityAtPosition reportedly doesn't work reliably.
- 2025-09-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628): The tutorial's code is not production-ready and omits optimisation; modify before shipping.
- 2025-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941): Add --!optimize 2 at the top of scripts; live games already run at optimize 2, but Studio testing may differ. Level 0 disables all Luau optimizations.
- 2025-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941): Avoid loadstring, getfenv and setfenv; they force safeenv false and drop the script to optimize level 0.
- 2025-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941): Avoid unnecessary closures; non-capturing functions compile to cheaper DUPCLOSURE instead of NEWCLOSURE, and immutable captures may still be turned into plain functions.
- 2025-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941): Use operators instead of builtins where possible: // for math.floor, % for math.pow-style modulo, # for string.len, since they compile to direct bytecode.
- 2025-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941): Keep builtin calls on the FASTCALL-supported list (math, string, table.insert/unpack, bit32, buffer, vector, rawget/rawset, select, tostring, etc.) to skip normal call overhead.
- 2025-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941): Use for-loop forms ipairs/pairs/next with the generic for; the compiler emits specialized FORG/FORGPREP_INEXT/FORGPREP_NEXT instructions for them.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [32:18](https://youtu.be/3h25HUtBHFk?t=1938): Pre-generate AI assets (e.g., fish) at game start so generation latency is hidden; then use mini-games or engaging UX while waiting for slow generations.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [16:12](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=972): Prefer PreferredInput over LastInputType for showing hints and controls; it is stable and avoids costly re-renders from every keystroke.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [17:49](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1069): Define actions in the input action system with bindings for touch, gamepad and keyboard, so game code connects once to the action regardless of hardware.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [19:57](https://youtu.be/R1gecdxghwE?t=1197): Input action system is built on server authority: actions replicate and re-simulate on the server, so validate inputs there. Client beta available now.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [28:27](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=1707): Enable server authority in workspace properties to get latency compensation for avatars and nearby parts with no custom code.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [30:42](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=1842): Keep part prediction on auto mode; set prediction on only for key objects like balls, since predicting too many parts costs frame rate.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [31:21](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=1881): Put core game logic in the new fixed heartbeat hook, which runs at a fixed rate and re-runs during rollback.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [34:46](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=2086): Store rollback-sensitive state such as scores in attributes, not Luau variables, because attributes roll back on misprediction.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [33:38](https://youtu.be/V7MKrGggOOA?t=2018): Validate all client inputs on the server even with server authority, since clients can still send arbitrary values.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [5:15](https://youtu.be/lUc3Xj8Ojd8?t=315): Chat frame time is now under 1 ms on all platforms, and it costs zero frame time when chat is turned off.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [5:12](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=312): Test client memory on a real low-end device with the dev console; PC client readings differ from mobile.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [9:54](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=594): Anchor and disable animators for NPCs outside combat range; Dead Rails cut server CPU by about half.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [13:29](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=809): Don't resize welded or anchored server parts per frame; make cosmetic effects client-side.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [17:16](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1036): Don't store server-only data as attributes and tags on many parts; they replicate and multiply memory.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [29:26](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1766): Triage: confirm the game works, test on real hardware, check frame rate, then stats screen, then microprofiler.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [16:34](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=994): Wait for CharacterAdded and WaitForChild before reading character or humanoid references, since they are often missing when scripts load.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [24:51](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1491): Set a sprinting flag rather than toggling, and only drain stamina when the Humanoid is actually moving (Running event with speed > 0).
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [25:31](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1531): Use one server loop over all players with a short wait (0.2s) instead of a per-player loop or Heartbeat, to save server resources.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [28:19](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1699): Remove players from the sprinting table on PlayerRemoving so the table does not grow over long-running servers.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [36:41](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=2201): Use math.clamp for stamina so it stays between 0 and 100, then kill the humanoid when it hits zero.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [41:29](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=2489): Wait for child on UI loads from the server, since data loads can be slow and UI scripts may run before values exist.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [26:14](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1574): Test state transitions with print statements (moving/not moving) to catch duplicate Running events firing multiple times.
- 2025-09-28 PineappleDev [0:22](https://youtu.be/tmyowxxIv8M?t=22): Keep parts anchored whenever possible; unanchored physics parts cost far more FPS than anchored ones, even when off-screen.
- 2025-10-03 ArTheDev [4:00](https://youtu.be/KaCj7Jld35Q?t=240): Use HumanoidStateType changes (Jumping, Freefall, Landed) to track the character's last action instead of polling state every frame.
- 2025-10-03 ArTheDev [7:25](https://youtu.be/KaCj7Jld35Q?t=445): Use ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority for keys already used by default controls (W/A/S/D, Space) so you don't overwrite their built-in behaviour; BindAction overrides them.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [3:23](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=203): Establish a default baseline state (e.g. empty tycoon) and snapshot from it, so you can compare against it after each play cycle.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [12:47](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=767): Add every observer or signal connection to the cleanup trove; untracked observers keep references to destroyed instances like proximity prompts.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [14:09](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=849): When removing a key table entry on cleanup (e.g. per-inventory tracking), set the table entry to nil; otherwise destroyed instances pile up as keys.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [15:33](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=933): Add zone objects (and other third-party module objects) to the trove so they are destroyed on cleanup; check whether a leak is in the module or your misuse of it.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [16:53](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=1013): Use a per-model trove for connections that are recreated on each rebirth, cleaning it when a new model is created, instead of the player-lifetime trove.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [18:15](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=1095): Audit cached tables in third-party modules; caches of instances must be cleared on instance destruction or they leak. Removing a useless cache fixed it here.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [21:02](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=1262): Enable Workspace PlayerCharacterDestroyBehavior (default is disabled) so old characters are destroyed rather than parented to nil; test that cleanup on PlayerRemoving still works.
- 2025-10-06 Crusherfire [20:27](https://youtu.be/0moy04A9vFg?t=1227): Repeat the same snapshot process on the client; the workflow is identical to the server.
- 2025-10-07 Crit [4:00](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=240): Build door traversal with two anchored, non-collidable parts on each side of the door, each with an Attachment, linked by a PathfindingLink.
- 2025-10-07 Crit [6:01](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=361): Return true from PathfindingLinkReached (or it stalls the Forbidden movement loop); fall back to a warning and return true so the AI can self-recover.
- 2025-10-07 Crit [7:26](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=446): Open the door by setting its open BoolValue to true; the door's own script detects the change, so no door logic needs editing.
- 2025-10-07 Crit [8:07](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=487): Use humanoid:MoveTo(waypoint.Position) and wait for MoveToFinished before finishing the link, so the NPC walks through the opened door.
- 2025-10-07 Crit [10:51](https://youtu.be/nEvM3aYY2pY?t=651): Set collision groups: doors don't collide with NPCs, and NPCs don't collide with each other, to prevent NPCs getting stuck and improve pathing performance.
- 2025-10-11 janisjanis01 [6:44](https://youtu.be/MML_z-jpmF4?t=404): Pathfinding enemies took hours to get working; use established tutorials or known patterns rather than unverified AI-generated scripts.
- 2025-10-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940): For responsiveness, run actions on the client immediately (hit, damage, visuals), send the result to the server, and roll back if the server rejects it. Expect some rollback edge cases.
- 2025-11-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/launching-even-more-server-cpu/4044040): Double-check any system that scales based on number of connected players, since per-player CPU allocation now changes dynamically with player count.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Call GetValue for an experiment config key only when the feature is needed, since calling it enrolls the player immediately.
- 2025-11-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/live-now-use-configs-and-experiments-to-grow-your-game-faster/4051385): Group related config values into a JSON-type config to namespace keys and simplify management; add configs ahead of time.
- 2025-11-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-reflectionservice-programmatic-access-to-engine-api-information/4078522): Pass a Security filter to GetClasses to query as a specific context; default output depends on the calling script's SecurityCapabilities (Plugin, ModuleScript, or regular script).
- 2025-12-03 Lnor Roblox [1:31](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=91): Too many enemies using PathfindingService made the prototype very laggy. Limit active pathfinding agents and enemy counts.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157): Avoid frame-rate-dependent scripts when predicting; use the consistent-rate BindToSimulation callback so client and server state stay in sync. Replaces FixedHeartbeat.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321): Create or destroy instances on the server outside the simulation loop, e.g. inside task.defer, since new instances can't be created inside BindToSimulation.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321): Sync Humanoid enabled states via attributes set every frame in BindToSimulation, since Humanoid:SetStateEnabled does not replicate.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321): Always nil-check the instance before RunService:SetPredictionMode; passing nil currently crashes.
- 2025-12-13 janisjanis01 [14:04](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=844): Watch for memory leaks and button logic bugs after launch; a wrong pointer and leak appeared once real players joined.
- 2025-12-15 SimTek Game Development [4:51](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=291): Store face directions as unit vectors and multiply by block size, so changing block size later only requires one constant change.
- 2025-12-15 SimTek Game Development [32:21](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1941): Use OverlapParams with Include filter (Whitelist/Blacklist are deprecated) and workspace:GetPartBoundsInBox to block placement when something occupies the target cell.
- 2025-12-15 SimTek Game Development [33:01](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=1981): Shrink overlap test box by about 0.2 studs per axis to avoid false positives on edges between adjacent blocks.
- 2025-12-21 Possible Panda [9:33](https://youtu.be/aSE2D4_TwoU?t=573): Use Timer.new(interval) with Timer:Start and Timer.Tick:Connect instead of while-true wait loops; it wraps RunService-style timing for you.
- 2025-12-23 Cumin [20:45](https://youtu.be/hQpC_UHxJDU?t=1245): Ensure enemy attack hitboxes end when the animation ends. Lingering hitboxes causing damage after attacks stop feel amateur and unfair.
- 2025-12-29 Possible Panda [13:00](https://youtu.be/0rmh0FpHBYM?t=780): Guard data access with an is-loaded check, or expose a per-player loaded signal, to avoid nil errors before profile loads.
- 2025-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236): Don't replicate large NPC counts as moving Humanoids; use a custom system where the server sends position data and clients render it, to offload replication cost.
- 2025-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236): Use flowfield pathfinding (e.g. FastFlow) for hordes: one calculated field is shared by all NPCs and only recalculated when targets or the environment change.
- 2026-01-03 Leif [9:53](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=593): Always return a cleanup function from useEffect when subscribing to events or timers, so connections don't leak when the component unmounts.
- 2026-01-03 Leif [10:36](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=636): Always pass a dependency array to useEffect. Omitting it reruns the effect on every render; use an empty table if it has no dependencies.
- 2026-01-03 Leif [13:26](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=806): Use useBinding instead of useState for values that change every frame (animations). State causes a rerender each frame; bindings update properties directly.
- 2026-01-03 Leif [13:52](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=832): Keep UI-only state (open menus, selected tab) in React, and real game data (coins, inventory, settings) in an external reactive store such as Charm.
- 2026-01-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561): Search codebase (Ctrl+Shift+F) for Motor6D references; C0/C1/Part0/Part1 setters will error on AnimationConstraints, so switch to Attachments.
- 2026-01-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561): Scripts using instance:IsA("Motor6D") will not detect AnimationConstraints; update those checks.
- 2026-01-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/completing-the-dynamic-head-migration/4301387): Call AvatarEditorService:ConformToAvatarRulesAsync() before presenting an avatar so legacy Classic assets are swapped for their Dynamic Head equivalents with the correct HeadShape.
- 2026-02-10 GnomeCode [6:37](https://youtu.be/3D0l-WiEhLI?t=397): Wrap per-object while-true loops in task.spawn so one infinite loop doesn't block the module loader from processing the rest.
- 2026-02-10 GnomeCode [11:41](https://youtu.be/3D0l-WiEhLI?t=701): Use WaitForChild for Humanoid, HumanoidRootPart and Animate on the client, since they may not have replicated yet.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Keep the AbilityManagerActor folder intact with both ServerInitialize and ClientInitialize scripts when moving scripts into your experience.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Update to the latest library version (restart Studio to force update) to get the RemoteEvent Player argument verification fix.
- 2026-02-28 GnomeCode [0:21](https://youtu.be/HYBG2AtUBes?t=21): Wrap player-added setup in task.spawn and loop over existing players plus PlayerAdded so setup isn't missed if the script yields.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [3:17](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=197): Store large incremental values as strings and use a Big Num library to bypass Lua's number limit.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [8:55](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=535): Use math instead of for loops when opening many items at once so bulk opens don't crash or lag the server.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [11:49](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=709): Use a data service update helper that passes the current value into a function, simplifying stat increments.
- 2026-03-02 1_Bruce [12:33](https://youtu.be/9ZoyhYqBkcI?t=753): Tag objects (e.g. button, rune) and use a component system so game logic is modular and object-oriented.
- 2026-03-14 DitchyDevelopments [1:27](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=87): Use sub-step position checks between frames so fast-moving players still register tags/hits reliably.
- 2026-03-15 Stewiepfing [7:55](https://youtu.be/JG1fkhCKAB4?t=475): Use assert(condition, message) to fail loudly with a custom error message when a required value is missing, rather than failing silently.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [36:25](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=2185): Separate front end (UI, animation, feel) from back end (generation, AI, item logic, state); back end feeds front end via signals.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [1:29:03](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=5343): Discord bots that pull in-game stats via slash commands are a satisfying, low-maintenance way to engage the community.
- 2026-03-29 Stewiepfing [0:17](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=17): Drive all visual effects from RunService events (PreRender/RenderStepped) so they update every frame, using delta time for timing.
- 2026-03-29 Stewiepfing [5:42](https://youtu.be/1nPi3xsAPwM?t=342): Make lerp-based smoothing frame-rate independent: alpha = 1 - math.pow(smoothness, dt) instead of a fixed lerp factor.
- 2026-03-31 ArTheDev [3:55](https://youtu.be/R3q6hZs8FDE?t=235): Learn basics first: variables, functions, loops, services, RemoteEvents/RemoteFunctions, and the three script types, via a beginner YouTube playlist.
- 2026-03-31 ArTheDev [4:36](https://youtu.be/R3q6hZs8FDE?t=276): After basics, focus on how concepts connect in a script, e.g. button click -> RemoteEvent -> server validates action -> updates leaderstats.
- 2026-03-31 ArTheDev [7:12](https://youtu.be/R3q6hZs8FDE?t=432): When reading toolbox or others' code, trace where things connect and why, rather than copy-pasting it.
- 2026-04-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788): Set graph parameters from Lua with track:SetParameter("Speed", value) instead of manually blending tracks in code.
- 2026-04-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788): Existing LoadAnimation scripts keep working; adopt the graph only where complex blending or sequencing justifies it.
- 2026-04-03 ArTheDev [2:30](https://youtu.be/CnZEnFVAFSw?t=150): Initialize the weather module from a server script; a ModuleScript never runs unless required. Seed math.random once at server start so weather differs per server.
- 2026-04-03 ArTheDev [10:25](https://youtu.be/CnZEnFVAFSw?t=625): Keep the weather tick loop at task.wait(1) so the countdown matches real seconds; put any code after a while-true loop inside a separate thread or before it.
- 2026-04-03 ArTheDev [8:22](https://youtu.be/CnZEnFVAFSw?t=502): Use TweenService to smoothly transition Lighting, Atmosphere and Clouds properties per weather type instead of setting them instantly.
- 2026-04-06 ArTheDev [12:26](https://youtu.be/RDjsR4iIRlg?t=746): Clear the player's entry from the live inventories table on leave to avoid memory leaks as players join and leave.
- 2026-04-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575): Use RaycastParams.ExcludeInstances/IncludeInstances instead of FilterDescendantsInstances; new APIs are much faster, especially with large filter lists.
- 2026-04-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575): Set IncludeInstances = nil for most permissive filter and {} for include-nothing; they are opposites, don't confuse them.
- 2026-04-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575): If an instance is in both exclude and include lists, exclusion wins; design lists so they don't overlap unintentionally.
- 2026-04-07 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575): Repeatedly updating filter lists (e.g., adding parts dynamically) is cheap in the new APIs thanks to tuned reflection code.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Expect performance from equal to 2x faster than Humanoid on server and client depending on in-game circumstances; benchmark your own scene.
- 2026-04-12 Stewiepfing [9:23](https://youtu.be/DKsPBYvBbyY?t=563): Observe ClickDetectors globally with observe class so every click detector gets a connection, and its connection is disconnected automatically when the parent is removed.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [8:46](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=526): Validate summon amount on the server (reject values above 10 or below 1) and never trust client-sent prices; set the price server-side.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [14:14](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=854): Compute max affordable summons from the currency balance and cap the loop to it, so partial multis still work without errors.
- 2026-04-18 ArTheDev [14:56](https://youtu.be/Iy9xuT1bpY4?t=896): Set a summoning-in-progress flag on the server before rolling to block duplicate summon requests and double-spending.
- 2026-04-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056): Call parameters:Pause() inside long generation loops to spread work across frames and avoid dropping frames.
- 2026-05-09 Creators on the Blox [43:14](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=2594): Scrap features built in unfamiliar code styles if they bug out in playtest and can't be fixed quickly; rewriting from scratch may be cheaper.
- 2026-05-10 janisjanis01 [10:01](https://youtu.be/BLOToGi2Qt4?t=601): Leaderboards have a numeric cap (9.22 quadrillion); store values scaled down (divide by 100) and restore on load to bypass it.
- 2026-05-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/protect-your-games-with-script-capabilities-sandboxing/4634642): Avoid require(id), LoadAsset, LoadString and getfenv/setfenv in Store assets; these are blocked in sandboxed assets and prohibited by Store policy.
- 2026-06-07 ArTheDev [2:13](https://youtu.be/wG9XzLnJC5k?t=133): Learn data handling carefully early on, since doing it wrong can completely break your game.
- 2026-06-10 MoseyTube [2:07](https://youtu.be/esZQX5COI_E?t=127): Put shared cooldowns on reward mechanics so players can't abuse them by hopping between instances (e.g. plots).
- 2026-06-19 LovJak [3:32](https://youtu.be/ImcICq5XfL8?t=212): Reuse existing models and assets from your own previous games to speed up building.
- 2026-06-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/automated-performance-profiling-with-microprofiler-and-assistant/4704417): For advanced profiling, use the MicroProfiler API (LibMP) to programmatically access all MicroProfiler data.
- 2026-07-04 Generalist Programmer [3:46](https://youtu.be/DirIakVx7eQ?t=226): Give every dropped cash part a lifespan with Debris:AddItem so uncollected drops are deleted and the world does not lag.
- 2026-07-04 Generalist Programmer [6:41](https://youtu.be/DirIakVx7eQ?t=401): Debounce collector Touched events with an attribute on each part so a drop that fires Touched multiple times is only credited once.
- 2026-07-04 Generalist Programmer [2:47](https://youtu.be/YMvezOFyl8o?t=167): Enable File > Game Settings > Security > 'Enable Studio Access to API Services' or DataStore saves appear to do nothing in Studio.
- 2026-07-04 Generalist Programmer [3:26](https://youtu.be/YMvezOFyl8o?t=206): Wrap every SetAsync/GetAsync in pcall; DataStore calls throw on network or service failures and will halt the script mid-save.
- 2026-07-04 Generalist Programmer [4:47](https://youtu.be/YMvezOFyl8o?t=287): Retry failed saves in a loop, about three attempts with a short wait between them; most transient failures clear on the second try.
- 2026-07-04 Duckable [4:04](https://youtu.be/eRhFPgj8tc4?t=244): Don't use math.random alone for rolls; players can predict pseudo-random output. Seed from multiple entropy sources (time, IDs) and hash them, server-side.
- 2026-07-07 Generalist Programmer [2:42](https://youtu.be/Oj0nbY61qsY?t=162): In a server Script, ClickDetector.MouseClick already gives the player on the server, so earning doesn't need a RemoteEvent.
- 2026-07-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993): Enable Server Authority via Workspace Properties AuthorityMode=Server; it auto-sets NextGenerationReplication, PlayerScriptsUseInputActionSystem, SignalBehavior=Deferred, StreamingEnabled and UseFixedSimulation.
- 2026-07-15 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905): Serialise Vector3, CFrame and Color3 as packed buffers (12, 13 and 4 bytes) rather than JSON tables (CFrame about 200 bytes as JSON).
- 2026-07-23 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/reference-instances-directly-with-attributes/4753441): Replace ObjectValue children with attributes that hold Instance references directly via SetAttribute(name, instance), removing extra instances to manage.
- 2026-07-29 VuukStudios [11:29](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=689): Learn essential scripting first, or budget to hire it out; scripting is the one core skill needed to build a proper game.
- 2026-08-01 SimTek Game Development [15:31](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=931): Use WaitForChild on Humanoid and Head before iterating character parts to avoid race conditions on load.
- 2026-08-01 SimTek Game Development [18:56](https://youtu.be/169S0rLOMc4?t=1136): Clone accessories before calling Humanoid:AddAccessory so the template in ServerStorage stays unchanged.
- 2026-08-04 Duckable [8:46](https://youtu.be/Cc7HT3uNjeQ?t=526): When merge logic fails, check for stale references: deleted droppers may still be indexed by old lookups, causing wrong merge results.
- 2026-08-09 Stewiepfing [4:49](https://youtu.be/Apqkcrd_gz0?t=289): For most Roblox orientation work, plain CFrames are sufficient; reach for quaternions mainly for combining rotations and smooth blending between orientations.
- 2026-08-09 Stewiepfing [10:28](https://youtu.be/Apqkcrd_gz0?t=628): Multiply rotation steps by delta time in Heartbeat so turret/rotation speed stays frame-rate independent.
- 2026-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143): Scope collections to a small root instance via CreateCollection's rootInstance argument; workspace-wide queries force tracking of many instances and cost more.
- 2026-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143): Include a class specifier (Part#Killbrick, not #Killbrick) so the engine ignores non-matching classes and only monitors relevant instances for name changes.
- 2026-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143): Avoid combinators (>, >>, has, in-radius) where possible; they add significant tracking overhead, with has() and in-radius() called out as expensive.
- 2026-08-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143): Tag and attribute selectors do not speed up queries and slightly slow them, since extra listeners are needed to detect tag and attribute changes.
- 2026-08-27 Leif [3:35](https://youtu.be/UhpaVrb5Imo?t=215): Use a spatial hash grid to find nearby objects to merge instead of comparing every object's position to every other each frame; the speaker calls the brute-force way very inefficient.
- 2026-08-30 Duckable [12:26](https://youtu.be/3XX3Y74hzq0?t=746): For bot AI, score every legal action (lane vulnerability, risk of losing a character) and pick the highest; add play-style weights for variety.
- 2026-09-08 RoyalDev [0:43](https://youtu.be/MZ-nsDEVXH4?t=43): List required script systems upfront (e.g. level loader) so you know what to build before starting code.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [38:25](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=2305): Character Controller Library (Lua replacement for Humanoid) only simulates characters that matter, so crowds scale better.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [39:49](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=2389): Slim reduced drawn triangles by 93% and memory by 60% in a simulated scene; Slim for NPCs is coming.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [52:13](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3133): Use the upcoming safety callback API to remove bad content or actors instead of letting the server shut down on moderation triggers.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [53:33](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3213): Device integrity API for competitive servers; blocking low-integrity Android devices cut cheating prevalence by 97% in a test.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [54:56](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3296): Queue service plus compute functions will enable queue-based matchmaking and global auction houses; early access planned early next year.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [57:40](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=3460): Prepare for offline play by splitting server code into pure gameplay scripts and server-only scripts (e.g. purchases); Player Data Store coming.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-september-14%E2%80%9320-2026/4880336): Use EditableMesh Batching APIs and Parallel Queries for faster mesh editing; now available in published experiences.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [0:42](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=42): Put client-side camera code in a LocalScript under StarterPlayer > StarterPlayerScripts, since the camera is client-side.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [2:49](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=169): Workspace.CurrentCamera may be nil on startup; loop until it exists before using it, since WaitForChild doesn't work on it.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [3:31](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=211): Use Player.CharacterAdded:Wait() before reading the character, because local scripts can run before the character loads.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [3:31](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=211): Use character:GetPivot() for the character CFrame; it is the preferred method over the HumanoidRootPart.
- 2026-09-19 SimTek Game Development [9:44](https://youtu.be/w0n23oW0690?t=584): For a smooth zoom, step the distance each frame in RunService.RenderStepped; without delta-time normalization the speed varies with frame rate.
- 2026-09-20 Possible Panda [4:37](https://youtu.be/KVmPyoOPmRI?t=277): Require the server version of a shared data module (.server) and keep the require outside other logic for clean access.
- 2026-09-21 RoyalDev [3:26](https://youtu.be/e953Nvhpdjo?t=206): Call AnalyticsService:LogOnboardingFunnelStepEvent(player, stepNumber, stepName) server-side, passing the player, step number and step name.
- 2026-09-26 SimTek Game Development [4:03](https://youtu.be/-0H6JRtrA2Y?t=243): Prompt the agent to review for best practices and server authority; the client should only send input, the server should set WalkSpeed.
- 2026-09-26 ArTheDev [9:45](https://youtu.be/fIwzczfIu2U?t=585): Validate required inputs (e.g. player) with an early-return guard if they are nil before using them.
- 2026-09-27 janisjanis01 [7:52](https://youtu.be/WJpNCB5J9bg?t=472): Changing signal behaviour from immediate to deferred fixed a sync issue between plot and character; check event ordering when objects fail to update.
