- 2026-09-27 Possible Panda [0:22](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=22): Download Scythe from its GitHub source (scythe.lua) and place it as a ModuleScript in a packages folder to require it.
- 2026-09-27 Possible Panda [2:27](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=147): Use scythe.add(scope, object) rather than a method on the scope, since Scythe scopes are plain numeric IDs, not objects.
- 2026-09-27 Possible Panda [3:09](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=189): Check scythe.isAlive(scope) before calling scythe.clean(scope) so you only clean scopes that still hold items.
- 2026-09-27 Possible Panda [3:18](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=198): Add RunService connections to a scope so one clean call disconnects them along with the parts they affect.
- 2026-09-27 Possible Panda [3:59](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=239): Add functions to a scope to run custom cleanup logic when it is cleaned.
- 2026-09-27 Possible Panda [4:40](https://youtu.be/WN1S4BDrrDg?t=280): Any object with a Destroy function can be added directly to a scope and will be destroyed on clean.
- 2026-09-30 ArTheDev [9:41](https://youtu.be/HjDD_0cDfPg?t=581): Use task.delay per emitter for its emit delay so effects stack their delays independently instead of blocking each other.
- 2026-09-30 ArTheDev [27:52](https://youtu.be/HjDD_0cDfPg?t=1672): Drive moving effects with RunService Heartbeat multiplied by delta time so speed is frame-rate independent across clients.
- 2026-09-30 ArTheDev [30:35](https://youtu.be/HjDD_0cDfPg?t=1835): Always disconnect the movement connection and Destroy the cloned effect after its lifetime; otherwise leaked connections and instances tank performance over time.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-september-28%E2%80%93october-2-2026/4910267): Enable Player.FrustumStreaming to stream only instances in the camera's view, reducing memory and loading for players.
- 2026-10-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-september-28%E2%80%93october-2-2026/4910267): Audit external API calls before October 19: cookie and unauthenticated IP rate limits for six web API services are being lowered.
- 2026-10-04 Possible Panda [0:00](https://youtu.be/C0cOB7Xqc1M?t=0): Consider QuickZone for zone detection with many entities; creator benchmark (500 moving entities, 10,000 zones) held 60 FPS vs Zone Plus at 4 FPS.
- 2026-10-05 ArTheDev [22:53](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=1373): Use OverlapParams with a box query instead of .Touched for ability hits; exclude the hitbox, caster, and temp folder from the query.
- 2026-10-05 ArTheDev [23:36](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=1416): Multiply per-frame movement by delta time in the Heartbeat hit loop so it matches the client, and disconnect the connection when the ability ends to avoid leaks.
- 2026-10-05 ArTheDev [22:09](https://youtu.be/QRFuxryK4Vg?t=1329): Track already-hit characters in a table during the hit loop so one target isn't damaged every frame.
- 2026-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568): Don't call BindToCollisionSummaries from inside a Touched handler; it is a separate API for collision information.
- 2026-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568): Subscribe only the parts you need; processing overhead scales with subscribed parts, not total scene collisions.
- 2026-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568): Dot RelativeVelocity with Normal to get head-on impact speed; use the tangential component to detect glancing hits and sliding.
- 2026-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568): Parts with CanCollide = false produce no summaries; sleeping-vs-sleeping contacts are not recorded.
- 2026-10-06 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568): Disconnect the returned RBXScriptConnection when finished with the subscription.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [13:23](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=803): Disconnect signals on animators and characters before destroying them, otherwise connected tracks can leak memory.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [8:35](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=515): Under server authority, read/write only synchronized data: input actions under the player and attributes on the humanoid root part. Unsynced Lua data corrupts on rollback.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [7:53](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=473): Put simulation logic in bind to simulation, not RenderStepped or Heartbeat, since simulation callbacks re-run during reimulation.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [7:13](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=433): Share simulation code via a ModuleScript called from both a client and a server script; local scripts can no longer drive a server-authoritative humanoid.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [5:47](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=347): Enable server authority for just the humanoid by setting Workspace authority mode to Server; no code needed for movement anti-speed/fly hacks.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [10:47](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=647): Test under simulated latency (Studio network simulation, e.g. 60ms latency, 10ms jitter) before trusting prediction results.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [11:27](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=687): Create projectiles on both client and server with server authority rather than hacking fake invisible rockets to hide latency.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [27:01](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1621): Use the CCL character controller library (V2 adds crouch and sprint) for server-authoritative abilities instead of hand-rolling them.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [21:53](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1313): Keep game rules as plain Luau modules that take all data as arguments, so drop tables, crafting odds and pricing can be tested from the command line in under a millisecond.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [22:39](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1359): Mesh LOD/streaming can significantly cut GPU frame time in heavy scenes (gas station: ~12.5ms to just under 6ms on Samsung S20). Reducing geometry matters most on mobile.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [29:44](https://youtu.be/OToDz3Ghpjo?t=1784): Use Slim (runtime baked model optimisation) only if you can afford baking and don't change the object at runtime; mesh LODs/streaming work without that constraint.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [12:35](https://youtu.be/QhZpK2hdnjE?t=755): Rely on pre-built physics-based behavior scripts (car, boat, drone, helicopter) rather than prompting an LLM from scratch; the scripts retarget to new segmented meshes.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [2:33](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=153): Use Character Control Library v2 for NPCs; presented as 2-4x more efficient than Humanoid. Available as studio beta since the keynote week.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [17:18](https://youtu.be/QvNAMmGqdd4?t=1038): For large NPC counts, push Character Control Library as far as possible; navigation v2 reduces nav mesh memory footprint by up to 90%.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [8:26](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=506): Attach version number, device group and custom game-state metadata to alerts so the team can start investigating immediately.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [9:09](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=549): Event-heavy live events can act as a mobile stress test; monitor client FPS and crash alerts during visually intense events.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [10:31](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=631): When investigating FPS drops, pick affected phone or tablet sessions rather than healthy desktop sessions, so you profile the case that actually reproduces the issue.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [11:16](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=676): Capture microprofiler dumps from live affected clients during the issue and diff against a pre-event dump to find the specific regressing task (here, particle emitters).
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [13:21](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=801): Keep server and client debug logs with version and game-state context; pulling logs from live servers avoids joining them yourself or asking players for dumps.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [7:50](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=470): Use the Character Controller Library: transparent Lua replacement for the C++ Humanoid, debuggable and extensible with custom abilities like dashes or double jumps.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [9:51](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=591): Use CCL action slots so abilities are bound per action rather than per device, avoiding separate mobile/console fixes.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [11:26](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=686): Slim for avatars plus LODs gives about 10x fewer triangles at distance with no extra work, for standard R15 player avatars (not yet NPCs or custom scaled avatars).
- 2026-10-16 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-12-18-2026/4938638): Package Overrides lets you edit a package in place; changes are tracked as overrides and reapplied on source package updates (File > Beta Features).
- 2026-10-27 ItsZyonic [0:41](https://youtu.be/EulQilcCXAo?t=41): Add a simple cleanup loop that despawns objects once they pass the end zone, or they accumulate.
- 2026-10-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-build-2d-games-with-animatedimages/4960734): Use AnimationPlayer instead of manually stepping ImageRectOffset/ImageRectSize in scripts. Track names keep spaces from Aseprite tags, so match them exactly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Call ConfigService only from server scripts; client calls error. Wrap GetConfigAsync in pcall with fallback values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Snapshots don't auto-update; connect UpdateAvailable to Refresh and use GetValueChangedSignal to apply changes safely, e.g., between rounds in a shooter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/environmental-art/optimize-your-experience): Near the end of development, review all physics and rendering parameters and disable CastShadow on foliage away from gameplay areas to save performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/selling-items): Always reset the items value to 0 after paying out; otherwise the touch script keeps granting gold on every touch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/selling-items): Guard each level of lookups with an if-check (player, leaderstats, items/gold) before reading or modifying values to avoid nil errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/selling-items): Use GetPlayerFromCharacter() on partTouched.Parent to map a touched character part back to its Player before changing leaderstats.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3): The leaderstats folder name must be exactly "leaderstats", otherwise the script won't create the leaderboard.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/data-stores/throttling): Exceeding requests/minute or throughput returns HTTP 429 Too Many Requests; batch or reduce writes and reads to stay under limits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Use Group rules to merge errors that differ only in variable details like player names or IDs so counts aggregate under one label.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Use Ignore rules to move known noisy errors into the Other bucket; totals are unchanged.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Order rules carefully: the first matching rule wins, so put specific rules before broad ones.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Rules only apply to new errors as they are processed; existing recorded errors are not changed. Allow a few minutes for changes to take effect.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Avoid patterns starting with unanchored wildcards like .* or nested quantifiers like (\w+)+; these are rejected with 400.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Ignore rule alternatives must start with at least 3 literal characters, e.g. ^AdService: is valid but ^\w+ is not.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/error-reporting): Review AutoGenerated rules suggested by Roblox (with matchedCount and sampleMessage) and dismiss unwanted ones via the ignore endpoint, which only works on AutoGenerated rules.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials): Script hazard logic (lava, fading traps) and player data (score, health, persistent data) as separate gameplay systems.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges): Award badges only from server-side Scripts, and call GetBadgeInfoAsync first to confirm IsEnabled before AwardBadgeAsync, wrapping both in pcall.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges): Use UserHasBadgeAsync in PlayerAdded to check ownership, or GetUserBadgesAsync to check many badges in batches.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Use a BoolValue named CanHarvest inside the item as a state flag; check it with FindFirstChild before awarding anything.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Set canHarvest.Value = false immediately on harvest so one tool swing cannot award more than one item.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/creating-a-gui): Drive GUI text with .Changed connections on value objects instead of polling, and call the update function once at the end of the script so late joiners see current state.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/creating-a-gui): Use task.wait() with no argument in a timer loop for more accurate per-frame updates than a fixed wait.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog): Base timers on Workspace:GetServerTimeNow() rather than DateTime.now() to keep client and server timers synchronized.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog): Guard against infinite loops when initializing data for new players on the server (fixed in Missions Server.Missions).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming): Wait for Workspace.PersistentLoaded before accessing persistent models in client scripts; otherwise use WaitForChild and tolerate streaming.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/solid-modeling): Wrap GeometryService calls in pcall, since operations fail on non-watertight meshes or meshes exceeding limits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/solid-modeling): Use SplitApart = false when you want one result part rather than separate bodies; default is true, returning each body as its own part.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/scene-analysis): Use Script Memory view to see Luau VM heap per Script, LocalScript and ModuleScript, separate from asset memory.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/proximity-prompts): Handle prompt events centrally with ProximityPromptService (PromptTriggered, PromptButtonHoldBegan, PromptButtonHoldEnded) instead of attaching a script to each prompt object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/proximity-prompts): Keep event handlers thin: have the service script call functions in a ModuleScript (e.g. ObjectActions) so action logic stays in one reusable place.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/create-flickering-lights): Read attributes once at script start and cache references to lights and beams rather than looking them up every frame.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/lighting/create-flickering-lights): Drive flicker from time() modulo a loop duration inside a Heartbeat connection so the cycle is deterministic and frame-rate independent.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/first-experience): Place scripts in the correct container service: Workspace renders, ReplicatedStorage is shared with clients, ServerScriptService is server-only. Wrong placement can break gameplay.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/first-experience): Put shared gameplay logic that the UI needs in ReplicatedStorage, and put logic that launches or resets objects in ServerScriptService.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/change-the-script): Keep tunable values such as point rewards in one settings table (GameSettings.pointValues) so balance changes need no logic edits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/change-the-script): Value type keys in pointValues must match folder names exactly, or points will not be awarded correctly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world): Use one LocalScript handling all CollectionService-tagged instances instead of many scripts to maintain.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/develop-a-moving-world): Use point-in-box checks over pre-placed volumes rather than touch events, since seated players stop touching volumes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/selfie-mode): configure, setTheme, openSelfieMode, activateAction and similar functions can only be called from a LocalScript; place the module in ReplicatedStorage and configure it from StarterPlayerScripts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alerts/metrics): Track HttpServiceResponseTime P90 and HttpServiceRequestsCount by status to find slow or failing external HTTP calls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/parameters-practice-buttons): Connect the function with button.Touched:Connect(buttonPressed) outside the function body, not inside it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/parameters-practice-buttons): Use script.Parent to reference the button and workspace.<Name> with the exact Explorer name to avoid 'not a valid member of Workspace' errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Keep ability callbacks deterministic and non-yielding, since they run in both predicted client and authoritative server simulations. Never call task.wait or WaitForChild in callbacks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Store custom callback data only in abilityCtx.State (replicated, rolled back) or abilityCtx.Local (scratch). Writing custom fields directly to abilityCtx is an error.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Within callbacks, avoid restricted DataModel operations such as Destroy; set an attribute and handle instance lifecycle from a regular script.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/coding-concept-algorithms): Use selection (if/then) for conditional outcomes, such as checking skill level before allowing item use.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/coding-concept-algorithms): Use iteration (loops with task.wait) for timed effects, such as lava dealing 5 health per second until respawn at 0.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/proximity-prompts): Use HoldDuration of 0 for instant actions; non-zero values require hold-event handling (PromptButtonHoldBegan/Ended).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/managing-players): Use table.remove(arenaSpawns, 1) when assigning spawns so each player gets a unique spawn location.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/managing-players): Use player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait() before accessing the character to avoid errors when the character hasn't loaded yet.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Put shared UI controller modules in ReplicatedStorage so both client and server-side scripts can require them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/interactive-ui): Wrap UI callbacks in pcall so an error in one callback logs a warning instead of breaking the slider.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/inventory): Use the filter parameter with comma-separated assetIds to check ownership of several items in one request instead of one call per item.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/inventory): Combine filters with semicolons (e.g. onlyCollectibles=true;inventoryItemAssetTypes=HAT) to narrow results server-side.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/inventory): When paginating, check both a non-empty nextPageToken and a non-empty inventoryItems array; a divisible result count can yield an empty final page with a token.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/data-model): Place scripts in the correct containers: Script in ServerScriptService, LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts/StarterGui, ModuleScript in ReplicatedStorage if shared. Wrong container means the script won't run or runs in the wrong place.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/client-server-boundary): Rate limit server operations that hit DataStoreService or BadgeService, since exceeding their built-in limits makes requests fail.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts): Many partially transparent parts can slow performance; merge them using solid modeling to reduce the count.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/frustum): Set Player.FrustumStreaming only on the server; client-side assignments have no effect, so relay requests via RemoteEvent and validate them server-side.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability): Reduce request volume: DataStructureRequestsOverLimit and TotalRequestsOverLimit throttling triggers a critical alert above 10% of requests in an hour; count is what matters, not size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/observability): Investigate request failures immediately: a critical alert fires when failures exceed 20% of requests within an hour.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging): Disconnect MessagingService subscriptions when the player leaves (e.g. on AncestryChanged) to avoid leaking subscriptions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging): Keep messages under 1,024 characters and topic names under 80 characters; larger payloads will be rejected.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/usage-messaging): Stay within rate limits: messages sent per server are 600 + 240 × players per minute; subscribe requests are capped at 240 per minute per server.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/mcp): Use script_grep and script_search to locate scripts; they return up to 50 and up to 10 results respectively.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop): Keep task.wait() inside repeat loops; without it the loop runs too many times per second and overloads Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop): Set intermissionDuration above 1 second; shorter values make the wait loop repeat too often and cause slowdown.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop): Use a repeat...until loop for intermission so the lobby always runs at least once before checking player count.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop): Keep all game-loop code inside the while true do block; code placed after it never runs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation): Trigger sound and effects from marker signals rather than timing waits in code, so audio stays in sync with the animation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/objects): Avoid the legacy Sound, SoundGroup and SoundEffect objects; Roblox now discourages them in favor of AudioPlayer, AudioEmitter, Wire and audio effect objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/audio/objects): Trigger audio from scripts by calling AudioPlayer:Play() on a correctly wired AudioPlayer, e.g. connected to a gameplay event.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/cframes): Use CFrame.lookAt(pos, target) to orient a part's front surface toward a point; then apply a relative rotation to aim a different face.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/proximity-chat): Use a dedicated channel instead of RBXGeneral in production so proximity rules don't affect the global chat.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes): Call RankProductsAsync once at game join rather than repeatedly, because it has a strict rate limit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/passes): Wrap RecommendTopProductsAsync and RankProductsAsync calls in task.spawn with pcall so slow ranking calls don't block the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/input-and-camera/control-the-users-camera): Bind camera updates with RunService:BindToRenderStep at Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1 so your function runs after Roblox's default camera update.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/cross-server-messaging): Store the SubscribeAsync connection and call Disconnect when the player leaves to avoid leaking subscriptions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Set CanTouch to false on objects that don't need collision events to improve performance.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Don't call BindToCollisionSummaries from inside a Touched handler; it is a separate API. Disconnect the connection when no longer needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Debounce Touched handlers with an attribute cooldown, since Touched can fire repeatedly from settling or multi-part contacts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Register all collision groups in a single script to avoid race conditions where a group is not yet registered when assigned.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Use NoCollisionConstraint for part-to-part filtering (e.g. wheel vs chassis) so models stay shareable without configuration scripts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/collisions): Use Box or Hull CollisionFidelity for small or non-interactive meshes; PreciseConvexDecomposition is the most expensive and slowest to compute.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching): Start calls to the same supported API concurrently (task.spawn per item) rather than awaiting one at a time; the engine batches them into fewer HTTP requests.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching): Start batched calls close together in time; calls started much later fall into a separate batch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching): Do not manually group requests or tune batch sizes; the engine picks the batching strategy automatically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching): Rely on built-in caching: repeated requests for the same data return from cache instead of making a new network call.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/transparent-batching): Batching only applies when the same supported method is called concurrently; serial waiting on each call sends one HTTP request per call and risks rate limits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/video-frames): Wait for VideoFrame.IsLoaded (or Loaded event) before calling Play() to avoid playing before the video is ready.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/roblox-for-the-ap-csp-create-performance-task): Write logical, reusable functions with parameters rather than trial-and-error code; reuse functions called from multiple places.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/coding-concept-algorithms): Structure a main function that calls smaller helper functions (e.g. pickClothes, putOnClothes) so each piece is testable and reusable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/coding-concept-algorithms): Use task.wait inside loops for timed logic, such as a countdown that decrements every second.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Replace custom camera scripts only if necessary; custom cameras opt out of updates and require manual merging of VR camera fixes. Merge current VR camera scripts if using a custom one.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Enable instance streaming so content streams in and out dynamically on the headset.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Limit CPU-heavy operations such as raycasting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Use RunService.Heartbeat rather than task.wait() for frame-precise updates.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/vr-guidelines): Use InputAction instead of keyboard-specific code so input bindings work across multiple sources.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/engagement): Test on various devices and platforms to find and fix performance issues; limit excessive textures, meshes and other variables that slow the game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/physics/create-spinning-objects): Apply angular impulse from a server Script on the part; example uses part:ApplyAngularImpulse(Vector3.new(0, math.random(0,100), 0)).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-1/object-properties): Use the Explorer to find an object's exact path before scripting; PracticePart under Workspace is referenced as workspace.PracticePart.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-1/object-properties): Use comments (--) to note what each script does for yourself and other programmers; comments are not executed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/galactic-speedway-lesson): Changing a single speed variable is a simple way to tune vehicle feel; introduce variables as placeholders for values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/camera): For a Scriptable camera, update Camera.Focus every frame, because nearby visuals get more detail based on distance to the focus point.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/bundles): Enable Studio Access to API Services in Experience Settings > Security so the package can track purchases with data store calls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/code-the-story): Keep variable names case-exact; Luau treats name1 and Name1 as different variables, so mismatched capitalization breaks the output.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/code-the-story): Use the .. operator to concatenate strings; put variable names outside quotation marks so their values are inserted, not literal text.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/code-the-story): Add a trailing space inside the string before the closing quote so the player's answer does not run into the next word.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Remove unique identifiers (user IDs, coordinates, timestamps) from error messages so repeats consolidate into one entry with a higher count.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Use Ignore rules only for permanent noise such as unavoidable engine sound errors or legacy print statements.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Use Group rules to consolidate families of related errors into one named row, e.g. HttpError:.* as 'Network Issues'.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Test regex patterns against real error strings before saving; patterns cannot start with .* or .+ and cannot nest repeaters like (\w+)+.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Rules apply only to errors logged after they are saved; order rules by priority since the first match wins.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/error-report): Use Developer Console (F9), Script Profiler, and Studio Output window to find and verify errors before publishing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points): Put scripts that set up game state in ServerScriptService so they run automatically when the game starts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points): Name the service variable exactly as the service (e.g. local Players = game:GetService("Players")) per Roblox convention.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points): Name the leaderstats folder exactly "leaderstats"; the leaderboard silently fails otherwise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/score-points): Use WaitForChild on the Character to safely wait for Humanoid before connecting Died, since model contents assemble at spawn.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority): Use RobloxGlobals time() inside simulation code; tick(), os.time() and os.clock() are local clocks that do not roll back and cause desync.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/customize-and-share): Keep tunable values (speed, boost duration, steering) in a single Settings module table so they can be adjusted without touching core logic.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/detect-hits): Put shared logic like getDirectionsForBlast in ReplicatedStorage so the server can recompute expected values and compare them against client data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Use BindableEvents for same-side server or client communication; use RemoteEvents for communication across the client-server boundary.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/bindable): Place server BindableEvents in ServerScriptService and client ones in ReplicatedStorage, as the docs recommend.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/create-a-timed-bridge): Use task.wait(1) inside a for loop for a one-second countdown tick rather than deprecated wait calls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/landing): Use Touch events on a sell part to convert player items into currency.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/stream-in-immersion): Do not have client scripts scan the workspace for required instances at game start; streamed instances may not be loaded yet.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/stream-in-immersion): Check whether scripts or elements depend on other content, since dependencies may be unloaded at certain times.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/intro-to-arrays): The legacy Chat API shown is for demonstration only; in real games use TextChatService.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/spawn-respawn): Store per-player GetAttributeChangedSignal connections in a table and disconnect them in PlayerRemoving to avoid connection growth over time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/gameplay-scripting/implement-blasters): Store weapon config as attributes on config instances and read them at use time, so tuning needs no code changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library/quick-start): Configure abilities via a server Script in ServerScriptService using AvatarAbilities.getAbilityConfigurationForCharacter(), since the Abilities folder contents vary with which abilities are enabled.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library/quick-start): Wait for the ability configuration and ControllerManager to exist before editing controllers; the example uses timeouts of 10 seconds while polling with task.wait().
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/types): Duration values must be strings ending in 's' with at most nanosecond precision, within ±315,576,000,000 seconds.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards): Loop through a dedicated Hazards folder with GetChildren and connect Touched once per part so new hazards are added by just placing them in the folder.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards): Check that the touched part belongs to a player's character with Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter before acting on it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards): Run WaitForChild calls inside task.spawn so waiting for a Humanoid does not block the player-setup loop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/create-a-health-pickup): Use FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") instead of FindFirstChild("Humanoid") so only an actual Humanoid instance is returned, not any child with that name.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/social-interactions): Call configure() only from a LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts; it cannot be called from a server script.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/extended-services): Before paying for more CPU, reduce the work your servers do; Extended Services for Compute is rarely needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/assistant/prompt-engineering): Distinguish edit time from run time: ask Assistant to 'add a script that does X' for runtime behaviour, since it otherwise biases toward edit-time changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio): Use Studio's script editor syntax highlighting, code completion and inline docs to speed up scripting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/modes): Use MicroProfiler Timers mode to list tasks with processing times and call counts; horizontal bars highlight the busiest tasks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Limit code on RunService PreRender/PreSimulation/PostSimulation/Heartbeat to what truly needs per-frame updates (e.g. camera); run other work in other events or on a throttled loop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Avoid recursive or large-table serialization, deserialization and deep cloning per frame; they scale badly with table size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Remove entries from per-player tables on PlayerRemoving; tables that only grow slow iteration and consume memory over long server sessions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/improve): Don't use Humanoids on static NPCs; use an AnimationController for animation-only NPCs and a custom movement controller for moving ones.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/create-with-module-scripts): Use WaitForChild() when requiring a module from a script that may load first. For ServerScriptService or ServerStorage paths, dot access is safe.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/use-microprofiler): Avoid casting large numbers of raycasts every frame in RunService.Stepped; 1,500 random rays per second caused a major frame rate drop in the example.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/configs): Read configs in server scripts via ConfigService and react to changes with UpdateAvailable/Refresh or GetValueChangedSignal.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/experiments): Use x-api-key with the Open Cloud creator-configs API and store keys outside source code; OAuth 2.0 apps are an alternative.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox): Plan for scale: free data storage limits have been raised, and extra service usage can be purchased as the game grows.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/emotes): UserEmotesEnabled in StarterPlayer > Character can only be set in Studio, not by scripts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/surface-appearance): Do not modify SurfaceAppearance properties by script at runtime; the engine needs pre-processing. Use Color tinting, which is free performance-wise, for runtime changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization): Target 60 FPS by default; expensive computation overtaxing CPU or GPU is the typical cause of low frame rate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization): Keep server heartbeat high; a low server heartbeat lowers frame rate for all clients and increases latency.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization): Fix memory leaks, since memory use can grow over time even when starting usage is low.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization): Reduce memory usage to avoid server crashes that disconnect everyone and to reach lower-end and mobile devices.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/traps-with-if-statements): Use FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") to locate a Humanoid by class rather than by name.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/3D-drag-detectors): Set PermissionPolicy to Scriptable and register a function; if the function is missing or returns an invalid result, all players are blocked from dragging.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): Put drag-response scripts in a LocalScript (or client-run Script) because user-initiated drag events only fire on the client.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/ui-drag-detectors): Use AddConstraintFunction with priority values to chain clamping logic; constraints run from lowest to highest priority.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/identify): Check server heartbeat in Developer Console Server Jobs tab (Steps Per Sec); heartbeat is capped at 60, so lower values indicate a performance issue.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/record-and-display-player-data): Use FindFirstChild to check whether an object exists before using it; it returns nil instead of erroring.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Indent code and write accurate comments (e.g. 'Changes color of PracticePart' not 'Runs code') to keep scripts readable as they grow.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Match exact capitalization of service and property names; a single wrong letter in names like FindFirstChildWhichIsA causes errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Define functions above the event connection that calls them (e.g. giveUpgrade() above clickDetector.MouseClick), since order matters.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Name the leaderstats folder exactly "leaderstats" or the leaderboard script will not create the leaderboard.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson): Set Anchored on for parts that fall or disappear during playtesting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/instance): Scripts open in Studio can't be updated via Open Cloud; close the Script Editor tab first, or you get a TypeError.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/instance): Collaborative session must be enabled for the game before Engine Instance APIs can access it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/instance): Keep Open Cloud update request bodies under 200 KB; larger payloads are rejected.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/instance): Start instance traversal from the 'root' ID, then use returned child IDs to drill deeper into the hierarchy.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/instance): Poll the Operation path for results; these methods are asynchronous and return an Operation object, not the resource. Example polling: every 5 seconds, up to 10 retries.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/best-practices): Apply static key patterns and prefixes to both keys and data structure names so every server routes the same logical data to the same location.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/best-practices): Shard sorted maps by assigning each user Id to one of N maps via modulo (userId % N + 1) using a helper function.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/best-practices): Shard queues with a revolving pattern: multiple queues, rotate read and add pointers; adds, reads and removes only occur at queue front/back.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/supporting-systems): Use a sphere-based Touched/TouchEnded detector around the player for highlights instead of scanning the whole map each frame.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/streaming/techniques): Avoid direct dot-indexing into Workspace descendants and character parts on client; use WaitForChild when progress requires it, or nil-check with FindFirstChild otherwise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/in-game-sounds): Check that the touched part is a character (Humanoid present) and CanCollide before triggering effects, to avoid false triggers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/code-the-leaderboard): Name the leaderboard container exactly "leaderstats" (case-sensitive); other names like "leaderboard" won't create a built-in leaderboard.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/code-the-leaderboard): Create the leaderstats Folder and parent it to the player inside a Players.PlayerAdded handler so every joining player gets a leaderboard.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/code-the-leaderboard): Use IntValue objects parented to leaderstats for each stat; the IntValue Name is what players see on the leaderboard.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/code-the-leaderboard): Set IntValue numbers via .Value (e.g. gold.Value = 10), not by reassigning the object.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/device-trust): Hold a player in PlayerAdded only when routing needs the level before spawn. Resume with a timeout of about 10 seconds so no player waits forever, and handle PlayerRemoving.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/characters/facial-animation/animate-heads): Play head animations from scripts manually, or replace default character animations with them to add expressiveness such as an annoyed idle or silly walk expression.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync): Avoid syncing folders that contain scripts alongside other instance types; Script Sync ignores non-script instances in synced folders.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/sync): Use the file naming suffixes (.server.luau, .client.luau, .local.luau, .legacy.luau, .plugin.luau) to set script types; plain .luau becomes a ModuleScript.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/landing): Learn functions, loops and if/then statements through small mini-projects that plug into a real game, such as traps and color-changing platforms.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/packages): Inspect scripts in packages you didn't create before running them, since unfamiliar assets can contain malicious scripts; save the place first so you can revert.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/nested-loops): Delete or disable an old test script before running a new one, otherwise two scripts spawn parts at the same place at the same time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/queue): Call RemoveAsync immediately after finishing processing read items so the same item is never processed twice.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/queue): Set the invisibility timeout longer than your expected read-and-remove duration; items reappear for processing after it elapses if not removed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/queue): In processing loops, add task.wait(1) after a failed ReadAsync to avoid hammering the service on errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/animating-parts): Use TweenService with TweenInfo and Completed:Connect for state changes instead of manual per-frame loops.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/hash-map): Wrap all MemoryStore calls in pcall; they are async network calls that can occasionally fail.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/hash-map): Return nil from an UpdateAsync callback to abort the update; contention triggers automatic retries until success, nil, or max retries (then a conflict is returned).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/powerups-with-if-statements): Combine conditions with `and` (e.g. `if humanoid and humanoid.WalkSpeed <= 50`) so stacking effects is capped and cannot grow unbounded.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/powerups-with-if-statements): Touched fires on every step or bounce; disable the trigger with CanTouch=false after applying an effect to prevent repeated application.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/powerups-with-if-statements): Use character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid") on otherPart.Parent to verify a touched part belongs to a character before acting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/glow-lights-with-for-loops): Use task.wait(timeChange) inside loops to spread changes over time; without it the whole loop runs instantly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/design-for-roblox): Watch performance on low-end devices; most users are on mobile and are sensitive to friction and load times.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/animation/play-character-animations): Use a debounce flag around animation triggers on Touched so a continuous collision does not replay the animation repeatedly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/use-particles-for-explosions): Check for a Humanoid via character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA('Humanoid') in a Touched handler before acting on the touching part.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/fading-trap): Use a for loop for repetitive property changes instead of writing many repeated lines; a 10-step fade needs one loop, not 20 lines.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/fading-trap): Debounce Touched-triggered functions with a boolean flag, since Touched fires on every body part contact and would otherwise restart the effect repeatedly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators): Configure RTBF deletion templates for each game's data stores so Roblox auto-deletes matching keys when processing a request; use this first for supported data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/RTBF-and-creators): Use a creator webhook with the Right to Erasure Request trigger for complex cases: custom deletion logic, unsupported data store schemas, or personal data stored outside data stores.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/deadly-lava): Guard Humanoid lookups with an if check; Touched can fire for non-character parts, which have no Humanoid.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/deadly-lava): Connect events with colon syntax (lava.Touched:Connect(fn)), not dot syntax.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing): Use GetProductInfoAsync or GetDeveloperProductsAsync at runtime to show the regional price in custom UI so it matches the purchase prompt.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/regional-pricing): Call GetUsersPriceLevelsAsync at player join and do not cache results after the session, since user price levels can change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/give-points): Put while-true loops at the bottom of a script; code below an infinite loop never runs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/give-points): Use player:WaitForChild on leaderstats and child values rather than assuming they exist when the Touched event fires.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/give-points): Destroy one-time reward parts after awarding points so they cannot be farmed repeatedly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/cli): Use --openScriptPath or --openScriptFromId with line/character range flags to jump directly to a specific line in a script.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation): Reuse EditableImage instances per TextureID via a lookup map instead of creating duplicates for each MeshPart.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/emote-bar): Call configureServer and setEmotes only from a Script; changes replicate automatically to all clients.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/story-games-lesson): Use variable names with numbers like name3 or myVerb1 as good examples; variable names cannot contain spaces.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/scriptprofiler): Use Script Profiler (Developer Console > ScriptProfiler) to find the scripts consuming the most CPU time before optimizing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/scriptprofiler): Use the 1 KHz default for general profiling; 10 KHz gives higher precision but costs more performance and may still miss very frequent API calls.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/scriptprofiler): Profile Client and Server separately; server data is cleared at the end of each session, so export it before starting a new one.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/group-chat-tags): Wrap player:IsInGroupAsync in pcall so a failed web request does not error out; default to false on failure.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/use-case-tutorial-overview): Organize scripts by type and location, and use services, properties, attributes and events to keep logic clean and performant.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/using): Use GetMarkerReachedSignal(name) on an AnimationTrack to trigger gameplay events (e.g. hit frames) tied to named keyframe markers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/retention): Test on different devices and platforms, and monitor crash rates, frame rates and errors on every update.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/camera/free-camera): To use the enhanced freecam config menu and post-processing, opt in via Workspace.PlayerScriptsUseInputActionSystem set to Enabled.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/vfx/laser-traps-with-beams): Check for a Humanoid via FindFirstChildWhichIsA before modifying a touched part's character to avoid errors on non-character parts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores-vs-memory-stores): Use in-memory Luau tables for session-only values that change often (timers, counters, enemy health, status effects) to avoid external service call cost and latency.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/pathfinding): For client-side pathfinding targets, use a BasePart in a persistent model and wait for Workspace.PersistentLoaded, since streamed-out objects cannot be resolved by clients.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/rate-limits): On HTTP 429, honour the retry-after header as the retry guideline; if absent, use exponential backoff.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/rate-limits): Monitor x-ratelimit-remaining and x-ratelimit-reset response headers to pace requests before hitting 429s.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/rate-limits): In-game HttpService calls also count toward the fixed 500 HTTP requests per minute limit per game server.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred): Set Workspace.SignalBehavior to Enum.SignalBehavior.Deferred; it is recommended for engine performance and correctness. Template places already default to it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred): Don't rely on an event firing synchronously. Under deferred behavior, handlers run only at the next resumption point, so yield before checking results set by callbacks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred): For one-shot listeners, use Event:Once() or call Connection:Disconnect() inside the handler. Destroy() on the instance does not drop pending handler calls and still runs them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/events/deferred): Avoid relying on Default SignalBehavior; it currently equals Immediate and will eventually switch to Deferred, so set the value explicitly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/leaderboards): Name the folder exactly "leaderstats" in lowercase; Roblox silently skips the player from the leaderboard otherwise.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/intro-to-module-scripts): Place ModuleScripts used by server scripts in ServerScriptService or ServerStorage, and those used by client LocalScripts in ReplicatedStorage.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/alert/metrics): Track server CPU time by CpuTimeCategory and subcategory to find which systems drive server cost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/multiple-parameters-and-arguments): Separate multiple parameter names with commas in a function definition, e.g. local function assignTeam(playerName, whichTeam).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/multiple-parameters-and-arguments): Arguments bind to parameters by position: the first argument fills the first parameter. Swapping argument order swaps behaviour.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/model-generation): Wrap GenerateModelAsync in pcall and warn on failure; always check success before using the returned model.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/model-generation): Set an optional max triangle count and specify bounding boxes to keep generated objects performant and correctly proportioned.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace): Access Workspace via game:GetService("Workspace") or the workspace global; all three forms are valid.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/data-storage/save-player-data): Run autosave loops in coroutine.wrap so they don't block the rest of the script; loop while player.Parent is set.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/data-storage/save-player-data): Use a constant such as AUTOSAVE_INTERVAL for save frequency so it can be tuned in one place.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/memory-usage): Monitor memory in the Developer Console (F9) during testing and live sessions; memory limits can crash user devices or servers.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/memory-usage): Use debug.setmemorycategory to assign custom memory category names so script memory is easier to attribute.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/optimization/memory-usage): Check Graph view paths (registry, upvalue, env, array) to see what is holding references keeping objects alive.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/hit-detection-with-lasers): Use RaycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances to exclude the firing player's character so the shot does not hit yourself.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/hit-detection-with-lasers): Normalise direction vectors with .Unit before multiplying by a max distance so the ray length is predictable.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/pivot-tools): Use PivotTo() and GetPivot() to move and query both single parts and models uniformly.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/pivot-tools): Changing Pivot Offset Position/Orientation or PivotOffset does not move or rotate the object; it only redefines the pivot.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/pivot-tools): For models with a PrimaryPart, pivot values are relative to that part (Pivot Offset), not world space (World Pivot).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/script-game-behavior): Put gameplay logic scripts in ServerScriptService so they run on the server and clients cannot access them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/adding-scripts): Store templates (beams, particle emitters) in ReplicatedStorage/ServerStorage and Clone() them at runtime instead of keeping them in the workspace.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Keep client CPU time under 16 ms to hit a 60 FPS client frame rate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Keep server CPU time under 16 ms per frame to sustain a 60 FPS server frame rate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Keep server memory below 50% of total server memory and ideally below 3 GB; investigate if it stays high or keeps rising.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): If server CPU time is high but core usage is low, investigate multithreading; use Extended Services for Compute if core usage stays high after optimizing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/performance): Check memory tags with the Developer Console and drill into process groups in the MicroProfiler to locate expensive processes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/multiple-conditions): Place the timer while loop at the bottom of the script so earlier event connections and functions are set up before the loop runs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/multiple-conditions): Use task.wait(1) for a once-per-second timer loop rather than tight loops.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/surface-art): Call SurfaceArt.configure from a Script in ServerScriptService only; it is server-only and overrides defaults via a config table.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/speech-to-text): Clear AudioSpeechToText.Text after processing each recognized phrase so stale text doesn't re-trigger actions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/speech-to-text): Match recognized text with string.find on lowercase phrases (e.g. 'open sesame') and ignore empty strings before acting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/intro-to-scripting): Always include a task.wait() inside a while true loop; without it the game freezes because Studio never gets to run other code.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/basic-scripting/intro-to-scripting): Use local variables and camelCase names; local scopes the variable to the block where it is declared.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/security/access-control): Do not mix server-only and client-only code in one ModuleScript; server logic and secrets like passwords get exposed through decompilation.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-3/intro-to-if-statements): Check that a variable was assigned (e.g. if mysteryPart then) before using it, since a failed lookup like workspace.MysteryPart yields nil and throws an error.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/versioning-listing-and-caching): Keep GetAsync caching on by default for reads; cached reads don't count toward server and throughput limits, but bypassing it always does.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/robux-transfers): Call PromptRobuxTransferAsync from a server-side script and wrap it in pcall; store the returned transferRequestId to correlate with receipts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/task-scheduler): Use RunService:BindToRenderStep() instead of PreRender when you need strict control over execution order.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/task-scheduler): Be aware Heartbeat script execution timing depends on Workspace.SignalBehavior (see Deferred events).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/properties): Store custom data as attributes (string, boolean, number, Vector2/3, Color3, CFrame, UDim/UDim2, etc.) rather than separate value objects.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/scripting/intermediate-scripting/save-data): Handle a nil result from GetAsync (new player) by defaulting to 0 before using it; also check the pcall success flag before indexing returned data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor): Use AnimationTrack:SetParameter from every script that runs the graph; NPCs need RunServer, server-authority players need both RunServer and RunClient.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/graph-editor): Multiple SetParameter calls in one frame are coalesced last-writer-wins; set only the final value per frame.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/http-service): Enable 'Allow HTTP Requests' under File > Experience Settings > Security before GetAsync, PostAsync or RequestAsync will work; it is off by default.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/script-an-upgrade-button): Set RunContext to Client on scripts that only handle UI, to run them on the client and reduce network traffic.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-2D-audio): Listen to AudioPlayer.Ended as well as IsPlaying changes, because IsPlaying false-to-true events fire but true-to-false may not replicate reliably from server to client.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-2D-audio): Prevent rapid-click audio restarts by checking IsPlaying/Ended before replaying a UI sound.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/loops-and-arrays): Use constants in ALL_CAPS at the top of the script (e.g. VANISH_RATE) so tunable values are easy to find and adjust.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/loops-and-arrays): Use GetChildren() with ipairs() to iterate a folder's children without hard-coding the array size.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale): Keep code performant and easy to read so it can be reused and learned from; the kit is designed this way as a reference.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/assemblies): Anchor only the assembly's root part to anchor a whole assembly; anchoring every part is less performant because it creates more assemblies.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/assemblies): Don't set AssemblyLinearVelocity/AngularVelocity directly; use VectorForce, LinearVelocity, Torque, AngularVelocity constraints, or ApplyImpulse/ApplyAngularImpulse, to avoid unrealistic motion.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/assemblies): Store physics-joined parts only in Workspace or a WorldModel; welds/joints in other containers are non-functional.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/voice-chat): Check a player's voice status with VoiceChatService:IsVoiceEnabledForUserIdAsync() wrapped in pcall, from a LocalScript or client-context Script.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/server-authority/techniques): Put simulation logic in a ModuleScript required on both client and server so BindToSimulation code produces matching results and avoids mispredictions.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/global-wind): Update GlobalWind in a task.wait loop with ~100 steps per gust cycle for smooth ramping rather than instant jumps.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/units): If you redefine studs or RMUs, apply the interpretation consistently across the whole game so physics and conversions stay coherent.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/physics/create-moving-objects): Connect Touched events on a jump pad part and check the Humanoid before applying impulse so only characters trigger it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/build-an-obby-lesson): Write AI Assistant prompts in specific, plain English; small grammar or wording changes can produce different results, so examine Explorer and Properties to shape prompts.
