- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/ending-matches): Connect a Died event with an anonymous function when you need the player reference inside the handler, since Died itself does not pass the player.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/ending-matches): Keep similarly named variables distinct (e.g. player vs whichPlayer) and declare functions above the code that calls them so they are in scope.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/ending-matches): Remove eliminated players from the active-player table and recheck the count after each removal so the win condition is accurate.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores): Use a hash map when you need key-based access; use a sorted map for ordering; use a queue for ordered FIFO processing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/alerts): Monitor client memory, unexpected out-of-memory exits, server CPU time, and frame rate with alerts to catch performance problems early.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/start-coding): Put game logic in separate scripts, one per responsibility; Roblox games often use a distinct script for each thing the game must do.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/plant-reference-project): Route WalkSpeed/JumpHeight changes through a multiplier-based manager to avoid conflicts when several systems modify the same humanoid value.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/play-background-music): Play background music from a LocalScript in StarterPlayerScripts, since the sound plays per client.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Prefer event-driven code over per-frame calculations; at 60 FPS each frame has a 16.67 ms budget.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Break long-running work into chunks (e.g. 5 ms per frame with task.wait) instead of one 100 ms block, which would cap a per-frame task at 10 FPS or cause stutter.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Consider Parallel Luau (multithreading) for heavy work that can run off the main thread.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Call Disconnect() on connections you no longer need so functions stop firing unnecessarily on later events.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Call a method once, store the result in a variable, and update it when needed instead of calling it every time you need the value.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/design): Don't put everything in ReplicatedStorage, since clients load all of it; put server-only assets in ServerStorage.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts): Check player.MembershipType == Enum.MembershipType.Premium before scripting Premium logic or triggering the modal.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/engagement-based-payouts): Debounce the Premium purchase modal (example: show at most once every 5 seconds) and handle Players.PlayerMembershipChanged to react to upgrades.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/accelerated-workflows): Use Code Assist for real-time completions based on script context and comments; the Assistant can run and write Luau and playtest.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-1/parents-and-children): Use script.Parent instead of hardcoded part names when one script should work on any part it is attached to; makes the code reusable across copies.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): Always fetch a fresh description with Humanoid:GetAppliedDescription() before modifying and reapplying; stale descriptions cause unexpected results.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): Do not change assets on a character while also changing HumanoidDescription; this may lead to undefined behavior.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/appearance): Set Players.CharacterAutoLoads = false before PlayerAdded to apply a custom HumanoidDescription on spawn via LoadCharacterWithHumanoidDescription.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes): If a server crashes within minutes of startup, suspect a massive memory spike during initialization, e.g. heavy assets cloned at startup without being parented or destroyed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes): Use viewer breadcrumbs to trace bloated nodes to their parent containers, then inspect those paths in Studio Explorer and add cleanup logic.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/nil): In long-running scripts, set large variables to nil once no longer needed so the garbage collector can reclaim them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/nil): Setting an instance's Parent to nil removes it from the game without destroying it; reassign Parent to return it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/input-and-camera/detect-user-input): Use task.wait instead of wait for timed effects like reload delays.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/events): Connect GetMarkerReachedSignal(name) on the AnimationTrack to react to a named keyframe; pass a Parameter string for per-marker data.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/attributes): In client scripts, use WaitForChild() to access Workspace objects; replication order is not guaranteed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/attributes): With StreamingEnabled, objects may not be in Workspace yet, so WaitForChild() is an even more important safety measure.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/attributes): LocalScripts in ReplicatedFirst can safely read ReplicatedFirst objects without WaitForChild, but WaitForChild on other services negates that benefit.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/attributes): Client scripts in ReplicatedStorage or PlayerScripts can safely read ReplicatedStorage objects without WaitForChild, since those load before the character spawns.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/attributes): Use GetAttributeChangedSignal for one attribute and AttributeChanged for any attribute; use GetPropertyChangedSignal for one property. Changed fires for property and attribute changes alike.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/animation/inverse-kinematics): Set IKControl Type, EndEffector, Target and ChainRoot explicitly; wrong values produce unnatural poses. Test each IKControl during Play test.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/modules/scavenger-hunt): Set resetOnPlayerRemoving = true in configureServer while testing so progress is not saved during development.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/timers-and-events): Check dot vs colon usage in Event:Wait() and Event:Connect(), and confirm the object is a BindableEvent, not a RemoteEvent, when events fail to fire.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/account-verification): Use Player:IsVerified(Enum.VerifiedLevel.High) to gate ranked matchmaking queues; verified users are less likely to cheat, spam, or get blocked.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/foundational-gameplay-systems): Store per-player grab state in a server table and clean up attachments, constraints and collision state on release.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/frequently-asked-questions-education): Luau is the scripting language used in Studio; there is no drag-and-drop coding in Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-text-to-speech): Guard Touched-triggered TTS with a debounce flag set true before Play and false after Ended:Wait() to prevent repeated triggering.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-text-to-speech): Check the touching player with Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter and compare to LocalPlayer so only the touching client plays the audio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-2): Write comments that describe specific actions, e.g. 'Changes color of PracticePart', not vague 'Runs code'.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-2): Indent if-statement code to match samples; readability matters more as scripts grow larger.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/monitor): Clean up data added to large tables on interaction; unbounded tables that never release entries cause infinite memory growth and crashes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/sorted-map): Wrap every MemoryStoreSortedMap call in pcall; these are async network calls that can fail occasionally.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/sorted-map): Delete a sorted map by paging GetRangeAsync in batches of 100 and calling RemoveAsync per key, using the last key as the exclusive lower bound.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/memory-stores/sorted-map): Remember sort order: numeric sort keys first, then string sort keys, then keys without sort key; ties break by key alphabetically.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits): Use UpdateAsync rather than blind SetAsync for shared data; it consumes both read and write budgets, so count it twice.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits): Call DataStoreService:GetRequestBudgetForRequestType() before issuing requests to confirm the current server's available budget.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/error-codes-and-limits): Use SetRateLimitForRequestType() to cap each server's share of the shared experience budget when also using Open Cloud.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core): Track and store player data (such as coins) on the server and display it on a leaderboard, rather than trusting client values.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies): Optimize performance; many users are on low-end devices and non-performant games struggle to grow.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/procedural-models): Procedural models are not supported inside Actor instances; do not plan generation there.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/procedural-models): If parameters change while a generation thread is yielded, the outstanding generation is cancelled and restarted with the new parameters, so avoid unnecessary yields.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/models): Use Model:PivotTo() to move whole models including all descendant PVInstances to a CFrame; MoveTo() only moves the PrimaryPart.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Scripts run serially by default; put logic under Actors and call task.desynchronize() to get real parallel execution, since actors alone don't speed anything up.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Combine serial and parallel phases: compute in parallel, then call task.synchronize() before modifying instances or global state.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Scripts in the same actor run sequentially; use multiple actors for logic units that should run on separate threads.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Use more actors (e.g. 64+) split by logic unit rather than few large ones; finer granularity gives better load balancing across cores, but don't overdo it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Avoid long unyielding computations in parallel phases; they block other scripts and cause lag.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Don't call require() inside a desynchronized phase; require modules in a serial context first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Use RBXScriptSignal:ConnectParallel() for signals like Heartbeat when the callback's work is mostly computation; it avoids calling desynchronize inside the callback.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/multithreading): Use actor messaging (SendMessage / BindToMessageParallel) or SharedTable for cross-thread communication; writing to the data model from parallel code is restricted and forces frequent synchronization.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-6/landing): Use module scripts to store functions and variables shared by multiple scripts, so logic lives in one place instead of being duplicated.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/ui/create-hud-meters): Reset HUD state on respawn by running the update script from StarterCharacterScripts, which runs on every character spawn.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/examples/custom-text-chat-commands): Use TextChatCommand instances with Triggered:Connect for chat commands instead of parsing chat messages manually.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/importer): Note that Add to Workspace, when importing from a published game, grants that game permission to use the restricted asset.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/physics/adaptive-timestepping): Set Workspace PhysicsSteppingMethod to Adaptive; docs say it can improve physics performance by up to 2.5x and is recommended in most cases.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/enums): Assign enum items to properties using the full declaration (e.g. Enum.PartType.Cylinder); the docs call this best practice over using a name string.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation-makeup): Call WrapTextureTransfer:PrepareProjectionMeshDataAsync() before any projected draw/sample, and re-prepare if the head mesh changes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation-makeup): Limit cage-space conversion to MakeupRules.WrapTextureTransferUVBounds from GetValidationRules(), not the whole cage; bounds must be finite with max > min on both axes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ui/text-filtering): Wrap FilterStringAsync and GetNonChatStringForBroadcastAsync in pcall and handle failures.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/secrets): Enable 'Allow HTTP Requests' in Experience Settings > Security before using GetSecret or RequestAsync.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/raycasting): Set the direction vector's magnitude to the desired ray length; maximum ray length is 15,000 studs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/raycasting): Compute rayDirection as destination minus origin when casting between two moving points, such as two player characters.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/raycasting): Pass a RaycastParams object to exclude your own character or script parent so the ray does not hit itself.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/raycasting): Set BasePart.CanQuery to false on parts that should be ignored by raycasts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/workspace/raycasting): Always nil-check the Workspace:Raycast result before reading Instance, Position, Distance, Material or Normal.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/environment/skybox): Wrap SkyboxOrientation Y in modulo 360 for long-running spin animations to avoid floating-point precision loss over time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/debounce): Debounce Touched events: Touched fires multiple times in quick succession from subtle physical contacts. Guard damage with a state flag so one contact deals damage once.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/debounce): Store debounce state in an instance attribute (e.g. SetAttribute('Touched', true)) and reset it after a cooldown with task.wait.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/debounce): Before calling Sound:Play() on Touched or Activated, check `not sound.IsPlaying` to avoid overlapping restarts of the same sound.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/debounce): For reusable pickups, use a CoolingDown attribute and a cooldown timer, and show the cooldown state visually, e.g. lowering Transparency to 0.75 while cooling down.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/main-design-requirements): Run object functionality that affects other players' simulation (e.g. collisions) on the server; run purely visual/audio effects (lights, particles, environmental animation) on clients to reduce bandwidth.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/buying-upgrades): Use FindFirstChild with exact string names matching the PlayerSetup leaderstats IntValues (e.g. "Gold", "Spaces"); mismatched names silently break upgrades.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/buying-upgrades): Check that both currency and capacity values exist and that gold is at least the cost before subtracting, to avoid negative balances.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-5/pairs-and-ipairs): Guard for empty results before using collected data, e.g. check #array > 0 and handle the else case to avoid runtime errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices): Shard hot read keys only for per-key read throttling: store copies under fixed keys (e.g. Weather_1..3) and assign each server a shard by hashing its JobId. Does not fix write throttling.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud-services/data-stores/best-practices): Rate-limit operational Open Cloud scripts; Open Cloud and game servers share the experience-level request budget.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/queues): Use a table-based queue (head/tail indices, nil out dequeued slots) for custom scheduling or request handling instead of table.remove from the front.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/luau/queues): Set priority above the current highest value to place an item at the front; equal priority items queue behind existing ones.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler): Profile on a mobile device via the MicroProfiler web UI; if a game runs well on a midrange Android tablet it will perform much better on a gaming desktop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler): Wrap expensive script sections in debug.profilebegin/debug.profileend to create named timeline labels and measure their cost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Reduce callbacks to Humanoid.StateChanged, Running, Died, and AnimationTrack KeyframeReached/Ended; they add per-step cost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Keep functions connected to RunService.Stepped, Heartbeat and RenderStepped as light as possible; delay or spread expensive work across frames.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Pool tables and reduce temporary table creation to lower Luau GC cost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/performance-optimization/microprofiler/tag-table): Break large property-update batches across frames to reduce updateInvalidParts cost.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/capabilities): Use HttpService JSONEncode/JSONDecode/GenerateGUID freely; they need no capabilities beyond script execution.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting/capabilities): Do not rely on getfenv/setfenv in sandboxed scripts; they are unavailable and throw errors.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-2/use-parameters-and-events): Connect functions to events with Event:Connect(functionName) so they run each time the event fires, e.g. trap.Touched:Connect(onTouch).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/loading-screens): Call ReplicatedFirst:RemoveDefaultLoadingScreen() before showing your custom screen, otherwise the default screen still appears.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/players/loading-screens): Wait for game.Loaded (if not game:IsLoaded()) before destroying the custom loading screen so it does not close before the game is ready.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/repeat-code-with-while-loops): Put a task.wait() between repeated actions in a loop; without it, changes happen too fast to see and the loop can run without yielding.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-4/repeat-code-with-while-loops): Make sure wait values are greater than 1 second in visual loops, since very short waits can hide intermediate states.
- 2026-10-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4964592): Web Play currently uses WebGL (GLES-level rendering), so avoid relying on high-quality shaders or advanced lighting until the planned WebGPU migration.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4966810): Shapecasts gained margins, size gradients and CFrame/size overrides; existing casts run up to 4.1x faster, so review physics casts for upgrade.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/teleport-private-servers): Generate reserved server GUIDs with HttpService:GenerateGUID(false) from a server Script so clients cannot predict or control reserved server IDs.
